### [1] [VAR-Q: Tuning-free KV Cache Quantization for Visual Autoregressive Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05380)
*Boxun Xu,Jiaji Lu,Yuxuan Yin,Zihu Wang,Ziyue Liu,Yu Wang,Zirui Liu,Peng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VAR-Q：一种免调优的视觉自回归KV缓存量化框架，利用massive-value outlier在feature与block两个维度上的结构，实现低比特KV缓存压缩与显存大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉自回归模型逐块生成图像/视频，产生大量visual tokens和KV缓存，使KV显存超过权重成为大规模部署瓶颈；其KV统计规律不同于语言模型，需要新的量化依据。

Method: 先分析KV缓存中的异常值通道：发现少量massive-value channels承载关键功能且去除后会破坏生成质量；这些异常值沿feature和block两轴组织，并源于projection bias与RoPE-induced scattering。据此提出VAR-Q，按相同两轴分组量化，利用生成循环天然提供的block边界避免检测与校准，并配合bit-packing内核降低运行时显存。

Result: 在涵盖next-scale与next-frame两种范式的9个模型上实现低比特KV量化，KV显存最高降低78.5%，生成质量几乎无损，并支持6倍更大的推理batch。

Conclusion: 视觉自回归KV缓存中的outlier结构具有强功能依赖性和规律性；VAR-Q无需调优即可有效压缩KV显存，为视觉AR模型的规模化推理提供实用低比特方案。

Abstract: Visual autoregressive generation spans two dominant paradigms: next-scale prediction for images and video, and next-frame prediction for video. Both decode block-by-block rather than token-by-token, yielding KV caches whose statistics diverge from those of language models. At scale, KV memory exceeds the weights and limits large-scale deployment caused by a large amount of visual tokens. Our analysis of these caches reveals three findings. First, a small set of massive-value channels carries the functional load: ablating these outlier channels collapses generation, whereas ablating a matched set of inlier channels leaves quality intact. Second, the outliers organize along two axes, feature and block, with magnitudes persistent within each cell and shifting across cells. Third, this structure arises from two independent mechanisms: projection bias and RoPE-induced scattering. Guided by these findings, we propose VAR-Q, a tuning-free KV-cache quantization framework that groups along the same two axes. The generation loop provides block boundaries for free, removing the need for outlier detection or calibration. Paired with bit-packing kernels for runtime memory reduction, VAR-Q delivers low-bit quantization across nine models spanning both paradigms, cutting KV memory by up to 78.5% with negligible quality degradation and enabling 6x larger inference batches.

</details>


### [2] [Visual Forensic Detection of Fabricated Bangla News Photocards using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05403)
*Anindya Kundu,Kabya Mithun Saha,Md. Muhaiminul Islam,Fayek Ahmed,Aminul Kader Bulbul,Muhammad Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将孟加拉语“图片卡假新闻”转化为视觉图像伪造检测问题，构建了带增强的本地化数据集，并比较了ANN、小型CNN和YOLOv8n分类骨干模型，结果显示小型CNN可行但存在欠拟合，预训练模型性能更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在孟加拉国，虚假新闻常以单张新闻图片卡形式传播，误导信息直接嵌入像素而非机器可读文本中，因此传统文本事实核查工具难以检测。

Method: 作者收集969张真实图片和1077张伪造孟加拉语新闻图片卡，来自脸书公开页面；通过16种光度与形态变换扩展到32736个样本。随后训练全连接ANN、四模块批归一化小型CNN，并采用防泄漏的分层分组五折交叉验证，同时微调ImageNet预训练YOLOv8n-cls进行对比。

Result: ANN仅达到约52%准确率，损失停在ln(2)≈0.693，说明扁平化图像会破坏伪造检测所需的空间线索。小型CNN达到87.45%±0.53%准确率，分类头热启动后为88.75%±1.55%；YOLOv8n-cls微调模型达到97.36%±0.42%。论文还报告分类别指标、混淆矩阵、单类坍塌诊断、人工错误分析和偏差—方差诊断。

Conclusion: 将图片卡假新闻检测建模为视觉伪造检测是可行的，但小型CNN在该任务上仍明显欠拟合；空间特征建模和更强预训练骨干能显著提升性能，后续需通过更合适的网络结构、正则化或更大容量模型来改善泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: In Bangladesh, much localized misinformation spreads as photocards: single-image news summaries that imitate the branding of well-known news organizations. Because the misleading message lives in the pixels rather than in machine-readable text, text-based fact-checking tools cannot see it. We recast photocard fake-news detection as visual image forgery detection. We build a dataset of 969 authentic and 1,077 fabricated Bangla photocards scraped from public Facebook pages: authentic cards from the verified pages of seventeen national television broadcasters, fabricated ones from pseudo-broadcaster pages, anonymous profiles, and public groups. Sixteen photometric and morphological transformations per image grow the corpus to 32,736 samples. A fully connected ANN reaches only about 52% accuracy, its loss stuck at ln(2) ~ 0.693, showing that flattening the image destroys the spatial cues forgery detection needs. A compact, fully batch-normalized four-block CNN with 489,954 parameters, about 1,644x smaller than the ANN, is evaluated with leakage-free stratified group five-fold cross-validation, which keeps every augmented version of a card in one fold. Under this protocol the CNN reaches 87.45% +/- 0.53% pooled accuracy from scratch and 88.75% +/- 1.55% when its classifier head is warm-started from the ANN. An ImageNet-pretrained YOLOv8n-cls backbone (1.44M parameters) fine-tuned on the same folds reaches 97.36% +/- 0.42%, marking the headroom above the compact model. We report per-class precision, recall, F1, and pooled confusion matrices, diagnose and fix a one-class collapse caused by an unnormalized classifier head, and give a manual error analysis and bias-variance diagnosis showing that the compact CNNs under-fit.

</details>


### [3] [When Does Sharing Work? Scaling a Shared SigLIP2 Across Image, Video, Audio, and Audio-Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05406)
*Xiaofei Sun,Peng Wang,Huayao Liu,Xiaoxiao Xu,Jun Song,Deqing Li,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Omni-Encoder 是一个使用单一 SigLIP-2 Vision Transformer 共享参数统一处理图像、视频、音频和音视频输入的通用多模态编码器，通过端到端联合优化实现跨模态对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像、视频和音频编码器通常独立训练，跨模态对齐主要依赖冻结锚点空间或后置绑定，难以实现真正统一、端到端的多模态表征学习。

Method: 方法包括三个核心模块：1）模态原生输入阶段，将不同信号映射到共享嵌入空间，其中音频通过 1D 卷积 tokenizer 将 mel 频谱转换为与视觉 patch 对齐的 token；2）多模态旋转位置编码，以绝对时间戳将视频帧和音频片段纳入统一时空坐标；3）交替模态训练，混合图像、视频、音频和时间对齐的音视频批次，并将梯度累积到单次更新中以稳定联合优化。

Result: 在覆盖图像、视频、音频和音视频四类模态的 16 个基准上取得有竞争力的结果，仅使用一套共享参数；联合训练相比各模态单独训练的模型还能提升每个模态的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明通过统一输入建模、时空位置编码和稳定联合训练，可以在单一 Transformer 中高效学习多模态表示，减少对锚点绑定或按模态适配器的依赖。

Abstract: Contrastive pretraining has advanced image-text understanding, yet image, video, and audio encoders are still trained independently, with cross-modal alignment realized mainly through post-hoc binding to a frozen anchor space. We present Omni-Encoder, a general multimodal encoder that processes image, video, audio, and audio-visual inputs within a single SigLIP-2 Vision Transformer with fully shared parameters. Unlike anchor-centric binding or per-modality adapters, all modalities are optimized jointly end-to-end, and the framework is organized from input to training. A modality-native input stage first maps each signal into the shared embedding space, including a 1D-convolutional audio tokenizer that converts mel-spectrograms into tokens aligned with visual patches. A multimodal rotary position embedding parameterized by absolute timestamps then places visual frames and audio segments in a unified spatiotemporal coordinate system. Finally, alternating-modality training interleaves image, video, audio, and temporally aligned audio-visual batches and accumulates their gradients into one update, which we find central to stable joint optimization. Omni-Encoder achieves competitive results on 16 benchmarks across all four modalities with one shared parameter set, and joint training improves each modality over its separately trained counterpart. Code and models will be publicly released.

</details>


### [4] [PEEK: One-Step-Look-Ahead Exposure Bias Correction for Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05407)
*Yunwei Bai,Ying Kiat Tan,Tsuhan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PEEK，一种免训练、确定性的采样器插件，通过“前瞻一步并插值”的更新方式缓解扩散模型采样中的曝光偏差，从而提升图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散图像生成器的去噪网络通常在接近真实数据分布的噪声图像上训练，但采样过程中却要在去噪网络自身预测产生的、逐渐偏离真实数据分布的状态上继续预测，形成训练—测试差距（exposure bias）。这会导致生成图像模糊、细节不足或形状不自然。因此需要一种不改变训练、不增加过多计算成本的采样改进方法。

Method: 作者提出 PEEK：在每个可应用的去噪步骤中，先执行一次临时的一步更新，得到“前瞻状态”；在该状态上额外评估去噪器一次，得到更接近真实图像分布的预测；再将该预测与下一步预测进行插值，以构造下一个含噪状态。插值位置通过搜索调度表优化，并利用前缀缓存和基线噪声调度重定基缓存提高效率。整个过程是免训练且确定性的，可作为采样器插件使用。

Result: 在 8 个像素空间和潜空间扩散模型上，覆盖无条件生成、标签引导生成和文本引导生成，以及多种输出分辨率，PEEK 在相同 NFE 约束下持续优于基线采样器，FID 提升最高达 49.3%。额外计算成本很低，每个模型一次性校准最多约 2.2 分钟。

Conclusion: PEEK 通过引入前瞻评估与插值更新，有效缓解了扩散采样中的曝光偏差问题，在不重训练、低额外开销的条件下提升生成图像质量，说明采样流程中状态匹配问题对扩散模型生成效果具有重要影响。

Abstract: Diffusion image generators are successful due to accurate denoising networks and strong numerical samplers. Yet the two components are not fully matched. During sampling, the denoiser predicts for the next step based on flawed input that differ much from the real data. This train-test gap, or exposure bias, caps image generation quality (i.e., blurry, lacking in details or awkward in shape). To mitigate the problem, we propose PEEK, a novel training-free deterministic sampler plugin. At each eligible denoising step, PEEK first performs a provisional one-step update, evaluates the denoiser once at the look-ahead state, and interpolates it with the one-step-later prediction to form the next noisy state. By taking the additional step, we obtain a generally more-image-like prediction from the higher-signal noisy state, mitigating the exposure bias. To optimize the interpolation positions, PEEK searches for an interpolation schedule search made efficient by the prefix and baseline noise-schedule-rebasing caches. On 8 pixel-space and latent-space diffusion models with unconditional, label-guided, and text-guided settings across various output resolutions, PEEK consistently improves over NFE-matched baselines, with FID improvements up to 49.3\% at minimal extra computation cost (e.g., the one-time-per-model calibration takes at most 2.2 minutes across our experiments.). Project website is at this https URL.

</details>


### [5] [Augmentation with Reinforcement Transformation for Low-textured Scientific Sketch Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05408)
*Zhiqiang Wang,Sheri L. Barton,Jennifer H. Johnson,Yi He,Xingquan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ART方法，将图像增强变换的选择建模为多臂老虎机问题，用于自适应构建自监督微调视图，从而提升低纹理素描图像检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低纹理素描图像广泛应用于野生动物保护、考古绘图和法医复合画像等科学场景，但其缺乏现代深度学习模型常用的表面纹理和视觉变化，导致学习与检索困难。现有随机增强策略可能引入破坏结构完整性的变换，影响表示学习效果。

Method: ART以预训练模型为基础进行微调。其核心观察是素描图像对旋转、翻转、缩放、拉伸等变换高度敏感，但不同变换对结构保持程度不同。ART将这些增强变换视为多臂老虎机中的“臂”，通过奖励机制持续评估各变换的有效性，并在自监督微调前自适应构建奖励引导的视图库；后续表示损失不反向更新ART。

Result: 在来自Mote Marine Laboratory的佛罗里达海牛素描图像上，ART在transformed-scar retrieval任务中达到71.40%的平均Top-1准确率，高于随机增强选择的68.83%；在location-aware sketch retrieval任务中达到55.84%，显著高于冻结CLIP的18.18%。

Conclusion: ART能够有效识别对素描表示学习更有利的增强变换，并通过自适应构建微调视图提升预训练模型在低纹理素描图像检索中的性能，相比随机增强和直接冻结CLIP具有明显优势。

Abstract: Low-textured sketch images are widely used in scientific applications, including wildlife conservation, archaeological illustration, and forensic composites. Learning from and retrieving such images is challenging because they lack detailed surface textures and visual variations commonly exploited by modern deep learning models. This paper proposes Augmentation with Reinforcement Transformation (ART) to fine-tune pretrained models to learn effective representations for accurate sketch image retrieval. Our key observation is that sketches are particularly sensitive to image transformations, such as rotation, flipping, zooming, and stretching. While some transformations preserve their structural integrity, others may introduce distortions that hinder representation learning. Instead of randomly choosing transformations to construct fine-tuning views, ART formulates augmentation selection as a multi-armed bandit (MAB) problem, where transformations are treated as arms and their effectiveness is continuously evaluated through rewards. This enables ART to adaptively construct reward-guided view banks before self-supervised fine-tuning; the subsequent representation loss does not feed back to ART. On Florida manatee sketches provided by Mote Marine Laboratory, ART achieves 71.40% average Top-1 accuracy for transformed-scar retrieval, compared with 68.83% for random augmentation selection, and 55.84% for location-aware sketch retrieval, compared with 18.18% for frozen CLIP.

</details>


### [6] [PointNDF: Cascaded Neural Displacement Fields for Point Cloud Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05411)
*Michael Y. Wang,Pingping Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出PointNDF点云去噪框架，通过PTv3提取全局补丁几何上下文，并利用级联神经位移场进行局部、有界且考虑分布的位移预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数学习式点云去噪方法采用“先提取逐点特征、再简单回归位移”的两阶段流程，但往往缺乏稳健的特征引导和面向点分布的几何细化，难以同时保持精细表面细节、几何质量和点云均匀性。

Method: PointNDF使用Point Transformer V3编码器聚合多尺度几何信息，生成全局补丁描述符。随后构建神经位移场（NDF），将全局补丁描述符与逐点邻域特征融合，并通过有界位移向量分支和尺度因子分支预测每个点的位移。该模块以三个级联NDF阶段重复传播局部特征并逐步修正点位置，实现分布感知的几何细化。

Result: 在PU-Net和ModelNet10数据集上，PointNDF取得具有竞争力的去噪精度，并表现出较好的推理效率。作者已公开源代码。

Conclusion: 将补丁级全局几何上下文与局部、有界且分阶段的位移场预测相结合，能够增强点云去噪中的特征引导与分布感知细化，从而提升几何保真度和点云均匀性。

Abstract: Point cloud denoising aims to recover clean geometry from noisy 3D observations while preserving both fine surface details and the underlying spatial distribution of the points. Many learning-based methods adopt a two-stage pipeline that first extracts point-wise features from the noisy input and subsequently predicts point-wise displacements via simple regressions. However, these approaches often lack robust feature guidance and distribution-aware geometric refinement, causing them to fail at maintaining optimal point uniformity and geometric quality. To address these limitations, we propose PointNDF, a denoising framework that combines patch-level geometric context with local displacement prediction. Specifically, a Point Transformer V3 (PTv3) encoder aggregates multiscale geometric information into a global patch descriptor. Rather than directly regressing an unconstrained displacement vector, a Neural Displacement Field (NDF) fuses this global patch descriptor with per-point neighborhood features. It then predicts each displacement through bounded-vector and scale factor branches, thereby achieving a superior, more uniform point distribution. Three cascaded NDF stages propagate local features and progressively refine the point positions. Experiments on PU-Net and ModelNet10 demonstrate competitive denoising accuracy and favorable inference efficiency. The source code is publicly available at this https URL.

</details>


### [7] [SO(3)-Equivariant Learning on CAD Boundary Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05449)
*Matteo Ballegeer,Dries F. Benoit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SO3brep，一种在B-rep面邻接图上运行的SO(3)等变图神经网络，通过局部特征、Wigner D矩阵和张量积耦合相邻面方向，实现旋转鲁棒的CAD零件表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAD模型通常不以标准朝向存储，因此B-rep学习需要对零件姿态变化具有鲁棒性。已有方法直接使用世界坐标会在旋转下性能下降；使用局部参考系虽可保持旋转不变性，但会把面间消息简化为标量，并丢失相邻面之间的相对方向信息，限制表达能力。

Method: SO3brep在B-rep的面邻接图上进行消息传递。节点特征首先在各面的局部参考系中计算，然后通过Wigner D矩阵映射到共享基坐标系中。随后利用Clebsch-Gordan张量积，将这些特征与相邻面之间的方向信息进行耦合，从而在整个消息传递过程中保留面与面之间的相对几何方向，并使最终输出对SE(3)变换保持不变。

Result: 在随机旋转测试零件上的三个面分割基准和两个分类基准中，SO3brep取得了所比较的B-rep网络和等变点云网络中的最高精度，同时参数数量最少。在标注零件较少的工业CAD常见设置下，其优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: SO3brep通过等变图神经网络机制兼顾了旋转不变性与相对方向建模，为CAD/B-rep零件学习提供了一种高效且鲁棒的表示方法，特别适合标注数据有限的工业场景。

Abstract: Learning on boundary representations (B-reps) requires robustness to the pose of a part, since CAD models are not stored in a canonical orientation. Existing B-rep networks either operate on world-frame coordinates and normals, and thus degrade under rotation, or encode each face in a local reference frame. Local frames yield invariance, but reduce inter-face messages to scalars and discard the relative orientation of adjacent faces. We propose SO3brep, an SO(3)-equivariant graph neural network on the B-rep face-adjacency graph. Node features are computed in local frames and mapped into a shared frame by Wigner D-matrices. Clebsch-Gordan tensor products couple these features with the directions between adjacent faces, so that relative orientation is preserved throughout message passing and the output remains SE(3)-invariant. Across three face-segmentation and two classification benchmarks with randomly rotated test parts, SO3brep achieves the highest accuracy among the compared B-rep networks and equivariant point cloud networks, with the fewest parameters. Its advantage is largest with few labeled parts, a setting typical of industrial CAD. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [8] [From Tokens to Trajectories: Parameter-Efficient Continuous Adaptation of Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05451)
*Gayatri Deshmukh,Onkar Susladkar,Adheesh Juvekar,Halil Kilicoglu,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出FlowShift，一种参数高效的后训练框架，将预训练的离散统一多模态模型转换为连续整流流生成器，在保留语义理解能力的同时显著改善细粒度视觉生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型中常见的离散tokenization虽有利于语义推理，但会引入严格的量化瓶颈，导致图像细节丢失，生成结果虽然结构正确却常含有伪影，难以满足高保真图像生成需求。

Method: FlowShift不直接替换语义主干，而是将离散视觉接口替换为连续潜空间投影，并在注意力层中引入LoRA进行参数高效微调。为提升视觉质量同时避免语义漂移，作者提出双目标速度向量蒸馏（DOVD），使模型同时对齐高保真连续教师模型和真实连续轨迹，从而学习更准确的连续生成速度场。

Result: 将该方法应用于主流离散统一多模态模型后，COCO-30K上的FID和美学评分显著提升，同时保留超过99.5%的多模态理解能力。消融实验表明，DOVD是提升生成质量的关键组件。

Conclusion: FlowShift能够在不牺牲预训练模型语义基础的前提下，实现高保真连续图像生成，为离散统一多模态模型的生成质量瓶颈提供了一种高效后训练解决路径。

Abstract: While discrete tokenization in Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) establishes strong semantic reasoning, it introduces a strict quantization bottleneck that degrades fine-grained visual details, often resulting in structurally accurate but artifact-heavy images. We introduce FlowShift, a parameter-efficient post-training framework that bypasses this bottleneck by converting pre-trained discrete UMMs into continuous rectified-flow generators. Instead of discarding the backbone's semantic priors, FlowShift replaces discrete visual interfaces with continuous latent projections and applies LoRA to the attention layers. To efficiently boost visual quality without semantic drift, we propose Dual-Objective Velocity Vector Distillation (DOVD), which aligns the model with a high-fidelity continuous teacher and ground-truth continuous trajectories. This approach drastically improves perceptual quality while preserving prompt adherence and reasoning. When applied to leading discrete UMMs, FlowShift significantly improves COCO-30K FID and aesthetic scores, while retaining over 99.5% of multimodal understanding capabilities. Ablations confirm DOVD drives generation quality. Ultimately, FlowShift achieves high-fidelity continuous generation without sacrificing pre-trained semantic grounding.

</details>


### [9] [From Activation to Intervention: Interpreting Sparse Visual Features through Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05522)
*Jianyang Gu,Jacob Beattie,Mridul Khurana,Julia Chae,Xi Xiao,Cheng Zhang,Anuj Karpatne,Wei-Lun Chao,Sara Beery,Tanya Berger-Wolf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Reconstructive SAE Intervention（RecSAEI）框架，通过沿稀疏自编码器（SAE）解码方向干预视觉表征并重建视觉输出，主动验证特征语义，从而缓解传统“强激活图像/区域”解释方法中的语义歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE 可将视觉表示分解为可解释特征，但现有解释通常依赖哪些图像或像素区域最能激活某一特征。当不同语义特征在同一位置频繁共激活时，例如同一纹理和颜色模式同时激活，这种被动观察证据会变得含糊，难以确认每个特征的真实语义。

Method: 作者提出从被动观察转向主动视觉重建的解释框架：首先针对 SAE decoder directions 对内部表示进行干预，然后重建对应的视觉输出，以重建结果直接反映被干预特征的语义含义；进一步将 [CLS] 层面的 SAE 干预锚定到一组稀疏的 patch SAE decoder directions，从而建立局部视觉模式与全局语义响应之间的联系。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够在多个数据集和多种视觉编码器上有效消歧不同特征的含义；同时，[CLS] SAE 干预在稀疏 patch 解码方向下表现出稳定的目标响应，并揭示了基础视觉元素与高层语义之间的组合关系。

Conclusion: 干预式重建为神经网络特征解释提供了比单纯激活相关证据更直接的因果式信号，是理解和验证视觉模型内部表示的重要补充方法。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose visual representations into individual features, offering a promising basis for explaining model behavior. Interpreting these features typically relies on images or pixel regions that most strongly activate them. However, this evidence becomes ambiguous when multiple features with different semantics frequently activate in the same locations, such as texture and color in the same patterns. We propose Reconstructive SAE Intervention (RecSAEI), a framework that shifts feature explanation from passive observation to active visual reconstruction. By intervening on representations along SAE decoder directions and reconstructing the visual output, we provide direct evidence of a feature's semantic meaning. We demonstrate that our method successfully disambiguates feature meanings across multiple datasets and vision encoders. Furthermore, our framework grounds [CLS] SAE interventions in a sparse set of patch SAE decoder directions. Beyond stable target response, this attribution also exposes the compositional relationship between elementary visual patterns and high-level semantics. Our results advocate for intervention reconstruction as an essential complementary signal for explaining neural networks.

</details>


### [10] [Discarded or Unreachable? Diagnosing and Repairing Spatial Relation Failure in Vision--Language Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05565)
*Mahdi Mostajabdaveh,Zirui Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究指出对比式视觉-语言模型在近机率表现上并非单一缺陷，而是由“可达性失败”和“池化信息丢失”两类原因共同造成，并提出一种零参数、查询条件化的空间矩方法从patch特征中补充左/右等空间关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比式视觉-语言模型在左/右等空间关系检索中接近随机，但表面症状下存在不同机理：有些模型的整体池化嵌入仍包含可解码关系，只是原生图像-文本相似度无法读取；有些模型则在池化过程中丢失关系极性，尽管dense特征仍保留。

Method: 作者在干净的双物体照片和受控C4轨道数据上分析多种编码器，检验左/右极性是否可从池化向量的翻转奇分量线性解码，以及池化是否保留排列轴和极性。随后提出零参数、查询条件化的空间矩读出方法，从patch网格重新计算空间几何，并将其作为与原生嵌入并存的向量用于检索。

Result: 在118,287张图像索引上，该方法将左/右成对准确率从0.51-0.53提升至0.81-0.96；在21个类别匹配候选中的条件R@1从0.05提升至0.37-0.75，并且每个批量查询耗时低于1毫秒，同时不改变未显式命名空间关系查询的原生排序。

Conclusion: 左/右空间关系在视觉-语言模型中的失败是“嵌入中可达但读取失败”与“池化丢弃但patch保留”双重机制导致；基于patch网格的轻量空间矩读出能同时覆盖两类失败，在不引入参数和不干扰原生非关系查询的前提下显著改善空间关系检索。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language models score left/right relations near chance, but the symptom has two causes that call for opposite repairs: the relation may survive in the pooled embedding yet be unreachable by native image-text similarity, or pooling may discard it while the dense features retain it. On clean two object photographs, across ten encoders, the lateral polarity is linearly decodable from the flip odd component of the pooled vector at 0.77-0.99, yet a search over a million captions leaves nine of the ten at chance under native scoring: an access failure. On controlled C4 orbits, where the relation is the only thing that varies, pooling keeps the arrangement axis at 0.998-1.000 and loses its polarity, which the patch grid retains: a pooling failure. Both sit downstream of the dense features, so one readout covers both. Our zero parameter, query conditioned spatial moment recomputes the geometry from the patch grid and stores it as a vector beside the native embedding. On a 118,287 image index it raises left/right pairwise accuracy against reversed geometry negatives from 0.51-0.53 to 0.81-0.96 and conditional R@1 among 21 category matched candidates from 0.05 to 0.37-0.75, at under a millisecond per batched query, while reproducing the native ranking exactly for queries that name no relation.

</details>


### [11] [Whose Values Are Generated? Measuring Visual Value Leakage in Multilingual Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05578)
*Zucan Lyu,Binwei Yao,Rheeya Uppaal,Jing Yao,Xiaoyuan Yi,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VisionValueBench 多语言基准，从霍夫斯泰德文化价值维度评估文生图模型如何在文化价值层面呈现中性社会场景，发现模型存在跨语言、跨地区的价值取向同质化，即“视觉价值泄漏”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型的文化代表性评测多关注地标、服饰、活动等可识别文化符号，忽略了生成图像所隐含的文化价值取向。作者希望从价值观层面系统考察文生图模型如何框定中性社会场景，并衡量语言提示与地理线索是否会影响模型输出的文化价值倾向。

Method: 作者将霍夫斯泰德的六个文化价值维度操作化为多语言评测基准 VisionValueBench，围绕 960 个中性社会场景生成文生图提示，并设置不同语言条件和地理线索条件。随后在六种文生图配置上评估生成图像所传达的价值取向，分析模型是否偏向某一特定文化价值框架。

Result: 研究发现，即使使用价值中性、可被多元文化解释的提示，生成图像仍稳定偏向特定价值取向，形成“视觉价值泄漏”现象。六个价值维度均出现一致倾向，例如更偏好平等而非等级（低权力距离）、更偏好个人自主而非群体义务（个人主义）、更偏好享乐而非克制（放纵倾向）。提示语言对这些价值取向影响有限；地理线索虽会使部分维度向相关文化价值偏移，但总体上仍保留模型的主导价值取向。

Conclusion: 文生图模型在全球范围内生成的文化价值表达存在显著同质化，其输出并不只是简单受语言或地区提示影响，而是倾向于稳定地传达某些特定文化价值框架。因此，评估文生图模型的文化代表性应从可见文化符号扩展到隐含文化价值观层面。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) systems serve users across languages and cultural contexts. However, evaluations of cultural representation focus mainly on recognizable elements such as landmarks, clothing, and activities, overlooking the cultural values that generated images convey. To bridge this gap, we introduce VisionValueBench, a multilingual benchmark that operationalizes six of Hofstede's cultural value dimensions (e.g., individualism versus collectivism) to investigate how T2I systems frame neutral social situations through a particular cultural orientation. We find that value-neutral prompts, which admit diverse cultural interpretations, nonetheless yield images that favor particular value orientations, a phenomenon we call visual value leakage. We evaluate prompts varying in language and geographic cues across 960 social scenarios and six T2I configurations. Generated images consistently favor the same value orientations across all six dimensions, for example, equality over hierarchy (low power distance), individual autonomy over group obligation (individualism), and enjoyment over restraint (indulgence). Prompt language has limited influence on these orientations, whereas geographic cues shift some dimensions toward the associated cultural values while largely preserving the prevailing orientations. These findings reveal substantial homogeneity in the cultural values conveyed by T2I systems worldwide.

</details>


### [12] [GaussianRenderFormer: Transformer-based Rendering of 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05593)
*Shahaf Valencia Levy,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 GaussianRenderFormer，一个不使用传统光栅化器的 Transformer 渲染器，直接从 3D Gaussian splats 和相机参数生成图像，并指出性能差距主要来自 view decoder 的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索神经网络能否摆脱显式 rasterizer，自主学习将 3D Gaussian splats 渲染为图像，并分析此类 learned renderer 相对于传统光栅化渲染的性能瓶颈来源。

Method: 将每个 Gaussian 作为一个 token 输入 view-independent encoder；view-dependent decoder 将相机射线划分为小 patch 并作为像素查询，通过 attend 到 Gaussian tokens 直接输出图像。模型在 Objaverse 数据集上训练，每个物体裁剪为约 20,000 个 Gaussian。作者还进行诊断实验，例如向 view decoder 提供 Gaussian 的投影 footprint，以判断信息是否被正确利用。

Result: 在 300 个未见物体上，GaussianRenderFormer 相对于完整未裁剪 splats 达到 38.08 dB PSNR，而使用相同裁剪输入进行光栅化渲染达到 44.02 dB。提供 Gaussian 投影 footprint 可使 view decoder 提升约 1.4 dB，说明差距并非主要来自泛化失败，而更可能源于 view decoder 的表示或注意力瓶颈；同时不能完全排除训练不足的影响。

Conclusion: GaussianRenderFormer 证明 Transformer 可以在无显式光栅化器的情况下完成 3D Gaussian splatting 渲染，但仍显著落后于标准光栅化基线。当前主要限制似乎不在模型无法泛化，而在 view-dependent decoder 难以有效利用或推断 Gaussian 的投影几何信息。该结果将 learned renderer 的研究问题从能否渲染转向如何改进视图依赖解码器与几何推理能力。

Abstract: Can a neural network learn to render 3D Gaussian splats on its own, with no rasterizer in the renderer? We present GaussianRenderFormer, a transformer that takes a set of Gaussians and a camera and outputs the image directly. Each Gaussian becomes one token in a view-independent encoder, and a view-dependent decoder turns small patches of camera rays into pixels by attending to those tokens. Trained on 26,820 Objaverse objects pruned to 20,000 Gaussians each, GaussianRenderFormer renders 300 unseen objects at 38.08 dB PSNR against the full, unpruned splats, while rasterizing the same pruned input reaches 44.02 dB. The gap is not one of generalization, and our diagnostics are consistent with a bottleneck in the view decoder: telling it the projected footprint of each Gaussian, which it could in principle infer, adds 1.4 dB. Under-training is not ruled out. Code, trained models and videos: this https URL

</details>


### [13] [CAPTOR: text-encoder-free grounding by counting caption co-occurrence over the visual words of a frozen encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05599)
*Elad Yoshai,Natan T. Shaked*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAPTOR提出一种无需文本编码器的图像区域定位方法，通过视觉词计数和标题关联来估计文本词项与图像patch的匹配矩阵，在通用数据和病理切片上均优于训练无关基线，并接近需要文本对齐训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本查询在图像中的定位通常需要构建“词项—图像区域”得分矩阵。现有视觉语言模型依赖文本编码器与图像编码器对齐，但许多图像编码器（如仅基于病理切片训练、缺乏语言监督的模型）不具备文本对齐能力，因此难以直接用于文本引导的细粒度区域定位。该研究旨在为这类无文本能力的图像编码器提供一种低成本、训练无关的文本接口。

Method: CAPTOR使用冻结图像编码器提取patch token，并将其聚类为视觉词。每幅带标题图像表示为各视觉词在图像patch中的占比直方图。对于给定文本词项，计算“标题包含该词项的图像”与“其余图像”在视觉词占比上的标准化差异，作为该词项的视觉签名。由此，词项—patch得分矩阵被分解为一个词项—视觉词查找表和一个与查询无关的图像因子，因此图像只需编码一次，新增词项仅需增加一行查找表。

Result: 在COCO、Pascal VOC和Cityscapes上，CAPTOR优于所有训练无关基线；在COCO按单个目标所属细胞评分时达到0.565 mIoU，高于基线的0.502。其性能还与在同一骨干网络上进行文本对齐训练的模型相当，但后者需要梯度更新、文本编码器以及数倍于CAPTOR的带标题图像。在留出乳腺癌病理切片上，CAPTOR超过使用病理文本编码器的最强基线0.160 mIoU。结合仅用切片训练的UNI图像编码器时，CAPTOR在共同网格上较这些基线提升0.132 mIoU。

Conclusion: CAPTOR表明，仅依靠图像patch的视觉词计数即可为缺少文本监督的图像编码器建立有效的文本查询接口。只要存在带标题图像，该方法就可将视觉特征转化为词项区域匹配能力，为通用图像和病理图像中的文本定位提供低计算、可扩展且训练无关的新途径。

Abstract: Grounding a text query in an image needs a matrix scoring every term against every patch. Vision-language models compute it with an aligned text encoder, which many image encoders, such as histopathology-related models trained on slides alone, lack. The Caption-Anchored Patch-Term Occurrence Representation (CAPTOR) estimates the matrix without a text encoder. It clusters a frozen image encoder's patch tokens into visual words and describes each captioned image by its share of patches in each word. A term signature is the standardized difference of those shares between images whose captions mention the term and the rest. The matrix factorizes into a term-by-word table and a query-independent image factor, so an image is encoded once and a new term adds one table row. Against every training-free baseline, CAPTOR leads on Common Objects in Context (COCO), Pascal Visual Object Classes (VOC) and Cityscapes. On COCO, scored on the cells one object owns, it reaches 0.565 mean intersection over union (mIoU) against 0.502. On all three, it is also level with a text alignment trained on the same backbone, which needs gradients, a text encoder and several times the captions. On held-out breast-cancer slides, it exceeds the strongest baseline scoring patches with a pathology text encoder by 0.160 mIoU. Read through CAPTOR, UNI, an encoder trained on slides alone, exceeds such baselines by 0.132 on a common grid. Counting can therefore give a text interface to any image encoder trained without language, wherever captioned images exist.

</details>


### [14] [Spatio-Temporal Multi-Order Interaction for Video Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05633)
*Kevin D. Nguetche,Lilian Hollard,Eric Granger,Marco Pedersoli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoMogaNet针对视频识别中时间建模计算开销大、时空表示偏向局部或全局交互的问题，提出基于深度卷积的并行多阶时间处理与隐式门控聚合，以及空间-时间双向交互机制，在降低计算成本的同时保持或提升动作识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多视频识别模型依赖显式跨通道聚合投影，其复杂度随通道数二次增长，带来显著计算开销；同时，CNN偏向局部顺序交互，Transformer偏向全局顺序交互，导致时空表示存在偏差，因此需要兼顾低计算成本与更均衡的时空建模。

Method: 以MogaNet的多阶空间表示为基础，VideoMogaNet提出两项核心设计：1）并行多阶时间建模，仅使用深度卷积并通过隐式门控进行聚合，使时间分支复杂度与通道数线性相关；2）空间维度与时间维度之间的双向交互机制，在不增加计算成本的情况下获得更优的时空联合表示。

Result: 在UCF101、HMDB51、Kinetics-400和Diving48等多个视频动作识别基准上，VideoMogaNet取得了与最先进方法相当或更优的性能，同时显著降低了时间建模分支的计算成本。

Conclusion: VideoMogaNet通过线性复杂度的时间建模与时空双向交互，在保持准确性的同时缓解了视频识别模型中跨通道聚合计算昂贵和时空表示失衡的问题，为高效视频动作识别提供了可行架构。

Abstract: Many temporal approaches for video recognition rely on explicit cross-channel aggregation projections, which have quadratic complexity in the number of channels, introducing significant computational overhead. In addition, the spatio-temporal representation used for video recognition is biased either toward local order interactions (for CNNs) or toward global order interactions (for transformers). Starting from the multi-order spatial representation of MogaNet, our method introduces two complementary contributions to tackle these problems. First, for the temporal modelling, we introduce parallel multi-order processing with depthwise-only convolutions and implicit gating-based aggregation, which is linear in the number of channels, inducing a considerable computational reduction. Second, we propose a dual interaction between spatial and temporal dimensions, which allows a better spatio-temporal representation without increasing the computational cost. By combining these two contributions, our proposed VideoMogaNet achieves performance on par with or better than state-of-the-art action recognition approaches across multiple video benchmarks (UCF101, HMDB51, Kinetics-400, Diving48), while considerably reducing the cost of the temporal branch.

</details>


### [15] [ARIDA - Active railway infrastructure dataset annotation using neural network predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05646)
*Maximilian Dornik,Christian Sieberichs,Simon Geerkens,Alexander Braun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向铁路感知数据的半自动标注框架，将实例分割预测转化为框、多边形、折线及成对折线，并结合置信度门控、轮廓一致性评分和不确定性驱动的主动学习，大幅降低人工标注负担，尤其适用于轨道折线等复杂基础设施几何标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代铁路场景感知需要大规模综合图像数据集，但轨道折线和复杂基础设施几何的人工标注成本高、速度慢；在缺少真值的情况下，自动几何标注的可靠性难以保证，同时标注预算、停止时机和精度—人力成本权衡也缺乏实用指导。

Method: 以掩码评分实例分割网络的实例预测为基础，将结果转换为边界框、多边形、单条折线和成对折线等标注格式。为保证无真值条件下的自动标注可靠性，采用置信度阈值，并引入独特的轮廓一致性质量评分作为接受门控。随后构建主动学习闭环，对剩余图像按不确定性排序，自适应确定每轮人工查询数量；简单图像自动接受，人工仅聚焦困难样本，从而形成数量控制机制。

Result: 在不同但互不重叠且多样化的图像以及时间连续且冗余度高的图像上开展实验，量化了人工成本降低与精度之间的权衡。结果证明在离散/多样化场景下可约减少三分之二的人工标注负担，在时间序列冗余场景下最高可减少约93%。同时提供了初始标注预算设定与停止标注的建议，以避免过度标注。

Conclusion: 该框架通过质量门控与不确定性主动学习提升了铁路基础设施数据集标注的效率和可靠性，特别适合复杂轨道几何标注，并可为实际部署中的标注预算分配、人工查询策略和停止时机提供可操作指导。其核心价值在于在缺乏真值验证的自动标注环节引入可解释的质量接受机制，并在人力有限条件下优先处理难例。

Abstract: Modern railway perception requires large comprehensive image datasets, but creating them is slowed by manual labeling, especially for track polylines and other complex infrastructure geometry. This paper presents the Active Railway Infrastructure Dataset Annotation framework, a railway-specific semi-automated pipeline that converts instance predictions from a mask scoring instance segmentation network into bounding-boxes, polygons, polylines, and paired polylines. To keep automatic track geometry annotation reliable without ground truth, it applies confidence thresholds and an unique contour-agreement quality score as an acceptance gate. An active learning loop ranks remaining images by uncertainty and adapts the number of manual queries per cycle. This is achieved through a novel quantity control mechanism, in which easy images are auto-accepted and human effort targets hard cases. Experiments on disjoint, diverse imagery and time-sequential, redundant imagery quantify effort reduction and accuracy trade-offs, achieving about two thirds reduction in the disjoint regime and up to 93% in the sequential regime. The study also provides guidance for initial label budgets and stopping to avoid over-annotation.

</details>


### [16] [NitroFusion2: Bringing Adversarial Loss to the Text Stream of Multimodal Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05648)
*Dar-Yen Chen,Yi-Zhe Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 NitroFusion2，将文本流状态作为对抗蒸馏的可控判别表示，把多模态扩散 Transformer 的少步蒸馏从常见的 4–8 步压缩到 1–2 步，并在质量上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对抗扩散蒸馏主要依赖图像特征进行判别，忽视了多模态扩散 Transformer 中文本流通过联合注意力已获得图像信息、同时仍受文本条件控制的独特性质，因此文本流可成为更有判别力且可控的对抗信号来源。

Method: 提出 Text-Stream Adversarial Distillation，利用中间文本 token 状态作为新的对抗表示；并提出 Aspect-Cued Discrimination，让判别器根据字形、物体、风格等不同方面进行条件化，以控制对抗训练中强调的图像属性；结合 NitroFusion 的专家判别器设计构建 NitroFusion2。

Result: NitroFusion2 将少步蒸馏范围从 4–8 步降低到 1–2 步，且质量相当。在 1 步生成中可获得清晰渲染文字和丰富细节，使一步生成更具实用性；在 2 步生成中于评测基准上仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: MMDiT 的文本流是一种强大且可控的对抗蒸馏表示，能够显著提升少步生成模型的质量与实用性，为扩散模型蒸馏提供了新的判别信息利用方式。

Abstract: Adversarial diffusion distillation has conventionally relied on image features for discrimination. We revisit this choice for multimodal diffusion transformers (MMDiTs), whose text stream becomes image-dependent through joint attention. We introduce Text-Stream Adversarial Distillation (TSAD), which exploits intermediate text-token states as a new adversarial representation. Because the text stream has already read the image while remaining tied to the text condition, changing that condition changes what information it extracts from the image. Building on this property, we introduce Aspect-Cued Discrimination (ACD), which conditions the discriminator on different aspects of the sample, such as typography, objects, or style, to steer which properties are emphasized during adversarial training. Building on the expert-discriminator design of NitroFusion, we integrate TSAD and ACD in NitroFusion2. NitroFusion2 brings few-step distillation from the conventional 4-8-step regime down to just 1-2 steps at comparable quality. At one step, it produces sharp rendered text and rich fine-grained details, making one-step generation practical rather than merely a stress test for distillation. At two steps, it remains competitive across the evaluated benchmarks. These results establish the MMDiT text stream as a powerful and steerable representation for adversarial diffusion distillation.

</details>


### [17] [Toward an Optimal Configuration for Tree Species Classification from Bark Images: A Leakage-Safe Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05680)
*Maeve Côté*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在防止同一树干图像泄漏的树级划分协议下，对BarkNet 1.0树树皮树种识别任务进行了系统性消融实验，发现较大图像块、足够多的推理图像块和简单的模型配置即可获得高精度，而学习型聚合和更大骨干容量仅带来二阶影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树皮照片的树种自动识别对林业资源调查和木材加工具有实际应用价值。已有深度学习方法中的若干设计选择尚未被系统研究，并且部分先前结果可能因训练集与测试集中包含同一树干的图像而发生数据泄漏，导致准确率被高估。因此，需要在泄露安全的树级划分协议下，系统评估影响树皮树种识别性能的关键因素。

Method: 研究使用公共BarkNet 1.0数据集，包含20个树种，并采用树级、无数据泄漏的训练/测试划分。以ConvNeXt-V2作为骨干网络，通过五折交叉验证，逐一评估多个因素：图像块大小、从逐块预测聚合为图像级决策的策略、训练流程，即单阶段与两阶段训练、骨干模型容量，从pico到tiny、整图分类基线，以及推理阶段可用的图像块数量。

Result: 逐块准确率随图像块尺寸增大而单调提高，从160像素的75.6%提升到512像素的89.6%；图像级准确率随之提升，并在约512像素处接近94.9%的饱和水平。学习型聚合仅比多数投票高出最多0.7个百分点，且随着图像块变大和注意力趋于均匀，这一优势进一步减小。骨干容量从pico到tiny对准确率影响不到1个百分点。两阶段训练相比单阶段训练没有优势。整图分类准确率为91.9%，低于基于图像块聚合的方法，因为整图分类缺少可平均的多块信息。推理时增加图像块数量可提升准确率，并在约64个图像块处饱和。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了一个简洁且可复现的推荐配置：在尽量大的图像块与保留足够多的图像块进行平均之间取得平衡，例如约512像素，并采用较小如nano的骨干网络。结果表明，树皮树种识别性能主要由图像块尺寸、聚合策略的简单有效性以及推理时图像块数量决定；学习型聚合和模型容量只具有二阶影响。该工作为实际应用和未来树皮识别模型设计提供了系统、公平且可复现的参考基准。

Abstract: Background: The automatic identification of tree species from bark photographs is of practical interest for forestry inventory and the wood-processing industry, yet the design choices that govern the accuracy of deep-learning approaches have not been studied systematically, and some prior results may be inflated by leakage of images of the same trunk across training and test partitions. Methods: A systematic ablation study is conducted on the public BarkNet 1.0 dataset (20 species) under the established tree-level, leakage-safe partitioning scheme. Using ConvNeXt-V2 backbones, the following factors are varied in turn and evaluated by five-fold cross-validation: patch size, the strategy by which per-patch predictions are aggregated into an image-level decision, the training regime (single- versus two-stage), backbone capacity, the whole-image baseline, and the number of patches available at inference. Results: Per-patch accuracy rises monotonically with patch size (from 75.6% at 160 px to 89.6% at 512 px), and image-level accuracy rises in step, plateauing near 94.9% around 512 px. Learned aggregation exceeds majority voting by at most 0.7 pp, an advantage that shrinks as patches grow and attention becomes near-uniform. Backbone capacity (pico to tiny) alters accuracy by less than a point, and two-stage training offers no advantage over one-stage. Whole-image classification (91.9%) trails patch-based aggregation, since it admits no bag to average over; accuracy increases with the number of patches at inference, saturating around 64. Conclusions: The study yields a simple recommended configuration: patches as large as possible while leaving enough to average over (around 512 px here), with a nano backbone. It also quantifies the second-order role of learned aggregation and model capacity, providing a reproducible reference point for practitioners and future research

</details>


### [18] [TRUST-Stereo: Teacher-Assisted Robust Uncertainty-Guided Search and Transfer for Fast Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05689)
*Pouya Sohrabipour,Yihong Feng,Chaitanya Kumar Reddy Pallerla,Tuan Dang,Ngan Le,Dongyi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出TRUST-Stereo，针对粗到细立体匹配中传播视差误差导致真实匹配点可能落在局部搜索范围外的问题，通过不确定性引导的残差搜索和教师辅助知识蒸馏，实现快速且鲁棒的立体匹配。模型仅用1.4M合成立体图像对训练，即可在多个基准上取得SOTA指标，并保持约50ms/对的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗到细立体匹配能够提升视差估计效率，但依赖上一阶段传播的视差初始化下一阶段的局部搜索范围。如果传播视差存在误差，真实对应关系可能被排除在后续小窗口搜索之外，从而造成精度损失。与此同时，轻量立体匹配模型通常需要更强的先验表示来提升泛化能力，而大基础模型中蕴含丰富的单目视觉先验。因此，论文希望解决两个关键问题：一是如何根据不确定性自适应校正局部搜索中心，二是如何将大模型的单目表示高效迁移到轻量立体匹配网络中。

Method: 论文提出TRUST-Stereo，核心包括两部分。首先设计不确定性引导的残差搜索模块UGRSM，用于评估传播视差的可靠性，并预测残差偏移量，从而自适应地重新定位局部代价体中心，避免误差传播导致正确匹配落入搜索范围之外。其次提出两阶段Senior-Junior Teacher Assistant知识蒸馏策略，将大规模基础模型中的丰富单目表示逐步迁移到轻量主干网络，使模型在保持低推理延迟的同时获得更强的特征表达能力和立体匹配精度。

Result: 实验在KITTI 2012、KITTI 2015、ETH3D和Middlebury等数据集上进行。TRUST-Stereo仅使用1.4M合成立体图像对训练，即可在多个基准指标上取得领先结果，展现出较强的零样本泛化能力。其性能优于许多依赖更大真实数据或伪标签训练的高效立体匹配方法。具体指标包括：KITTI 2015 EPE为0.93，ETH3D Bad 1.0为2.13%，Middlebury Bad 2.0为6.79%，同时推理速度约为每对立体图像50ms。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，粗到细立体匹配中的局部搜索范围不应仅依赖传播视差，而应结合不确定性进行自适应校正；同时，基础模型中的单目表示可以通过蒸馏有效注入轻量立体匹配网络。TRUST-Stereo在精度、鲁棒性、泛化能力和推理效率之间取得了良好平衡，为快速高质量立体匹配提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Coarse-to-fine stereo matching enables efficient disparity estimation but remains sensitive to errors in propagated disparity, which may place ground-truth correspondences outside subsequent local search ranges. We propose TRUST-Stereo (Teacher-Assisted Robust Uncertainty-Guided Search and Transfer for Fast Stereo Matching), which addresses this limitation through an Uncertainty-Guided Residual Search Module (UGRSM). UGRSM estimates the reliability of propagated disparity and predicts residual offsets to adaptively relocate local cost-volume centers. Additionally, we introduce a two-stage Senior-Junior Teacher Assistant knowledge distillation strategy to transfer rich monocular representations from a large foundation model to a lightweight backbone to achieve fast stereo matching performance. Experiments on KITTI 2012, KITTI 2015, ETH3D, and Middlebury demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization. Using only 1.4M synthetic stereo pairs, TRUST-Stereo achieves state-of-the-art results on several benchmark metrics, outperforming competing efficient methods trained with substantially larger datasets, including real-world pseudo-labeled samples. Specifically, it achieves a KITTI 2015 EPE of 0.93, ETH3D Bad 1.0 of 2.13% and Middlebury Bad 2.0 of 6.79% while maintaining inference at approximately 50 ms per stereo pair.

</details>


### [19] [Anchored Slice Propagation with Boundary Transport for 3D CT Segmentation from Three Orthogonal Annotated Slices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05693)
*Tushar Kataria,Md Rakibul Haque,Shireen Y. Elhabian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文针对切片级标注向三维传播时误差累积的问题，提出“锚定传播”并利用标注平面交叉处的已知标签校正漂移，同时比较截断SDF和轮廓追踪两种边界几何传递方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Sli2Vol等方法通过相邻切片亲和度传播二值掩膜，但每步预测会成为下一步输入，导致误差沿链持续累积。正交标注不仅提供少量初始掩膜，还使链与另一标注平面相交处的标签成为已知信息，可用于传播中校正漂移、评估链可靠性并在融合时加权。此外，二值掩膜丢失了到边界的距离信息，可能不利于在切片间跟随渐进形状变化。

Method: 使用固定的预训练亲和度骨干，对每条传播链进行锚定：当链穿过另一标注平面时，用该平面上的已知标签校正预测；再根据校正后与已知标签的一致性确定融合权重。作者比较三类表示/传播方式：1）锚定二值掩膜；2）用密集亲和度平均传播的截断符号距离场；3）从同一亲和度中提取运动线索，使轮廓顶点随切片边界几何移动，并以手工规则初始化轮廓、利用506个CT体积中的超体素伪对象学习运动校正，而不使用手动器官标签。实验在约403个CT扫描的2091个结构实例上进行，覆盖17个腹部结构。

Result: 锚定策略将平均Dice从正交掩膜传播的0.737提升至0.769，并改善所有结构的性能。锚定掩膜、SDF传播和轮廓追踪的平均Dice相近，为0.769–0.772。超出锚定掩膜后，学习式轮廓追踪使食管Dice提升5.7%、后腔静脉提升5.9%；SDF传播使右肾上腺Dice提升2.8%。但轮廓追踪在肾脏、胃和肝血管树上性能较差。

Conclusion: 利用正交标注平面交叉处的已知标签进行锚定校正，是一种稳定且普遍有效的误差控制机制；将边界距离或轮廓运动信息纳入传播可进一步改善某些细长或结构，但并非对所有器官都有一致增益。轮廓追踪需要更鲁棒的几何与运动建模，以避免在大器官或复杂血管结构上退化。

Abstract: Sli2Vol propagates a binary mask through affinities between adjacent slices, but an error can persist because each prediction becomes the input to the next step. Three orthogonal annotations reduce dependence on any one chain, yet they offer more than three starting masks: where a chain crosses another annotated plane, the correct label is already known. Those labels could correct drift during propagation and indicate how much to trust each chain at fusion. Between annotated planes, the information carried by the chain may matter as well. A binary mask records foreground membership but discards distance to the boundary, a cue that could help follow gradual shape changes between this http URL address these two sources of error with a fixed pretrained affinity backbone. We anchor each chain by correcting its prediction where it crosses another annotated plane, then use its agreement with those labels to weight fusion. We compare this anchored mask with two ways of transporting boundary geometry: a truncated signed-distance field (SDF) propagated by dense affinity averaging, and a contour whose vertices follow motion cues from the same affinity. The contour tracker starts from a hand-crafted rule and learns a motion correction from supervoxel pseudo-objects in 506 CT volumes, without manual organ labels. On 2,091 held-out structure instances from approximately 403 CT scans spanning 17 abdominal structures, anchoring raises mean Dice from 0.737 with orthogonal mask propagation to 0.769 and improves every structure. Beyond the anchored mask, learned contour tracking improves Dice by 5.7\% for the esophagus and 5.9\% for the postcava, while SDF transport improves right-adrenal Dice by 2.8\%. The anchored methods have similar mean Dice (0.769--0.772); contour tracking performs worse on the kidneys, stomach, and hepatic vessel tree.

</details>


### [20] [Learning Hierarchical Patch Splitting Policies for Faster Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05742)
*Aditya Gupta,Jean Sebastien Dandurand,Kai Qiu,Rohan Choudhury,Laszlo A. Jeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出 SPLIT，一种学习式图像分词方法，根据模型对任务相关语义的理解自适应分配 patch 分辨率，从而在保持 ViT 精度的同时显著减少 token 数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Vision Transformer 受固定 patch 大小导致的输入 token 序列长度限制。已有自适应 patch 方法主要依赖局部熵等像素级启发式，不能准确反映模型在任务中真正需要的语义信息，因此计算分配效率不足。

Method: 作者提出 Split Policy Learned Image Tokenization，让模型学习何时对简单或冗余区域使用更粗的 patch、对关键区域保留更高分辨率，使 token 化决策由任务相关语义驱动，而非原始像素统计。

Result: SPLIT 在分类任务中可达到与全分辨率 ViT 基线相当的准确率，同时减少约 30% 的 token。该优势也扩展到密集预测任务，在 COCO 和 ADE20K 上取得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 语义对齐的自适应 patchification 能够更有效地分配计算预算，在降低序列长度的同时保持性能，适用于分类及密集预测等广泛视觉任务。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are bottlenecked by the length of their input token sequence. Standard patchification fixes this length with a single patch size applied across the image, regardless of regional content. Prior work on adaptive patch sizing reduces token count by assigning coarser patches to visually simple regions, but relies on noisy pixel-level heuristics such as local entropy to make patchification decisions. We argue that this compute allocation should be grounded in the model's interpretation of task relevant information, not in raw pixel statistics. We introduce Split Policy Learned Image Tokenization which learns to allocate token resolution based on what the model finds meaningful, producing shorter token sequences that are better matched to task-relevant content. SPLIT matches the accuracy of full-resolution ViT classification baselines while using 30% fewer tokens. The same gains hold on dense prediction: SPLIT achieves competitive accuracy on COCO and ADE20K, demonstrating that semantically aligned patchification generalises well beyond classification.

</details>


### [21] [Decompose the Distillation: Interpretable Single-Pass Guidance for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05755)
*Wuyang Zhang,Xiaolin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CtrlFlow是一种单前向推理引导方法，在保留CFG可分解的base flow与guidance direction接口的同时，将条件/无条件两次评估合并为一次，并支持推理时无重训练调整guidance scale。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) 在每个采样步骤需要条件和无条件模型两次前向计算，使N步采样成本为2N次评估。现有单前向替代方法虽然降低了推理成本，但通常将CFG两个分量融合为一个不可分离的guided prediction，削弱了分步控制、诊断和独立调节guidance scale的灵活性。该工作旨在设计一种单前向、低成本且保留CFG可解释与可控结构的引导方法。

Method: CtrlFlow在冻结的扩散骨干模型上添加两个轻量LoRA适配器，每个仅增加约2.5%参数，从单次前向中分别预测base flow和guidance direction。推理时，guidance scale仅对guidance direction相乘，因此一次前向即可得到任意scale下的guided velocity，无需重新训练。为匹配推理时guidance direction误差被scale放大的误差结构，该方法采用unified multi-scale distillation loss，缓解朴素逐分量损失对两个输出误差权重不匹配的问题。针对极端scale下仍可能残留的误差，进一步引入scale-adaptive modulation以抑制速度场误差，并使用time-grid warping将求解器评估集中在轨迹变化最快的时间区域。

Result: 根据摘要描述，CtrlFlow能够在保持CFG式可分离输出接口和推理时可调guidance scale的同时，将原本需要2N次前向的N步采样近似降低为单前向/每步一次模型评估的推理成本。其multi-scale distillation loss与尺度自适应误差缓解策略旨在提高大guidance scale下的预测精度，减少guidance direction误差被放大导致的轨迹偏差。

Conclusion: CtrlFlow提供了一种兼顾效率与可控性的CFG单前向替代方案：通过两个LoRA适配器保留base flow与guidance direction的可分离结构，使guidance scale仍可在推理时动态调节，从而降低扩散模型条件生成的采样成本，并保留对引导过程的诊断和控制能力。

Abstract: Classifier-Free Guidance is a widely used inference-time guidance rule for conditional image generation with diffusion models, which exposes a guidance scale that adjusts conditioning strength after training. This flexibility requires conditional and unconditional model evaluations at every sampling step, so an N-step trajectory takes 2N forward passes. Single-pass alternatives reduce such cost, yet most collapse CFG's two components into one opaque guided prediction, losing the separable base flow and guidance direction that support per-step control and diagnostics, even when the scale remains adjustable. This work introduces CtrlFlow, a single-pass guidance method that preserves CFG's separable base-flow and guidance-direction interface. To preserve both components at one-pass cost, CtrlFlow attaches two lightweight LoRA adapters to a frozen backbone, each adding 2.5% parameters, and predicts the base flow and guidance direction from one pass of the shared frozen backbone. Because the guidance scale multiplies the guidance direction only after the forward pass, one evaluation yields the guided velocity at any scale, so a single trained checkpoint remains adjustable at inference without retraining. Preserving two outputs introduces a training mismatch: inference amplifies guidance-direction errors by the chosen scale, while naive component-wise losses weight both outputs equally. To align training with this composed inference-time error, CtrlFlow uses a unified multi-scale distillation loss. Residual guidance errors can still persist at extreme scales, where even small guidance-direction errors are amplified by the guidance scale. To reduce this inference-time error, CtrlFlow applies scale-adaptive modulation to damp velocity-field errors and time-grid warping to concentrate solver evaluations where the trajectory changes most rapidly.

</details>


### [22] [WayPOP: A Panoramic Open-Set Panoptic Tracking Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05801)
*Rohit Mohan,Maximilian Luz,Swarnava Chowdhury,Yao Lu,Florian Drews,Thomas Nürnberg,Yakov Miron,Federico Tombari,Stefano Gasperini,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WayPOP是一个基于真实Waymo PVPS场景、通过注入Objaverse未知3D物体构建的全景开放集全景分割跟踪基准，用于评估多相机、多时间步下未知物体的一致分割与关联能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放集全景分割基准多为单图或单视角，全景跟踪基准又依赖封闭类别体系，无法评估系统在同步多相机序列中对固定语义分类体系外物体进行一致分割、定位和身份跟踪的能力。

Method: 在真实Waymo PVPS序列中插入来自Objaverse的多样化分类体系外3D物体，将其固定在3D场景中，并利用校准的自车相机渲染，使未知物体在自车与周围交通参与者运动时跨时间和跨视角出现。由此生成具有时序和跨视角一致性的全景分割标注，并辅助LiDAR距离观测。基准约包含670个精选资产、980条唯一未知物体跟踪轨迹、53452个未知物体掩码标注和21300张相机图像。评测采用从视角无关开放集全景分割到完整全景开放集全景跟踪的任务层级，并使用known/unknown PQ与(w)STQ等指标。

Result: 对代表性开放集和开放词表分割方法以及附加时序、跨相机关联模块的基准测试显示，当前方法在真正开放集场景下仍存在明显不足，尤其是未知物体的鲁棒定位、以及跨时间和跨视角身份关联能力不足。

Conclusion: WayPOP暴露了当前感知系统在开放集全景分割与跟踪方面的关键短板，表明跨视图和时序的未知物体分割、定位与身份关联仍是自动驾驶整体感知的重要开放挑战。

Abstract: Holistic perception for autonomous agents requires reliably segmenting and associating scene elements across time and camera views, including objects outside a fixed semantic taxonomy. Existing open-set panoptic benchmarks focus on individual images or views, while panoramic tracking benchmarks assume closed-set taxonomies. Consequently, these benchmarks do not evaluate whether unknown objects can be consistently segmented and tracked across synchronized multi-camera sequences. We introduce WayPOP, a panoramic open-set panoptic tracking benchmark built on real Waymo PVPS sequences augmented with diverse out-of-taxonomy 3D assets from Objaverse. Inserted objects are placed at fixed locations in the 3D scene and rendered using calibrated vehicle-mounted cameras as the ego vehicle and surrounding traffic move, providing temporally and cross-view consistent panoptic annotations and auxiliary LiDAR range observations. WayPOP contains approximately 670 curated assets instantiated as 980 unique unknown-object tracks, yielding 53,452 unknown-object mask annotations across 21,300 camera images. Our evaluation protocol defines a hierarchy of tasks from view-independent open-set panoptic segmentation to full panoramic open-set panoptic tracking, using known/unknown PQ and (w)STQ metrics. We benchmark representative open-set and open-vocabulary segmentation methods with temporal and cross-camera association. Our results reveal substantial limitations of current methods in truly open-set scenarios, highlighting robust unknown-object localization and identity association across time and views as major open challenges.

</details>


### [23] [GREP: Grounding Generated Room Evaluation in Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05802)
*Mohan Cao,Yongsen Mao,Chucheng Xiang,Zhongyuan Liu,Zhihang Zhong,Haiping Wang,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出 GREP，用于评估 LLM 生成/摆放家具后的室内场景质量。它不再只依赖固定视角或预设问题，而是建立“物理有效性—视觉连贯性—人类使用逻辑”三层评估体系，并通过可自主取证与报告的评估智能体给出分数和场景特定问题。实验显示其人类偏好一致率约 87%，且反馈报告比仅给分或无反馈更能提升场景质量。论文还指出，随着规划能力增强，更强模型可能开始挑战外部约束，但仍需更灵活地协调冲突与适应指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向 LLM 的室内场景生成或布置评估通常局限于特定视角、预设问题或单一指标，难以全面反映整体场景质量。一个可实用的评估器需要同时检查物体是否物理可行、风格与构图是否协调、摆放是否符合人类日常使用习惯，并能在不同场景中自适应发现具体问题。

Method: 方法核心是将评估从“定义具体问题”转为“确立三层评估哲学”：第一层检查场景是否满足基本物理约束；第二层评估物体尺寸、风格与构图之间的视觉连贯性；第三层依据人类日常使用逻辑判断物体摆放是否合理。在此原则指导下，评估智能体根据场景上下文自主识别问题、收集证据，并生成评分与报告，从而支持面向具体场景的问题发现和可信判断。

Result: 实验表明：（1）GREP 与人类多数偏好的一致率达到 87%，明显优于基线评估器；（2）使用 GREP 生成的报告能够提升场景质量，而仅提供分数或完全无反馈的基线反而可能导致质量轻微下降；（3）结果暗示，随着模型规划能力增强，较新模型可能从单纯遵循外部流程与约束转向挑战这些指导与限制，但仍缺乏解决由此产生冲突的能力。

Conclusion: GREP 提供了一种更贴近人类整体判断的室内场景评估框架，不仅提高了评估准确性，还能通过可解释反馈改善场景生成质量。论文进一步指出，未来更强模型的关键不只是独立规划能力，还包括能够灵活适应外部指导、约束并协调冲突，以更可靠地完成复杂场景评估与生成任务。

Abstract: Recent LLM-centric advances can generate attractive furnished rooms by selecting and arranging assets. Yet evaluation remains limited to specific perspectives or predefined questions, failing to reflect overall scene quality. To address this pressing need, we introduce GREP. Its core is a focus shift from defining specific questions to establishing a three-tier evaluation philosophy: basic physical validity checks whether a scene obeys physical constraints; visual coherence concerns compatibility in object scale, style, and composition; and, at the highest level, human-use logic requires object arrangements to align with everyday human habits. Guided by these principles, an evaluation agent autonomously and adaptively identifies issues from scene context, gathers evidence, and produces scores and reports. This supports scene-specific issue discovery and reliable judgments. Experiments show that (1) GREP achieves 87% agreement with human majority preferences, substantially outperforming baseline evaluators. (2) GREP reports improve scene quality through refinement, whereas score-only and no-feedback baselines show slight declines. (3) GREP suggests that as planning ability improves, recent models such as GPT-5.6 Sol can move from relying on pipelines and harnesses to challenging their guidance and constraints, but still lack the ability to resolve the resulting conflicts. Stronger models are therefore expected not only to plan well, but also to adapt to external guidance and constraints more flexibly. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [24] [ReTailor: A Residual Feature Tailoring Framework for Real-Time Cross-Sensor Pansharpening Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05811)
*Jing-Ze Liang,Wei-Jie Xiao,Zi-Han Cao,Yang-Dong Li,Tian-Yi Hu,Liang-Jian Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReTailor，一种面向遥感全色锐化的实时顺序跨传感器自适应框架。该方法不重新训练整个融合网络，而是通过轻量级残差 Feature Tailor 在自监督、物理约束下快速修正不同传感器之间的特征偏移，从而实现高效、低成本的跨传感器泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全色锐化模型在已训练传感器上表现良好，但面对未见传感器时泛化能力不足。已有跨传感器适应方法存在两类局限：一是目标传感器重训练需要目标域训练配对数据，并依赖额外训练阶段；二是逐图像零样本适应需要对每个测试图像优化整个网络，计算成本高、难以实时部署。因此，需要在不修改已学习融合先验的前提下，以低开销方式缓解跨传感器退化。

Method: ReTailor 的核心是冻结主模型中已学习到的融合先验，仅优化一个轻量级残差 Feature Tailor，使其在自监督和物理感知约束下校正传感器间特征偏移。为提升效率与效果，该方法结合内容感知 patch 选择，从每张图像中挑选少量信息量高的图像块参与优化，以较低代价提高质量和速度；同时采用 rolling residual bootstrap，在连续图像流中继承跨图像、跨传感器的残差校正知识，进一步提升自适应效果。作者还与低秩微调方法 LoRA 进行对比，并将核心思想推广到超分辨率全色锐化和图像分割两个遥感任务中。

Result: 在跨传感器全色锐化实验设置中，ReTailor 能够有效缓解因传感器差异导致的性能退化，同时保持较高的计算效率，适合实时或近实时部署。与 LoRA 等微调方法相比，ReTailor 在跨传感器适应中体现出更强的优势。此外，在超分辨率全色锐化和图像分割任务中，该方法也能稳定提升性能，表明其核心思想具有跨任务和跨场景的适用性。

Conclusion: ReTailor 为遥感全色锐化模型在真实世界跨传感器退化场景中的部署提供了一条高效且实用的路径。其关键洞见在于：跨传感器适应不一定需要重训练整个模型或优化大量参数，而可通过轻量残差特征校正、内容感知优化子集和跨图像知识继承，在不破坏原有融合先验的情况下显著提升泛化能力。该方法不仅适用于全色锐化，也为其他遥感任务的跨传感器鲁棒性优化提供了参考。

Abstract: Recent pansharpening models achieve strong fusion performance on trained sensors, yet often generalize poorly to unseen sensors. Existing cross-sensor adaptation is limited by target-sensor retraining, which requires target-sensor training pairs and a dedicated training stage, and by per-image zero-shot adaptation, which is computationally expensive due to whole-network optimization for each test image. To address these problems, we propose ReTailor, a real-time sequential cross-sensor adaptation framework. Only a lightweight residual Feature Tailor is rapidly optimized under self-supervised, physics-aware constraints to correct cross-sensor shifts without modifying the well-learned fusion prior. For efficient and effective deployment, ReTailor combines content-aware patch selection, which identifies a few informative patches per image for optimization at negligible cost to improve both quality and efficiency, with rolling residual bootstrap, which inherits sensor-related knowledge across the image stream to further improve quality. Extensive experiments on cross-sensor adaptation settings show that ReTailor mitigates cross-sensor degradation both effectively and efficiently. Moreover, we compare ReTailor with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), a widely used method for fine-tuning, to demonstrate the advantages of our approach. We further evaluate the core principle of ReTailor on two additional remote sensing tasks: super-resolution pansharpening and image segmentation, where it consistently improves performance. In general, ReTailor provides a highly effective and efficient strategy for deploying pansharpening models under real-world cross-sensor degradation, while also offering useful insights into managing cross-sensor degradation in other remote sensing tasks. Code will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [25] [Navigational Safety of Marine Surface Vehicles in Restricted Visibility: From Intelligent Vision to Vision-Centered Multi-Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05818)
*Ryan Wen Liu,Fengchuan Song,Tianxiang Wang,Guobei Peng,Youngrong Kim,Xian Zhong,Hooi-Siang Kang,Maohan Liang,Junzhi Yu,Shanliang Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文是一篇综述，系统回顾面向水面无人艇（MSV）的智能视觉技术以及以视觉为中心的多传感器融合方法，重点关注受限能见度条件下的感知增强、目标检测跟踪、可航区域分割和红外/雷达/LiDAR/AIS等融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 船舶相机因性价比高被广泛用于水面航行感知，但受平台运动、恶劣天气和远距离成像影响，视觉信息容易退化。为提升水面无人艇在低能见度条件下的情境感知和航行安全，需要结合AIS、雷达、LiDAR等传感器，因此有必要综述智能视觉及视觉中心多传感器融合研究进展。

Method: 文章以综述方式展开：先分析受限能见度下图像质量退化的成因，再梳理船载视频稳像、视觉感知增强、目标检测与跟踪、可航区域分割等智能视觉方法；随后讨论红外-可见光、雷达-视觉、LiDAR-视觉、AIS-视觉及其他多传感器融合方案，并总结开源海洋数据集与安全关键应用。

Result: 论文归纳了从单一智能视觉到视觉为中心多传感器融合的技术路线和应用进展，整理了可用于研究验证的公开数据集，并讨论了当前方法在受限场景中的优势与不足。

Conclusion: 以视觉为中心的多传感器融合有助于提升水面无人艇在复杂恶劣环境下的感知鲁棒性和航行安全性；但数据质量、传感器配准、实时性、跨场景泛化和安全验证等仍是开放挑战，未来需要更可靠的融合算法、标准化数据集和实际船舶应用验证。

Abstract: Marine surface vehicles (MSVs) have attracted considerable attention for their commercial, scientific, and military applications. Owing to their favorable performance-to-cost ratio, shipboard cameras are widely used to detect and recognize potentially hazardous situations across diverse operational scenarios. However, visual perception is inevitably degraded by restricted conditions, including irregular motion of the imaging platform, adverse weather, and long imaging distances. To improve situational awareness and navigation capabilities, complementary sensors, such as the automatic identification system (AIS), radar, and light detection and ranging (LiDAR), etc., have become essential shipboard equipment. Multi-sensor fusion can provide richer perceptual information and help ensure navigational safety of MSVs in restricted visibility. This article reviews recent progress from intelligent vision to vision-centered multi-sensor fusion for newcomers and researchers in intelligent ship navigation and intelligent waterborne transportation system (IWTS). After introducing the generation of quality-degraded images in restricted visibility, we will review intelligent vision methods for four key issues, i.e., shipboard video stabilization, visual perception enhancement, object detection and tracking, and navigable area segmentation. We then examine vision-centered multi-sensor fusion methods, including infrared-visible image fusion, radar-vision fusion, LiDAR-vision fusion, AIS-vision fusion, and other multi-sensor fusion scenarios. Open-source datasets and safety-critical applications are also summarized and discussed. Finally, we highlight open challenges and future research directions. The collected maritime datasets are available at this https URL and will be regularly updated.

</details>


### [26] [DualCast: Dual-Image Conditioned Diffusion Transformers for Digital Human E-Commerce Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05825)
*Lifan Jiang,Yibo Zhao,Senlin Cheng,Bolei Xu,Xiaobo Li,Can Ye,Dehai Zhao,Deng Cai,Boxi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DualCast提出一种面向角色地址化条件的双图像视频生成框架，通过分离商品与人物条件、强化关键区域监督以及条件dropout，提升电商数字人视频中商品外观、人物身份与风格的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字人电商视频生成需要同时保持商品外观、人物身份和整体视频风格。现有支持多参考图的模型通常未显式区分“展示什么”和“谁来展示”的不对称角色，导致生成过程偏向某一参考，容易出现商品畸变、身份漂移或单参考坍缩等问题。

Method: 提出DualCast，包括三个核心设计：1）双时空槽位注入机制，分别对商品图和人物图进行VAE编码，并将其潜特征放置在DiT序列的前后边界，实现序列级条件分离与定向注入；2）掩码引导的区域加权损失，对商品、人脸以及商品-人脸重叠区域进行更强监督，提高关键区域生成保真度；3）图像条件dropout，使单个模型支持双图生成、仅商品生成和仅人物生成。此外，构建1万条电商视频数据集用于训练。

Result: 实验表明DualCast在数字人电商视频生成任务中表现出较强性能，能够更稳定地保持商品外观、人物身份和整体视频风格，并缓解多参考条件下的角色混淆与特征竞争。

Conclusion: 显式建模商品和人物参考图的不同角色，并通过序列级条件分离、区域感知监督和灵活条件训练，可以显著提升电商数字人视频生成的可控性与一致性，为多参考条件视频生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Digital human e-commerce video generation requires preserving product appearance, human identity, and overall video style during dynamic demonstrations. Although recent models support multiple reference images, their general conditioning schemes do not explicitly distinguish the asymmetric roles of "what is presented" and "who presents it." Consequently, generated videos may favor a single reference, resulting in product distortion, identity drift, or single-reference collapse. To address this issue, we propose DualCast, a role-addressed dual-image conditioning framework for video generation. By explicitly modeling the distinct roles of product and human conditions, DualCast reduces role ambiguity and feature competition between references. First, a dual-spatiotemporal slot injection mechanism VAE-encodes the product and human images separately and places their latent features at the front and rear boundaries of the DiT sequence, enabling sequence-level condition separation and directed injection. Second, a mask-guided region-weighted loss strengthens supervision over product, face, and product-face overlap regions, improving generation fidelity in critical areas. Third, image condition dropout enables a single model to support dual-image, product-only, and human-only generation. In addition, we construct a curated dataset of 10K e-commerce videos for model training. Experimental results demonstrate the strong performance of DualCast.

</details>


### [27] [Chronos: Sequential Identity-Preserving Video Generation by Temporal Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05893)
*Mengqi Huang,Wenchuan Wang,Zhendong Mao,Yijing Tu,Zhike Liu,Luoni Wang,Ning Tong,Xiaoxiao Sheng,Tianyu Huang,Xuecheng Qi,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Chronos 框架，用于身份保持视频生成，通过提示、关键帧和多候选评估统一单段人脸身份保持与多段连续动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖参考图增强静态人脸对齐，但单一身份约束与时间可控性之间存在冲突，在大姿态变化和多段连续动作下易出现身份漂移或动作抑制。

Method: Chronos 引入三项核心机制：语义提示锚定，自适应优化用户提示；视觉帧锚定，将条件转化为可视化的关键帧锚点以引导生成；度量引导的候选路由，通过多维评估从不同生成器中选择高质量视频。其目标是将单段身份保持与多段顺序动作生成统一到同一时序建模框架中。

Result: 在 ACM MM 2026 身份保持视频生成挑战赛中，该方法同时获得 Track 1 和 Track 2 第一名，证明其在人脸身份保持和连续动作生成任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: Chronos 通过提示、关键帧和候选选择三阶段协同，缓解了身份保真与时间可控之间的张力，提升了复杂运动和多段动作场景下的一致性视频生成质量。

Abstract: Identity-preserving video generation aims to synthesize high-quality and visually realistic videos from textual and visual conditions while faithfully maintaining the subject's identity across diverse motions. Prior methods have extensively introduced reference-guided control signals and identity-aware conditioning to improve facial alignment from reference images. However, improving static identity alignment alone creates a fundamental tension between identity fidelity and temporal controllability, which frequently causes identity drift or motion suppression under large pose variations and multi-segment sequential actions. To address this, we propose Chronos, a framework that unifies single-segment facial identity preservation and multi-segment sequential action generation under a common temporal formulation. Specifically, Chronos integrates Semantic Prompt Anchoring to adaptively refine the user prompt, Visual Frame Anchoring to transform conditions into visually grounded keyframe anchors for visual guidance, and Metric-Guided Candidate Routing to select the highest-quality videos from heterogeneous generators via multi-dimensional evaluation. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the superiority of our approach, which achieved first place in both Track 1 and Track 2 of the ACM MM 2026 Identity-Preserving Video Generation Challenge, demonstrating its effectiveness across both facial identity preservation and sequential action generation settings.

</details>


### [28] [DecoupAttn: Decoupled Sparse Attention for Training-Free Acceleration of Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05917)
*Yanjin Li,Bingbing Qiu,Qiang Huang,Rui Qian,Yanfeng Li,Jian Wang,Xiaoying Tang,Mingda Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DecoupAttn：一种训练无关的解耦稀疏注意力范式，将全键补偿式语义聚类与少量固定的精确注意力块分离，并以Distortion Budget优化跨时间步、层和头的稀疏计算预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力用于加速少步视频扩散Transformer时，存在三个瓶颈：每个去噪步反复查找关键块带来识别开销；按统一或局部指标分配稀疏预算并非最优；复用掩码或聚类会随去噪过程漂移，导致跨步误差累积。作者观察到，当每个键都被补偿时，被跳过块留下的误差可相互抵消，因此可解耦“补偿所有键”和“精确校正关键块”。

Method: DecoupAttn将稀疏注意力拆为两条路径：语义聚类负责补偿所有键，精确注意力仅校正聚类残差中最重要的少数块；这些精确块通过位置在离线阶段固定。为决定哪些块应精确计算，提出Distortion Budget，这是一种率失真目标，将预算分配到最终潜变量中由补偿残差造成最大失真的时间步、层和头。推理时进一步提出Reassign Cache：每一步把漂移的键重新分配到缓存中心，刷新补偿项，同时保持预算块固定。整体训练无关，仅用少量校准视频离线编译计划，推理兼容标准块稀疏核和一个融合补偿核。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数值，但声称该方案减少了每步关键块搜索开销，改善了跨时间步、层和头的预算分配，并通过动态重分配缓存缓解掩码/聚类老化带来的跨步误差累积，从而加速少步视频扩散Transformer推理。

Conclusion: DecoupAttn通过“全键聚类补偿 + 少量位置固定精确校正”的解耦设计，以及面向最终潜变量失真的Distortion Budget和Reassign Cache机制，为视频扩散模型提供了一种训练无关、可离线规划且可部署到块稀疏核的高效稀疏注意力新范式。

Abstract: Sparse attention accelerates few-step video diffusion transformers by computing a few key blocks exactly and compensating for the rest. However, existing sparse attention still faces key challenges, including identification overhead from finding critical blocks at every denoising step, suboptimal budget allocation that sets sparsity budgets uniformly or by local metrics, and cross-step error accumulation as reused masks or clusters go stale. Observing that the errors left on skipped blocks cancel each other once every key is compensated, we propose DecoupAttn, a new paradigm of decoupled sparse attention: semantic clusters compensate every key, and exact attention corrects their error on a few blocks fixed once offline by position. To support this paradigm, we introduce Distortion Budget, a rate-distortion objective for budget allocation that places exact computation across timesteps, layers, and heads where it removes the most distortion that compensation leaves in the final latent. Built on this budget, we further propose Reassign Cache, in which each step reassigns the drifting keys to cached centroids and refreshes compensation while the budgeted blocks stay fixed. Moreover, DecoupAttn is training-free: a few calibration clips compile one plan offline, and inference runs standard block-sparse kernels with one fused compensation kernel. Our code will be released after submission.

</details>


### [29] [Modality-Aware Expert Pruning for MoE-Based Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05927)
*Donggeun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种训练无关的多模态感知专家剪枝方法MAEP，用于高效压缩MoE-MLLM中的专家参数。其核心思想是按模态分解专家重要性，用Delta-H输出变化衡量移除专家后MoE输出的改变，并利用低注意力视觉token作为稳定、可跨数据集的视觉侧剪枝信号，同时以文本侧重要性作为保留信号，在层间对专家进行全局排序。实验表明MAEP可在三个MoE-MLLM上显著降低显存占用，在多数多模态基准上优于模态无关基线，并使约58GB的模型权重适合40GB单卡部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽然使多模态大语言模型能够以稀疏激活的方式扩展模型容量，但专家参数占据模型总大小的绝大部分，造成严重显存瓶颈。在如InternVL3.5-30B-A3B和Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B等现代MoE-MLLM中，专家参数占比超过90%，即使每个token只激活少量专家，全量专家权重仍需要数十GB显存。现有专家剪枝方法通常对所有token的重要性进行统一聚合，忽略了同一专家在多模态场景中可能承担不同功能：对视觉理解和语言处理分别具有不同重要性。因此，需要一种无需训练、能够按模态分解专家重要性并保持跨数据集稳定性的压缩方法。

Method: 论文提出MAEP（Modality-Aware Expert Pruning），是一种训练无关的MoE专家剪枝框架。该方法主要包括两点：第一，提出Delta-H输出指标，即通过测量移除某个专家后MoE模块输出H的变化，来感知专家对输出分布的贡献和重分配效应，而不是简单依赖路由权重或token级统计。第二，利用低注意力视觉token作为视觉侧剪枝信号，认为低注意力的视觉token对应的专家贡献对性能影响较小，并且这种信号在不同数据集上具有稳定的专家重要性排序；同时，文本侧重要性作为保留信号，用于保护对语言能力关键的专家。最终，MAEP在单次前向传播中分解视觉和文本模态下的专家重要性，并在所有层之间对专家进行全局排序，从而选择要剪枝的专家参数。

Result: 实验在三个MoE架构的多模态大语言模型上进行了评测，覆盖多模态基准和纯文本基准。结果表明，MAEP在多数多模态任务上优于不考虑模态差异的剪枝基线，同时在文本任务上保持有竞争力的性能。压缩效果方面，MAEP约减少27GB BF16专家权重，相当于总显存占用约47%的降低，使原本约58GB的模型完整权重能够装入单张40GB GPU。作者还公开了代码。

Conclusion: MAEP证明了在MoE-MLLM压缩中忽略专家跨模态功能差异会损害剪枝效果。通过训练无关、单次前向传播的模态感知重要性分解，该方法能够在不重新训练模型的情况下显著减少专家权重占用的显存，并维持较好的多模态能力。该研究为MoE-MLLM的低显存部署提供了一种实用压缩路径，尤其适合在消费级或单GPU资源受限场景下部署大型多模态模型。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) enables multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to scale capacity efficiently, but expert parameters dominate memory even though only a few experts are activated per token: they account for over 90% of model size in modern MoE-MLLMs (e.g., 54 GB out of about 58 GB in BF16 for InternVL3.5-30B-A3B and Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B). Expert pruning has emerged as an effective compression strategy, but existing methods aggregate importance uniformly across tokens, ignoring that the same expert may serve different roles for vision versus language processing. To address this, we propose MAEP (Modality-Aware Expert Pruning), a training-free framework that decomposes expert importance by modality in a single forward pass. MAEP introduces (1) Delta-H Output ($\Delta H$), a redistribution-aware metric measuring the MoE output change when an expert is removed, and (2) low-attention vision tokens as a vision-side pruning signal that is dataset-stable: its expert-importance ranking remains stable across different datasets. Text-side importance serves as a preservation signal while vision-side importance serves as a pruning signal, and experts are ranked globally across layers. Experiments on three MoE-based MLLMs across multimodal and text benchmarks show that MAEP outperforms modality-agnostic baselines on the majority of multimodal benchmarks while remaining competitive on text-only tasks, reducing the BF16 expert weight footprint by about 27 GB (about 47% of total memory) so that the full weight footprint of an approximately 58 GB model fits within a single 40 GB GPU. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [30] [FLINT: Coupling Proximal Initialization and Bounded Stochasticity for Flow-Matching Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05945)
*Junseo Bang,Daewon Choi,Dong Ju Mun,Se Young Chun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向成像逆问题后验采样的统一视角：轨迹空间求解策略与源空间初始化优化并非独立，而是通过“初始噪声距离”和“采样随机性累积”形成耦合稳定性关系。基于这一理论联系，作者提出 FLINT 框架，先用少量迭代获得高保真的源初始化，再通过受限随机性调度进行引导采样，从而在质量、失真指标与计算效率上取得更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow matching 模型已成为成像逆问题中生成式后验采样的主流方法。现有求解器通常沿两条路线发展：一是轨迹空间方法，重点改进从随机初始噪声出发的采样动力学；二是源空间方法，重点优化初始噪声本身。以往工作多将二者视为独立设计选择，但本文发现它们之间存在内在耦合，需要统一建模并据此设计更稳定、高效的采样流程。

Method: 作者推导了一个统一稳定性界，用乘性包络刻画初始化接近程度与采样过程中累积随机性之间的关系，并通过两项经验观察补充该理论：首先，存在一个充分区间，适度的近端源初始化即可达到性能平台，无需穷尽式优化源噪声；其次，这种初始化优势较为脆弱，会被引导采样中过度的随机性累积侵蚀。基于此，作者提出 FLINT 两阶段框架：第一阶段通过流形约束的近端目标在少量迭代内建立高保真起始点；第二阶段采用有界随机性调度进行引导采样，以保护初始化优势。

Result: 在多种成像逆问题中，FLINT 达到了最先进的感知质量，并保持具有竞争力的失真指标，同时展现出优于现有方法的计算效率。

Conclusion: 轨迹空间采样与源空间初始化不应被分开设计；二者的耦合稳定性是提升生成式逆问题采样性能的关键。通过少量优化获得稳健初始化，并在采样阶段严格限制累积随机性，能够同时改善质量、稳定性与效率。FLINT 为这一耦合机制提供了可操作的框架。

Abstract: Flow matching models have emerged as a dominant paradigm for generative posterior sampling in imaging inverse problems. Existing solvers follow two distinct lines of work: trajectory-space methods, which refine sampling dynamics from random initial noise, and source-space methods, which optimize the initial noise itself. While most prior work treats these as independent design choices, we show that they are inherently coupled. We derive a unified stability bound that links initialization proximity and cumulative sampling stochasticity through a multiplicative envelope, complemented by two empirical observations. First, we identify a sufficiency regime in which a moderately optimized proximal source initialization is enough to attain the performance plateau, rendering exhaustive source-space optimization unnecessary. Second, this initial advantage is fragile: it is eroded by excessive stochasticity accumulation during guided sampling, necessitating a mechanism that bounds the cumulative noise injection. These findings motivate FLINT, a two-stage framework that operationalizes this coupling. FLINT establishes a high-fidelity starting point via a manifold-constrained proximal objective in only a few iterations, then performs guided sampling under a bounded stochasticity schedule that helps prevent erasure of the initialization advantage. Across diverse inverse problems, FLINT delivers state of the art perceptual quality and competitive distortion metrics while maintaining superior computational efficiency.

</details>


### [31] [CLEAR: Color-Texture Latent Alignment for Single Image Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05969)
*Tu Vo,Sheir A. Zaheer,Chan Y. Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CLEAR，一种基于预训练潜在扩散先验的单步非迭代潜在框架，通过物理引导分离与混合特征对齐，在单幅图像反射去除中兼顾保真度与感知质量，并在六个真实基准上取得最佳平均表现及最低 LPIPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在恢复方法常利用预训练生成先验，并将退化输入的高频特征通过 skip connection 回注解码器；这对模糊、噪声、雾霾等退化有效，但在单幅图像反射去除（SIRR）中，反射会引入第二层结构化干扰，直接复用输入高频细节可能恢复纹理的同时重新引入反射边缘，且潜在压缩还会造成颜色漂移和细节损失。

Method: 提出 CLEAR（Color-Texture LatEnt Alignment for Single Image Reflection Removal），其采用确定性、单时间步、非迭代的潜在框架，并以预训练潜在扩散模型作为自然图像先验。方法包含：（1）物理引导分离器，围绕透射、反射和残差形成建模，单次前向输出粗粒度分层潜变量；（2）混合特征对齐模块（HFAM），将来自输入的高频 skip 特征视为被污染的层混合特征，利用从输入特征自身学习、并以真值特征监督的 clean-transmission 对齐颜色查询来校正高频内容，从而保留与透射层一致的纹理并削弱带反射颜色的细节。

Result: 在六个真实世界基准上，CLEAR 在保真度指标和感知指标的综合平均值上取得最佳结果，并在所有数据集上获得最低 LPIPS；在难度最高的基准上提升幅度最大。

Conclusion: 对于 SIRR，不能简单将输入高频 skip 特征直接回注解码器；通过物理层分解与颜色—纹理对齐来识别和校正反射污染特征，可在恢复透射场景细节的同时抑制反射边缘和颜色漂移，实现更可靠的单幅图像反射去除。

Abstract: Single Image Reflection Removal (SIRR) seeks to recover the transmitted scene from a photograph captured through glass. Recent latent restoration methods use pretrained generative priors, but many recover detail by feeding high-frequency features from the degraded input back into the decoder. This works for degradations such as blur, noise, or haze, where input detail mostly belongs to the scene. It is less suitable for SIRR, where reflections add a second structured layer: directly reusing the input's skip connections can sharpen the image while also bringing reflection edges back. Latent compression also introduces color drift and fine-detail loss. We propose CLEAR (Color-Texture LatEnt Alignment for Single Image Reflection Removal), a deterministic, single-timestep, non-iterative latent framework that uses a pretrained latent diffusion backbone as a natural-image prior. A physics-guided separator organizes the prediction around the transmission, reflection, and residue formation model and outputs coarse layer latents in one forward pass. The Hybrid Feature Alignment Module (HFAM) then treats input-derived skips as contaminated layer-mixture features. It rectifies their high-frequency content with a clean-transmission-aligned color query, learned from the input features themselves under ground-truth feature supervision, keeping transmission-consistent texture while attenuating reflection-colored detail. CLEAR achieves the best average across fidelity and perceptual metrics on six real-world benchmarks and the lowest LPIPS on every dataset, with the largest gains on the hardest benchmarks.

</details>


### [32] [Clips2Story: Training-Free Video Storyboarding and Editing Using Multimodal Retrieval-Embedded Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05992)
*Junnuo Wang,Weihan Xu,Hao-Wen Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Clips2Story，一个基于大语言模型且无需训练的视频片段编辑流水线，能够从无序视频素材池中根据用户关键词检索、筛选并组织片段，自动生成具有连贯叙述的短视频和故事板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际视频编辑场景中，创作者常面对大量碎片化、缺乏时间顺序的视频素材，需要从这些片段中筛选并组织成连贯故事。然而，许多现有视频编辑或摘要方法默认输入是时间有序的视频，并试图保留原始叙事结构，难以满足基于无序素材池和用户关键词进行创意剪辑与故事生成的需求。

Method: Clips2Story采用多阶段流水线：首先，为视频素材池中的每个片段生成基于文本的多模态描述；其次，根据用户指定关键词检索最相关的候选片段；然后，将候选片段文本描述输入大语言模型，由LLM选择片段并排序生成包含连接性旁白的可编辑故事板；最后，拼接选中片段，并根据旁白检索或匹配相应画面，合成输出视频。该方法为训练-free pipeline，主要依赖多模态描述、检索模块和大语言模型规划能力。

Result: 作者从100部涵盖五种类型的长视频中构建合成评测数据集：利用场景切换检测将长视频切分为短片段，并打乱片段顺序以模拟无序素材池。客观评测和定性示例表明，Clips2Story能够检索与关键词相关的片段，并生成语义上连贯的故事板。人类评估进一步显示，相比强基线，该方法在叙事连贯性、关键词匹配度和整体真实感方面表现更优。

Conclusion: Clips2Story展示了面向无序视频素材池的关键词驱动短视频自动生成能力，验证了将多模态描述、检索和大语言模型规划相结合可用于片段选择、排序和叙事组织。其无需训练的特点增强了系统灵活性和可扩展性，但评测主要基于构造场景和短片语料，实际复杂素材库、多模态一致性、长视频叙事规划和生成效率仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Video editors often work with large collections of fragmented footage and must select and organize relevant clips into a coherent story. However, many existing video editing and summarization methods assume a temporally ordered source video and aim to preserve its original narrative and temporal structure. In this paper, we introduce Clips2Story, a training-free, LLM-based pipeline that enables users to create keyword-specified short videos from a pool of video clips. Given a pool of clips and a user-specified keyword, we first extract text-based multimodal descriptors for each clip and retrieve the clips most relevant to the keyword. We then provide the textual descriptors of these candidate clips to an LLM, which selects and arranges them into an editable storyboard with connective narrations. Finally, we assemble the selected clips and retrieve accompanying visuals for the narrations to compose the output video. To evaluate the proposed pipeline, we construct a synthetic evaluation dataset from 100 long-form videos across five genres by segmenting each video into short clips using a scene-change detection model and shuffling the resulting clips to remove their temporal order. Through objective evaluation and qualitative examples, we demonstrate that Clips2Story can effectively retrieve keyword-relevant clips and compose coherent storyboards based on user-specified keywords. Human evaluation further shows that Clips2Story outperforms a strong baseline in terms of narrative coherence, keyword alignment, and overall realism.

</details>


### [33] [FedIA: Instance-Aware Cross-Modal Representations for Federated Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05994)
*Xuanjing Zhang,Qi Guo,Yanan Qiao,Yong Han,Junge Bo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FedIA提出面向联邦提示学习的实例感知跨模态表示方法，将个性化从客户端级别提升到样本实例级别，并通过自适应全局—局部融合提升联邦视觉语言模型的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦提示学习方法通常使用全局共享提示和客户端专属本地提示来应对跨客户端数据异质性，但本地提示在同一客户端内对所有样本固定使用，且全局与本地提示以输入无关的固定方式融合。这种设计忽略了同一客户端内部样本的显著视觉差异，也无法根据具体输入动态平衡共享知识与个性化知识。
