Method: FedIA构建实例感知跨模态表示：首先使用全局文本提示作为客户端不变语义锚点，选择与候选类别最相关的图像区域以抑制干扰；随后基于被选择的视觉证据生成实例感知的本地文本表示；最后通过自适应全局—局部融合模块，针对每个图像—类别组合校准本地表示与全局表示的贡献。

Result: 在域偏移、病理标签倾斜和Dirichlet标签倾斜等多种联邦非独立同分布设置下，FedIA均优于现有FPL基线，相对于最新基线在三类设置中的最高准确率提升为1.44%至4.37%。消融实验进一步验证了实例感知表示生成与自适应全局—局部融合的互补作用。

Conclusion: FedIA在不交换原始数据的前提下，将联邦提示学习的个性化粒度由客户端级别细化到实例级别，同时保留跨客户端共享的语义知识，为异质性联邦视觉语言学习提供了更细粒度的输入自适应提示建模方案。

Abstract: Federated Prompt Learning (FPL) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of pre-trained Vision-Language Models across decentralized clients without exchanging raw data. To cope with cross-client heterogeneity, existing FPL methods typically pair a globally shared prompt with a client-specific local prompt. However, this design personalizes only at the client level: the same local prompt is applied to all samples of a client, and the global and local prompts are combined in an input-agnostic, fixed manner. Consequently, it overlooks the substantial visual variations among samples within the same client and cannot adjust the balance between shared and personalized knowledge to each input. To address these limitations, we propose Federated Prompt Learning with Instance-Aware Cross-Modal Representations (FedIA), an FPL framework that learns instance-aware cross-modal representations. Specifically, FedIA first uses global text prompts as client-invariant semantic anchors to select image patches most relevant to each candidate class, suppressing distracting regions. The selected visual evidence then conditions the generation of instance-aware local text representations. Finally, an adaptive global-local fusion module calibrates the contributions of local and global text representations for each image-class pair. In this way, FedIA shifts personalization in FPL from the client level to the instance level while preserving the semantic knowledge shared across clients. Extensive experiments under domain shift, pathological label skew, and Dirichlet-based label skew show that FedIA outperforms FPL baselines, with maximum accuracy gains of 1.44\%-4.37\% over the latest baseline across the three settings. Ablation studies and analyses further verify the complementary contributions of instance-aware representation generation and adaptive global-local fusion.

</details>


### [34] [MotoVerse: A Benchmark for Predictive Perception of Motorized Two-Wheelers in Dense Unstructured Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06007)
*Varun A. Paturkar,Shankar Gangisetty,C.V. Jawahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出MotoVerse，一个面向机动两轮车（MTW）自车视角的预测感知基准，包含949个密集非结构化交通场景下的机动片段、超过31000个道路参与者的轨迹标注，并定义自车骑手机动预判（ERMA）与交通参与者轨迹预测（TATP）两项任务，用于评测传统预测模型与视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机动两轮车在全球南方地区广泛承担个人出行，但在自动驾驶与ADAS的预测感知研究中长期被忽视。现有基准多围绕汽车视角和结构化交通设计，难以刻画MTW在密集、弱规则交通中的动态自车运动、遮挡和复杂交互问题。

Method: 作者构建MotoVerse数据集，从MTW自车中心视角采集真实交通机动片段，并为周围道路参与者提供轨迹标注。每个片段还附有MTW特有挑战分类，包括动态自车运动、弱交通结构和上下文遮挡，从而支持挑战感知评估。基准包含两个互补任务：ERMA用于预测骑手即将执行的机动，TATP用于预测周围交通参与者的未来轨迹。研究进一步评测常规预测模型、通用VLM、驾驶专用VLM和视觉-语言-动作模型，并开展初步LoRA适配实验。

Result: 实验表明：（1）基于视频骨干的模型在机动开始约五分之一时即可接近完整观测精度，而零样本VLM通常需要观察到大部分机动过程；（2）无论通用还是驾驶专用零样本VLM，在两项任务上大多仅接近简单基线；LoRA适配可显著提升骑手机动预判能力，但轨迹预测仍不及传统模型；（3）自车运动本身显著影响预测难度，各类模型包括静态基线在左转弯时误差最高，并在动态自车运动条件下误差上升。

Conclusion: MotoVerse为智能两轮车的预测感知研究提供了高挑战性、面向真实场景的基准，揭示了现有VLM在MTW ego-centric任务上的不足，并强调自车运动、非结构化交通和遮挡对预测模型鲁棒性的关键影响。公开数据集与评测代码有助于推动更安全、更稳健的两轮车智能预测系统研究。

Abstract: Motorized Two-Wheelers (MTWs) dominate personal transport across much of the Global South, yet remain largely overlooked in predictive perception research for autonomous driving and ADAS. We introduce MotoVerse, a benchmark for predictive perception from an MTW's ego-centric perspective. MotoVerse comprises 949 maneuver clips collected in dense, unstructured traffic and trajectory annotations for over 31,000 surrounding road agents, including over 16,000 MTWs, enabling two complementary tasks: Ego-Rider Maneuver Anticipation (ERMA) and Traffic Agent Trajectory Prediction (TATP). Each clip is further annotated with an MTW-specific challenge taxonomy comprising dynamic ego-motion, weak traffic structure and contextual occlusion, enabling challenge-aware evaluation. We benchmark established conventional prediction models, general-purpose Vision-Language Models (VLMs), driving-specific VLMs and vision-language-action models. Our experiments reveal three key insights: (i) video backbones reach most of their full-observation accuracy from the first fifth of a maneuver, whereas zero-shot VLMs need most of the maneuver to be observed; (ii) zero-shot VLMs, whether general-purpose or driving-specific, largely remain close to simple baselines on both tasks; a preliminary LoRA study shows that adaptation lifts anticipation sharply but leaves trajectory prediction behind conventional models; and (iii) the rider's own motion drives forecasting difficulty: for every model, including a static baseline, errors are highest during left turns and rise under dynamic ego-motion. We believe MotoVerse will serve as a challenging benchmark for safer and more robust predictive AI for intelligent two-wheelers. The dataset and evaluation code are publicly available at this https URL.

</details>


### [35] [METRIC-GS: Selective Metric-Scale Estimation for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06020)
*Sooyeun Yang,Jongseong Brad Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 METRIC-GS，一种面向真实室内 3D Gaussian Splatting 重建的“选择性尺度估计”方法：从重建自身的语义内容中推断公制尺度，并在证据不足时主动弃权，必要时路由到重型公制深度/度量模型，从而获得更可靠、可部署的尺度恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS/SfM 重建通常具有任意尺度，无法直接用于测量或增强现实内容摆放。常见做法（如室内层高启发式、与公制深度网络对齐）在真实拍摄中不稳定、误差大，且现有公制模型在已见基准上的成绩可能显著高估其部署性能，因此需要一种可靠、可控且能识别证据不足的尺度估计方案。

Method: METRIC-GS 利用冻结的 2D 教师模型对高斯体进行可见性感知的实例投票标注；针对门、桌子、地板到天花板距离等建筑与家具类别引入实例级尺寸先验；通过鲁棒最大似然融合多证据估计尺度；并用 inlier-mass 检验判断证据是否充分，证据薄弱时显式弃权，并将这些样本路由给更重的公制模型（如 MASt3R）。

Result: 在 47 个 ScanNet++ 场景和激光尺度真值下，方法将启发式平均误差从 21.1% 降至 16.6%，在其实际处理的 28 个场景中误差为 9.3%，约每场景 11 秒。MASt3R 单独使用更准（9.9%），但仅将 METRIC-GS 拒判场景路由给 MASt3R，可在少调用 60% 的情况下达到类似效果。可见性感知投票使可处理场景数从 16 提升到 28 且部署精度不变。作者自采 13 组手机/无人机数据上，MASt3R 多次未通过自身注册门限，而该系统在其处理的 5 组中均改善、无退化。此外，某前馈公制模型在训练集内基准得 2.3%，在未见采集上为 28–35%，提示公制尺度结果需区分最佳情况与部署估计。

Conclusion: 选择性尺度估计比单一启发式或单一公制模型更适合真实场景：通过语义先验、鲁棒融合与显式弃权机制，METRIC-GS 能在可操作场景上提供较可靠尺度，并用路由策略兼顾精度与计算成本。该工作也强调，公制尺度评估必须包含未见数据与拒绝机制，公开指标可能高估真实部署性能。

Abstract: A 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstruction inherits arbitrary units from structure-from-motion, so it cannot be measured or used to place AR content until someone supplies a scale. Both standard recipes are fragile on real captures: on five indoor scenes with tape-measured ceilings, a quantile floor-to-ceiling heuristic errs by 27% and metric-depth alignment by 35-71% across three networks. We present METRIC-GS, a selective scale estimator for real indoor photographs: it reads the scale off the reconstruction's own semantic content and, just as importantly, refuses when the evidence is thin. A frozen 2D teacher labels Gaussians by visibility-aware voting; instance-level size priors for architectural and furniture classes (doors, tables, the floor-to-ceiling gap) are fused by a robust maximum-likelihood step; and an inlier-mass test turns thin evidence into an explicit abstention that doubles as a router to a heavyweight metric model. On 47 ScanNet++ scenes with laser ground truth, with a configuration frozen before public evaluation, the estimator alone cuts the heuristic's mean error from 21.1% to 16.6% (9.3% on the 28 scenes it acts on) at ~11 s per scene. MASt3R remains the more accurate standalone estimator (9.9%), but routing only our refusals to it matches running it everywhere with 60% fewer invocations; this selective hybrid is the configuration we advocate. Visibility-aware voting raises the scenes the estimator can act on from 16 to 28 at unchanged deployed accuracy. Applied unchanged to 13 phone and drone captures of our own, on which MASt3R fails its own registration gate 12 times, the system improves all five captures it acts on and degrades none. We also report that a current feed-forward metric model scores 2.3% on a benchmark inside its training mixture and 28-35% on captures it has never seen: a published metric-scale number is a best case, not a deployment estimate.

</details>


### [36] [USI-Brain: Enhancing Underwater Spatial Intelligence via Spatial Data-Driven Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06040)
*Zhiliang Zhu,Zhiqiang Zhao,Xie'an Jiang,Yingxin Lai,Kangye Zheng,Dawei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 面向水下空间推理，作者构建空间数据引擎生成427万QA，训练USI-Brain并提出水下先验引导校正器UPR，在USI-Bench及外部基准上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像受散射、颜色畸变和低能见度影响，物体边界与几何线索难以获取；现有水下数据虽包含图像、标注和深度图，但对视觉-语言模型而言监督分散、空间几何信息利用不足。因此需要连接视觉外观与对象级几何的结构化数据、模型方法和评测基准。

Method: 提出水下空间数据引擎，通过图像增强、2D与几何辅助问题生成以及专家过滤，构建覆盖五类能力的427万QA语料。基于该语料训练USI-Brain，并引入水下先验引导校正器UPR，对视觉特征进行门控式校正。进一步提出USI-Bench，使用6525个留出的QA，对15项任务能力进行评估；评估中两种模型均使用原始图像。另在NautData、UWBench和MarineEval上验证泛化表现。

Result: USI-Brain相对Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct在USI-Bench的15项任务平均提升5.76分，USI-Brain+UPR提升7.63分，采用百分制下的有符号差异。消融实验显示，在关闭UPR时，几何辅助监督将深度能力与排序能力分别提升4.8和6.1分；在训练数据匹配条件下，UPR使场景描述提升18.0分。跨NautData、UWBench和MarineEval共32项指标中，USI-Brain在28项上取得最佳或并列最佳成绩。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将水下图像增强、对象级几何监督和先验校正相结合，可有效提升视觉-语言模型在水下空间场景中的理解与推理能力。论文贡献包括空间数据引擎、USI-Brain模型、UPR模块以及USI-Bench评测基准；模型权重和基准计划发布，有助于推动水下多模态空间推理研究。

Abstract: Underwater spatial reasoning depends on object boundaries and geometric cues that are obscured by scattering, color distortion, and low visibility. Existing underwater resources capture these cues through images, annotations, and depth maps, but provide fragmented supervision for vision-language models. We present a spatial data engine that connects visual appearance with object-level geometry through enhancement, 2D and geometry-assisted question generation, and expert filtering. It produces 4.27M QA pairs across five capability groups. We use this corpus to train USI-Brain and introduce an Underwater Prior-guided Rectifier (UPR) that applies gated corrections to visual features. USI-Bench evaluates the resulting capabilities with 6,525 held-out QA pairs. On its 15 task metrics, USI-Brain and USI-Brain + UPR improve over Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct by 5.76 and 7.63 points on average, respectively, using signed differences on 100-point scales. Ablations show that geometry-assisted supervision improves depth and ordering by 4.8 and 6.1 points with UPR disabled, while UPR adds 18.0 points in scene description under matched training data. Both variants use raw images at evaluation. Across NautData, UWBench, and MarineEval, USI-Brain achieves the best or joint-best score on 28 of 32 metrics. Model weights and USI-Bench will be released at this https URL.

</details>


### [37] [UniRestorer++: Scalable Universal Image Restoration with Multi-Granularity Degradation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06042)
*Jingbo Lin,Zhilu Zhang,Fan Li,Renjing Pei,Wenbo Li,Hang Xu,Hongzhi Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 UniRestorer++，通过多粒度退化建模、组专用专家、路径/层级路由和空间稠密退化表示，实现通用图像恢复的可靠扩展，并在跨分布与真实场景取得更强 scaling 效果和 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用图像恢复希望用单一模型处理多种退化，但现有扩展策略不够可靠：单纯增加骨干深度和宽度容易性能饱和，而依赖退化感知的专用化又需要准确估计退化类型，在分布外和真实图像上容易因估计错误而失效。

Method: 提出基于多粒度退化建模的可扩展框架 UniRestorer++。模型逐步细化退化分组，并为不同组引入 group-specific experts，使容量增长与专用化能力耦合。通过基于退化估计的路由选择根到叶路径，再通过基于粒度估计的路由选择路径中的层级，以平衡专用化与鲁棒性。同时利用空间稠密退化表示图实现区域感知路由，以处理空间上非均匀的混合退化。还构建包含从单一退化到五重退化共 50 类退化的 CDD-50 基准。

Result: 在评测模型规模范围内，PSNR 随模型规模呈现近似 log-linear 提升，相比普通 MoE scaling 在分布外和真实数据上获得更强的扩展增益。UniRestorer++ 在多个基准上取得 SOTA，并展示出对真实图像的 zero-shot 泛化和 few-shot 迁移能力。

Conclusion: 多粒度退化建模和分组专家机制能够为通用图像恢复提供比单纯扩大骨干或普通 MoE 更可靠的 scaling 路径，使模型在保持鲁棒性的同时提升对复杂、多样和空间非均匀退化的处理能力。

Abstract: Universal image restoration aims to handle diverse degradations within a single model, but a reliable scaling strategy remains underexplored. Increasing backbone depth and width can lead to performance saturation, while degradation-aware specialization depends on accurate degradation estimation and is vulnerable to errors on out-of-distribution and real-world inputs. We propose UniRestorer++, a scalable framework based on multi-granularity degradation modeling. It progressively refines degradation groups and introduces group-specific experts, coupling capacity growth with specialization. Degradation-estimation-based routing selects a root-to-leaf path, while granularity-estimation-based routing chooses a level along that path to balance specialization and robustness. Spatially dense degradation representation maps further enable region-aware routing for spatially non-uniform corruptions. We develop CDD-50, a benchmark covering 50 degradation categories from single to penta-degradation. Experiments show approximately log-linear PSNR trends over the evaluated model-size range and stronger scaling gains on out-of-distribution and real-world data than vanilla MoE scaling. UniRestorer++ achieves state-of-the-art performance across benchmarks and demonstrates zero-shot generalization and few-shot transfer on real-world images. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [38] [DeepfakeGenome: Toward Next-Generation Deepfake Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06046)
*Shunli Wang,Xinyu Zhou,Yandan Zhao,Wenbin Liang,Taiping Yao,Ke-Yue Zhang,Zhiyuan Yan,Shouhong Ding,Lizhuang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出面向深度伪造检测与归因的综合基准DeepfakeGenome（DFG），包含100种人脸伪造算法、200万图像和4种实用评测协议，尤其提出检索式归因范式，并基于大量实验分析任务性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIGC生成的超真实人脸伪造图像严重威胁人脸安全，但现有深度伪造检测与归因研究存在二分类性能饱和、数据集与算法多样性不足、评测协议不完善等问题，制约实际应用。

Method: 构建大规模深度伪造检测与归因基准DFG，覆盖100种伪造算法和200万图像；设计4种实用评测协议，其中提出面向真实黑名单注册防护场景的检索式归因范式，并进行超过2000次实验以研究归因任务的性能上限。

Result: 相比以往归因基准，DFG在规模上扩大4至100倍；大量实验得到10条有启发性的发现，为深度伪造检测与归因研究提供更全面的评测基础和实践参考。

Conclusion: 该工作通过大规模数据、创新评测协议和系统实验揭示了当前深度伪造归因能力的边界，有望为未来DF检测与归因研究提供新的思路、基准和方向。

Abstract: In recent years, AIGC technologies are capable of generating hyper-realistic forged facial images, posing severe threats to facial security safety. To tackle these challenges, early Deepfake research primarily centered on real-fake classification. Recently, growing efforts have been devoted to the Deepfake Attribution (DFA) of generated content. Nevertheless, existing research on Deepfake detection and attribution faces saturated performance in binary classification, limited diversity in datasets and algorithms, and imperfect evaluation protocols, which severely impede practical application. To address these limitations, we propose a comprehensive deepfake detection and attribution benchmark named DeepfakeGenome (DFG). It contains 100 facial forgery algorithms and 2M images in total, achieving 4x to 100x larger than prior DFA benchmarks. We further design 4 protocols for practical evaluation, including a novel retrieval-based attribution paradigm. Unlike previous open-set evaluation metrics, the proposed retrieval metrics are more aligned with the real-world active defense situation of blacklist registration mechanisms. Based on these elaborate designs, we investigate the performance ceiling of deepfake attribution task. Over 2k experimental evaluations are conducted, and 10 insightful findings are derived. We believe this work can provide new insights into the DFA research field. The Code and Benchmark of DFG are avaliable on this https URL.

</details>


### [39] [RI-Prune: Relation and Information Aware Visual Token Pruning for Multi-Image Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06047)
*Dongjin Lee,Eunju Lee,Jinhee Jang,Yoonji Lee,Youngbin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RI-Prune，一种免训练、不依赖注意力的多图视觉 Token 预解码器选择器，通过查询相关性、跨图像对应关系和图像内代表性进行均衡 Top-K 裁剪，以提升多图 LVLM 的推理效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多图大视觉语言模型会显著增加视觉 Token 预算，使 Token 裁剪成为必要。现有方法主要面向单图场景，依赖注意力估计 Token 重要性，但在多图场景下出现性能下降、位置偏置以及注意力判别信号减弱等问题。

Method: 作者先用 Leave-One-In 和 Leave-One-Deletion 两种因果探测分析注意力选择 Token 是否真正重要；随后提出 RI-Prune，从投影器输出中计算三种信号并进行 z-normalized 融合：查询相关性、与紧凑每图原型的跨图像对应关系、以及图像内代表性，最后按每图均衡的 Top-K 选择视觉 Token。

Result: 在多个多图基准和三个 LVLM 主干模型上，RI-Prune 在 Token 削减与任务性能之间取得优于已有裁剪方法的折中，尤其是在高压缩率或激进 Token 预算下表现更明显。

Conclusion: 注意力信号在多图视觉 Token 重要性估计中较弱，基于关系与信息感知的无注意力、免训练裁剪方法能更有效地支持多图 LVLM 的高效推理。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently been extended to handle multiple input images, but this extension dramatically increases the visual token budget, making visual-token pruning not merely an efficiency option but a practical necessity. Existing visual-token pruning methods, primarily designed for the single-image setting, rely on attention-derived saliency signals to estimate per-token importance. However, this attention-centric design transfers poorly to the multi-image regime: it causes substantial performance degradation on multi-image benchmarks, suffers from strong positional bias, and loses discriminative attention signals as the number of input images increases. To test whether attention-selected tokens are truly important for multi-image reasoning, we conduct two complementary causal probes Leave-One-In and Leave-One-Deletion, which measure token importance by restoring individual tokens to a zeroed visual sequence or removing them from the full sequence. Attention-based pruners provide only marginal gains over random selection, showing that attention is a weak proxy for visual-token importance in multi-image reasoning. Motivated by this gap, we propose Relation and Information Aware Pruning (RI-Prune), a training-free, attention-free pre-decoder selector for multi-image visual token pruning. RI-Prune scores each visual token using a z-normalized combination of three signals computed from projector outputs: query relevance, cross-image correspondence with compact per-image prototypes, and intra image representativeness. By integrating these signals, RI-Prune performs balanced per-image Top-K selection to preserve information across input images. Across multiple multi-image benchmarks and three LVLM backbones, RI-Prune achieves a stronger trade-off between token reduction and task performance than prior pruning methods, particularly under aggressive token budgets.

</details>


### [40] [SVGFaith: A Two-Layer Meta-Evaluation of Faithfulness Metrics for Text-to-SVG Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06048)
*Guotao Liang,Haitao Zhou,Biao Liu,Huanxuan Liao,Lingfeng Qiao,Qian-Wen Zhang,Qian Yu,Di Yin,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 SVGFaith 元评估框架和 SVGFaith-Score 可解释评分指标，用于评估文本到 SVG 生成模型的细粒度提示忠实度；实验表明现有图像文本指标难以准确捕捉空间关系和属性绑定，而 SVGFaith-Score 在两个评估集上显著优于九个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到 SVG 系统通常通过将生成结果光栅化后使用图像指标来评估，但这类分数是否真正反映细粒度提示忠实度尚不明确，尤其在对象、空间关系和属性绑定等复杂语义要求上缺乏可靠评估。

Method: 作者构建两层元评估框架：SVGFaith-Diagnostic 包含 1842 个受控对比样本，覆盖八种能力轴，分为“一个提示对应两个渲染结果”和“一个渲染结果对应两个描述”两个方向；SVGFaith-Arena 包含 1327 个人工标注的真实生成结果对比。评估指标 SVGFaith-Score 将提示通过领域调优分解器转化为原子视觉需求依赖图，再用视觉问答模型判断每项需求是否满足，并根据前置需求的得分对后续需求进行折扣，从而得到整体分数和逐项可解释分数。

Result: 在 SVGFaith-Diagnostic 上，CLIP 及另外三个图像文本嵌入指标在对象识别任务上的成对准确率至少达到 0.789，但在空间关系和属性绑定任务上最高仅为 0.672，而随机基线为 0.5，说明这些指标难以可靠捕捉细粒度语义忠实度。SVGFaith-Score 在 Arena 以及 Diagnostic 的两个方向上均显著优于包括 Qwen3.5-122B-A10B 评判器在内的九个基线，并能定位未满足需求及由前置需求导致的折扣。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了现有光栅化图像指标在评估文本到 SVG 生成系统细粒度忠实度方面的不足，并提供了面向 SVG 的元评估基准和可解释评分方法；SVGFaith-Score 通过依赖图式需求分解与前置折扣机制，提高了对对象、空间关系、属性绑定等复杂提示约束的评估能力。

Abstract: Text-to-SVG systems are commonly evaluated by applying raster-image metrics to rasterised outputs, yet whether these scores capture fine-grained prompt faithfulness remains unclear. We introduce SVGFaith, a two-layer meta-evaluation framework in which the more faithful candidate of each pair is determined by construction or by human judgment. SVGFaith-Diagnostic contains 1,842 controlled contrastive pairs across eight capability axes, split evenly between two directions: two renderings compared against one prompt, and two descriptions compared against one rendering. SVGFaith-Arena contains 1,327 human-annotated comparisons of real generator outputs. On SVGFaith-Diagnostic, CLIP and three other image--text embedding metrics reach at least 0.789 pairwise accuracy on object identity but at most 0.672 on spatial relations and attribute binding, where chance is 0.5. We further introduce SVGFaith-Score, an interpretable pointwise metric. A domain-tuned decomposer turns each prompt into a dependency graph of atomic visual requirements, a visual question answering model scores whether each requirement is satisfied, and a requirement's score is discounted when its prerequisites score low. In addition to the overall score, the per-requirement scores show which requirements are judged unsatisfied and which inherit discounts from their prerequisites. SVGFaith-Score significantly outperforms all nine baselines, including a Qwen3.5-122B-A10B judge, on Arena and in both Diagnostic directions. Code and data: this https URL.

</details>


### [41] [RouTex: Multi-reference 3D Texture Generation by Reprogramming Rotary Position Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06070)
*Chengyang Li,Yang Liu,Jiale Zhang,Qiang Rao,Wei Zhang,Taicheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RouTex，一种基于 in-context Diffusion Transformer 的多参考图像引导 3D 纹理生成框架，通过重编程 3D RoPE 与双粒度注意力实现参考视图到目标 3D 表面的精确对应与跨视角一致性，并取得新的 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像 3D 纹理生成方法已取得显著进展，但在仅依赖单一图像时难以准确生成未被观测区域的纹理表面。多视角参考图像能够提供更丰富的几何与外观线索，因此将多参考图像条件引入 3D 纹理生成对于缓解未观测区域的表面歧义非常重要，但该方向仍缺乏充分探索。

Method: RouTex 以 in-context Diffusion Transformer 为基础，核心观察是原生 3D Rotary Position Encoding 本身具有空间寻址能力。作者提出 Positional Routing，沿空间与语义维度对 3D RoPE 进行重编程，从而建立参考图像与目标视图之间的显式对应关系，且不增加额外参数。为了高效执行这种路由，提出 Dual-Granularity Attention：一方面对每个视图进行局部注意力，保留细粒度纹理保真度；另一方面对去重后的参考图像进行全局联合注意力，提升多视角生成结果之间的一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，RouTex 在多参考图像引导的 3D 纹理生成任务上取得了新的 state-of-the-art 性能，说明该方法能够在保持细节纹理质量的同时增强跨视角一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明，通过利用 3D RoPE 的空间寻址特性并设计轻量级的参考路由与双粒度注意力机制，可以有效解决多参考图像条件下的 3D 纹理生成问题。其贡献在于为多视角参考到目标表面的建模提供了显式且参数高效的方法，为更一致的 3D 内容生成开辟了新方向。

Abstract: While single-image 3D texture generation has advanced significantly, conditioning texture synthesis on multi-view reference images remains largely under-explored, despite its necessity for resolving surface ambiguity in unobserved regions. In this paper, we propose RouTex, a novel multi-reference 3D texture generation framework based on in-context Diffusion Transformers (DiT). Our key insight is that native 3D Rotary Position Encoding (RoPE) inherently possesses spatial addressing capabilities. We introduce Positional Routing (PR) to reprogram 3D RoPE along spatial and semantic dimensions, establishing explicit reference-to-view correspondence without introducing additional parameters. To execute this routing efficiently, Dual-Granularity Attention (DGA) combines per-view local attention for fine texture fidelity with global joint attention over deduplicated references for cross-view consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RouTex achieves new state-of-the-art performance in multi-reference 3D texture generation.

</details>


### [42] [SleuthVLM: Do We Really Need Audio-in-Video for Omni Video Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06083)
*Peiran Wu,Fangfei Gou,Zhaoxi Mu,Changhao Zhang,Jian Liu,Weiqiang Wang,Yuntao Wang,Yuanchun Shi,Junxiao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SleuthVLM 是一个基于 VLM 的视频理解智能体框架，不直接输入音频，而是通过语音转文本和自主视频推理完成长视频问答，并在多个 omni 视频基准上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 omni 模型主要依靠同时处理采样视频帧和原始音频，并通过扩大模型规模与多模态数据提升性能。作者质疑音频是否必须被模型直接“听见”，并探索能否用更强的 agentic 视频推理能力替代端到端音频-视频融合，以解决长视频理解中的信息检索、证据聚合和推理瓶颈。

Method: 提出 SleuthVLM：一个无需预定义工作流的智能体框架。该框架仅使用 VLM 处理视觉帧，语音被外部转写为文本；随后 VLM 自主“侦查”视频内容，根据问题主动搜索、查看和综合证据，完成跨模态理解与问答。

Result: 在四个包含最长 90 分钟视频的 omni 基准上，SleuthVLM 取得最佳平均准确率 75.0%，比 Gemini 3.8 Flash 高 2.6 个百分点；同时在 Video-MME-v2 排行榜上达到 77.8%。使用开源 27B VLM 时，其表现超过所有开源 omni 模型。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，对于 omni 视频理解，直接听见音频未必是关键瓶颈，智能体式推理与主动检索能力可能更重要。相比单纯扩大 omni 模型或增加模态对齐数据，构建更强的视频理解智能体可能更具前景。

Abstract: Omni models answer questions about videos by jointly processing sampled frames and audio in one turn, with progress driven largely by scaling model size and omni training data. We ask whether a model needs to hear the audio directly at all, and present SleuthVLM, an agent harness built on a VLM that never hears the audio directly: speech will be transcribed into text, and the VLM will sleuth the video on its own without any predefined workflow. On four omni benchmarks with videos up to 90 minutes, SleuthVLM achieves the best average accuracy: \textbf{75.0%}, 2.6 points above Gemini 3.8 Flash, and it also tops the Video-MME-v2 leaderboard with \textbf{77.8%}; with an open-source 27B VLM, it beats all open-source omni models. We draw three findings: SleuthVLM beats a model that glances; hearing is not the bottleneck, agentic ability is; omni understanding needs better agentic ability, not just more omni data for modality alignment. So we provide a simple but effective starting point for doing omni video understanding. Omni understanding needs better agents, not just bigger models that hear.

</details>


### [43] [GeoBiaset: A Counterfactual Benchmark for Demographic Bias in World-Level Geolocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06085)
*Niccolò Niccoli,Silvia Dani,Marco Mistretta,Andrew D. Bagdanov,Marco Bertini,Lorenzo Seidenari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 GeoBiaset，首个用于评估世界级图像地理定位模型如何受图像前景人物“看似种族”影响的基准，并引入 POPI 指标量化模型在反事实人物扰动下的预测偏移。实验发现，在缺少地标时，模型会从地理线索转向人脸特征，导致地理定位性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像地理定位模型存在地域偏差和数据集不平衡，但以往研究主要关注城市缺失或富裕地区表现更好等粗粒度现象，尚未深入分析哪些视觉线索会导致这种偏差。作者希望通过受控实验检验前景人物的人口属性，尤其是其“看似种族”，是否会影响模型对背景地点的判断，并揭示模型是否错误地将人脸等社会视觉线索用于地理定位。

Method: 作者构建 GeoBiaset 数据集，包含 9,541 张图像，将已有地理标签背景与经过反事实编辑的前景人物配对，人物覆盖七种种族类别，同时保持真实地理坐标不变。通过掩码、遮挡和背景漂移等对照实验排除简单视觉扰动的影响。此外，提出人物来源扰动指标 POPI，用于衡量模型在人物属性改变后预测是否被拉向该人物所属区域，从而评估模型对人口属性扰动的鲁棒性。

Result: 对先进地理定位模型和多模态大语言模型的评测表明，当插入与原始场景人口属性不匹配的人物时，模型在洲际地理定位层面的性能下降 16% 到 47%。地标有无分析和梯度加权热力图显示，模型在地标存在时主要关注地理地标，而当地标缺失时会转向关注人脸特征。受控掩码实验也表明，遮挡和背景漂移并不能完全解释性能下降。

Conclusion: 图像地理定位模型存在显著的人口属性相关偏差，尤其在地标稀缺时容易将人脸等与地理无关的视觉线索误用于地点推断。GeoBiaset 和 POPI 为系统测量此类偏差提供了新基准和指标，也提示未来模型需要增强对前景人物扰动的鲁棒性，并更严格地区分人物外观与地点证据。

Abstract: World-level image geolocalization is an increasingly important task, yet the biases of current models remain poorly understood. Prior work has identified coarse dataset imbalances noting, for instance, that some cities are absent from training sets and that models tend to perform better in wealthier regions. However, no one has yet examined which visual cues drive these disparities. To do so, we introduce GeoBiaset, the first benchmark designed to measure how geolocalization models are influenced by the apparent race of a person inserted into the image foreground. The dataset consists of 9,541 images, pairing existing geotagged background images with counterfactual edits spanning seven races, holding the ground-truth coordinates fixed. Controlled masking experiments show that occlusion and background drift do not fully explain geolocalization degradation. Alongside the benchmark, we introduce the Person-Origin Perturbation Indicators (POPI), novel metrics that quantify model resilience to these demographic perturbations by measuring whether predictions are pulled toward the region associated with the inserted group. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art geolocalization and multimodal large language models reveals a 16-47% drop in continent-level performance when a subject from a demographically mismatched group is inserted. A landmark versus no-landmark analysis and gradient-weighted heatmaps show that models preferentially attend to geographic landmarks when present, but shift attention to facial features in their absence.

</details>


### [44] [STARE: Scene Text-Aware multimodal Retrieval for Everyday objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06089)
*Kento Tokura,Ryosuke Korekata,Daichi Yashima,Kei Katsumata,Kanon Amemiya,Miyu Goko,Komei Sugiura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出面向日常物品检索与服务机器人任务的“Scene Text-Aware multimodal Retrieval for Everyday objects（STARE）”方法，通过融合视觉特征、空间关系和场景文本，提升机器人依据自由形式指令检索、识别和搬运物品的多模态理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常服务机器人需要根据自然语言自由指令完成物品检索和搬运，但指令往往同时包含视觉属性、空间关系以及环境中嵌入的场景文本信息。仅依赖普通视觉或文本模态难以准确理解复杂指令，因此需要面向场景文本感知的多模态检索方法。

Method: 提出STARE方法，利用视觉线索和文本线索响应自由形式指令。其核心设计包括“Crosslingual Visual Prompt”，用于增强对场景文本的解释能力，并对指令中的命名实体与场景中对应物体之间的复杂关系进行建模。研究还构建了两个新基准：GoGetIt和TextCaps-test，包含真实室内外图像以及涉及物体操作和导航的指令。

Result: 在标准多模态检索指标上，STARE优于所有基线方法。在零样本迁移设置下的真实物理机器人实验中，STARE取得了80%的整体成功率，验证了其在现实服务机器人任务中的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: STARE通过引入跨语言视觉提示和场景文本感知的多模态关系建模，显著提升了机器人对包含场景文本和复杂空间关系的自由指令的理解能力。实验和真实部署结果共同表明，该方法适用于现实环境中的日常物品检索与搬运任务，具有实际服务机器人应用价值。

Abstract: In this study, we focus on service robots that retrieve and transport everyday objects based on free-form instructions that may refer to visual features and scene text. This is challenging because it requires a multimodal understanding of the visual features and spatial relationships of objects, as well as scene text embedded in the environment. To address this, we propose Scene Text-Aware multimodal Retrieval for Everyday objects (STARE), which leverages visual and textual cues in response to free-form instructions. STARE introduces Crosslingual Visual Prompt to enhance scene text interpretation, and models the complex relationships between named entities in the instructions and their corresponding objects. We evaluate STARE using two novel benchmarks, GoGetIt and TextCaps-test, both of which consist of real-world indoor/outdoor images and instructions involving object manipulation and navigation. The results show that STARE outperforms all baseline methods on standard multimodal retrieval metrics. Furthermore, in physical experiments conducted under a zero-shot transfer setting, STARE achieves an overall success rate of 80%. Our project page and code are provided in the Supplementary Materials.

</details>


### [45] [Drift-adaptive Representation Preservation for Domain-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06093)
*Hyunmin Kong,Gyutae Oh,Jitae Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向域增量学习（Domain-Incremental Learning, DIL）的概念级遗忘追踪与校准方法：将“表征漂移”作为每个历史域-类概念遗忘风险的度量，并通过复用 JEPA（Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture）仅保存紧凑上下文摘要来在训练时估计该漂移，据此动态分配保护强度并修正类别原型，从而在 DomainNet、Office-Home、CORe50 和 CDDB-Hard 上取得较好的精度-遗忘平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉模型虽然能提供强泛化表征，但在域增量学习中，模型适应新分布时通常会灾难性遗忘旧域上的知识。作者观察到，这种遗忘并非均匀发生在所有域和类别上，也不能简单由训练顺序解释。因此，仅采用固定或全局的保护策略不足以有效缓解遗忘，需要以“历史域-类概念”为单元追踪其表示变化，并根据实际测得的遗忘风险动态调整保护分配。

Method: 方法核心是定义并使用“表征漂移”（representation drift）作为遗忘风险的代理指标：对每个过去的域-类概念，比较其当前表示相对于固定锚点表示的偏离程度。该漂移用于两个互补机制：其一，在整合损失（consolidation loss）中，对高漂移的域-类对施加更强的保持约束；其二，在类别原型构建过程中，降低高度漂移域对类参考表征的影响，使原型更可信。为了在不存储过去原始数据的条件下获得漂移信号，作者复用 JEPA 架构：仅对每个域-类概念在骨干网络特征空间中存储紧凑上下文摘要，再通过训练好的“上下文到目标表示”关系重新预测其目标表征，并与固定锚点比较以计算漂移。

Result: 在 DomainNet、Office-Home、CORe50 和 CDDB-Hard 四个基准上，该方法能够在所有数据集上保持较强的“精度-遗忘”平衡。与精度相当或具有竞争力的方法相比，该方法显著降低了训练内存需求，并在比较方法中实现了最小的存储开销，表明其概念级漂移度量与 JEPA 压缩上下文摘要机制在保留历史信息方面具有较高效率。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，DIL 中的遗忘问题可以通过概念级的表征漂移进行可测量化，并据此进行自适应保护与原型校正。相较于假设不同域或类别具有同等重要性，基于测量漂移分配保持资源更符合实际遗忘分布；同时，利用 JEPA 存储紧凑上下文摘要能够在不保留原始数据的条件下估计历史概念漂移，为增量视觉学习提供了一种轻量且有效的稳定性-可塑性权衡机制。

Abstract: Pretrained vision models provide strong general-purpose representations, but in Domain-Incremental Learning (DIL), where the input distribution changes sequentially, adapting to new domains often degrades performance on previously learned ones. We observe that forgetting is not uniform across domains and classes and is not explained by training order alone. This motivates us to track each past domain-class concept individually. We use representation drift, defined as the deviation of a past concept's current representation from a fixed anchor, as a measurable surrogate for forgetting risk. The measured drift then serves two complementary roles. A consolidation loss assigns stronger preservation to high-drift pairs, while prototype construction down-weights highly drifted domains to reduce their influence on the class reference. Preservation is thus allocated by measured drift rather than assumed importance. To obtain this signal during training without storing past raw data, we repurpose a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA). For each domain-class pair, we store only a compact context summary in the backbone feature space and rerun it through the learned context-to-target relation to measure its drift from the fixed anchor. Across DomainNet, Office-Home, CORe50, and CDDB-Hard, our method sustains a strong accuracy-forgetting balance on every benchmark, using substantially less training memory than accuracy-competitive methods and the smallest storage among compared methods.

</details>


### [46] [Semantic Prior-Guided Incomplete Cross-Modal Retrieval with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06113)
*Jiali Chen,Ruitao Pu,Yingke Chen,Dezhong Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPIRN框架，用于在模态缺失和标签噪声共同存在的场景中进行鲁棒的跨模态检索。核心思想是利用固定的语义先验作为一致参考，联合实现稳定表征学习、噪声标签处理、标签校正和缺失模态补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态检索依赖共享表示来弥合不同模态间的语义差异，但真实数据常因采集过程中的动态性和不一致性导致模态不完整。现有不完整跨模态检索方法主要依赖类别标签作为语义基准，然而标签噪声难以避免，且常与模态缺失同时存在。若仅依赖有噪声的标签进行监督，会降低模型鲁棒性，因此需要一种能同时应对模态缺失和标签噪声的新方法。

Method: 提出语义先验引导的不完整跨模态检索框架SPIRN。该框架以固定语义先验作为统一参考，主要包括四个模块：语义先验引导SPG用于建立稳定的类别级语义空间；质量感知鲁棒学习QRL用于评估样本质量、区分干净样本与噪声样本，并通过反向学习处理噪声；先验引导全局标签校准PGLC用于修正被污染的标注，提供更可靠的监督；置信度驱动的选择性补全CSI通过跨模态记忆库恢复缺失模态，并利用逐渐增高的阈值过滤不可靠的补全结果。

Result: 在四个常用基准数据集上进行大量实验，结果表明SPIRN在存在缺失模态和噪声标签的多种场景下均优于11种最先进方法。作者称据其所知这是首个在跨模态检索社区中联合处理模态不完整与标签噪声双重挑战的框架。

Conclusion: 以固定语义先验作为一致参考能够有效缓解标签噪声和模态缺失对跨模态检索的不利影响。SPIRN通过语义空间稳定、噪声感知学习、标签校准和选择性补全等机制，实现了更鲁棒的共享表示学习，为复杂真实环境下的不完整跨模态检索提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Cross-modal retrieval (CMR) aims to bridge the semantic gaps between modalities by learning shared representations. Unfortunately, due to the dynamism and inconsistencies in real-world data acquisition, multimodal datasets frequently encounter severe modality incompleteness. Existing incomplete CMR methods adhere to a label-driven paradigm, relying heavily on categorical annotations as the semantic benchmark. However, label noise is difficult to avoid and often coexists with modality incompleteness. To address this challenge, we propose a novel semantic prior-guided framework for incomplete cross-modal retrieval with noisy labels, termed SPIRN. Specifically, SPIRN uses fixed semantic priors as a consistent reference for robust representation learning, noisy-label handling, and missing-modality completion. Based on this reference, Semantic Priors Guidance (SPG) establishes a stable category-level semantic space, Quality-aware Robust Learning (QRL) dynamically evaluates sample quality to distinguish clean samples from noisy ones and handles noise via reverse learning, Prior-guided Global Label Calibration (PGLC) rectifies corrupted annotations for refined supervision, and Confidence-driven Selective Imputation (CSI) recovers missing modalities from a cross-modal memory bank while filtering unreliable imputations with an increasing threshold. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first framework to jointly tackle the dual challenges of modality incompleteness and label noise in the CMR community. Extensive experiments on four widely used benchmark datasets demonstrate that SPIRN outperforms 11 state-of-the-art methods in various scenarios involving both missing modalities and noisy labels.

</details>


### [47] [ReactWorld: Structuring Interaction Responses for Embodied Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06120)
*Hanwei Liu,Huijia Zhang,Hanzhang Wang,Guangyu Liu,Hanbei Zhan,Linjie Hu,Bin Xiao,Xuan Zhou,Dong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReactWorld 是一种面向具身视频世界模型的结构化交互预测引导视频生成方法，通过预测交互响应场并反馈到物体特征与时间注意力，提升机器人动作下物体运动响应与任务意图和接触动力学的一致性；其在 RBench 上平均得分 77.51，较 PhysisForcing 提升 3.29 分，并在 EZS-Bench 上取得 Domain 86.98、平均 81.87。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视频世界模型需要预测机器人动作引起的场景变化，但生成结果中物体的运动响应常常与任务意图和局部接触动力学不一致。关键交互响应通常集中在特定空间区域和特定时间区间，因此需要将交互角色、未来响应强度和局部运动变化显式建模，以增强视频生成过程中的物理与任务一致性。

Method: 论文提出 ReactWorld，在生成骨干的特征空间中引入“交互响应场预测器”，显式描述参与交互的角色、未来运动响应强度和局部运动变化。随后设计“响应反馈适配器”：一方面利用物体分支在输入预测局部动力学与置零局部动力学时的输出差异，构造物体特征增量；另一方面将预测位移转换为时间注意力的位置偏置，从而分别引导物体特征更新和跨帧信息检索，使生成过程更好地遵循交互预测。

Result: 在 RBench 上，ReactWorld 取得 77.51 的平均分，比 PhysisForcing 高 3.29 分，并在全部九个类别中均有提升。在 EZS-Bench 上，其 Domain 得分为 86.98，平均得分为 81.87，表明该方法在具身视频生成和域内/通用评测中均具有性能优势。

Conclusion: 通过将结构化交互预测与生成式反馈机制结合，ReactWorld 能够更准确地建模具身场景中局部、时序关键的物体响应，从而缓解生成视频与任务意图和接触动力学不一致的问题。该结果支持在视频世界模型中显式引入交互响应预测和特征反馈来提升物理一致性与任务相关性。

Abstract: Embodied video world models predict scene changes caused by robot actions, yet generated object responses may remain inconsistent with task intent and contact dynamics. Critical responses in embodied scenes often concentrate in local regions and specific time intervals. Inspired by chain-of-thought reasoning, we introduce ReactWorld, an embodied video generation model guided by structured interaction anticipation. In the generation backbone's feature space, an interaction response field predictor anticipates how the interaction will unfold, explicitly describing participant roles, future motion response intensity, and local motion changes. A response feedback adapter forms increments to object features from the difference between the object branch's outputs with predicted and zeroed local dynamics inputs. It also converts predicted displacements into positional biases for temporal attention. These two mechanisms guide object feature updates and information retrieval across frames, respectively. Through interaction anticipation and generative feedback, ReactWorld achieves an average score of 77.51 on RBench, outperforming PhysisForcing by 3.29 points with improvements in all nine categories. On EZS-Bench, it achieves a Domain score of 86.98 and an average score of 81.87.

</details>


### [48] [Grounding in the Dark: Text-Only Training for Zero-Shot Video Temporal Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06149)
*Divyanshu Mishra,Sabine Sternig,Rakshith Shetty,Erhan Gundogdu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探索仅用文本视频描述训练一个未经视觉-语言预训练的文本LLM，使其能够执行时间定位任务，并在推理阶段接收视频输入；提出的Nearest Entity Replacement（NER）可训练无关地将视频帧映射到文本嵌入空间，部分缩小模态差距，但效果仍受文本与视觉表征不对齐限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Video-LLM依赖大规模配对视频-文本数据进行时间任务训练，而精确时间标注和视频数据收集成本高。作者希望验证能否完全移除训练阶段的配对视频数据，仅通过文本化视频描述让语言模型学习时间结构，并在推理时以视频作为输入，从而迈向无需配对标注的多模态模型训练范式。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含13.4万样本、最长可达100个时间步的文本化视频序列语料，并使用LoRA微调Qwen3-8B。实验中分别基于CLIP和LLM2CLIP的文本嵌入进行适配，在Charades-STA和QV-Highlights上进行评测，且不在任一基准的视频数据上训练。为缓解文本训练与视频推理之间的模态差距，进一步提出无需训练的Nearest Entity Replacement（NER），将每个视频帧表示为其最近文本实体嵌入的归一化均值，使模型在推理时接收到更接近其训练分布的输入。

Result: LLM2CLIP由于支持更长输入，在Temporal-TextVid上的详细文本描述表征优于CLIP，mtIoU为61.54对53.86，并表现出更好的跨模态迁移潜力。然而，若推理时直接用帧嵌入替代文本嵌入，模型性能明显下降，在Charades-STA上仅为4.99 mtIoU，在QV-Highlights上为11.12 mtIoU，说明存在较大模态差距。使用NER后性能提升至Charades-STA的14.45 mtIoU和QV-Highlights的20.72 mtIoU，分别比四组模态差距基线中最强方法高5.00和1.71分。

Conclusion: 仅用文本视频描述训练时间定位模型是可行的，但其视频端迁移仍有限。NER作为一种训练无关的视觉到文本嵌入映射方法，能够在一定程度上桥接模态差距并提升时间定位性能。未来要实现更可靠、更稳定的视频时间定位，可能需要文本与视觉表征更对齐的编码器，或者更强的跨模态表示学习机制。

Abstract: Video-LLMs are typically trained on paired video-text data for temporal tasks, yet collecting precise temporal annotations and training on large-scale video datasets are both costly. We investigate whether a text-only LLM, with no vision-language pretraining, can be trained on textual video descriptions alone to perform temporal grounding and receive video only at inference time. This setting removes paired video data from training altogether and is a step toward training multimodal models without paired annotations. We generate a 134K-example corpus of textual sequences with up to 100 time steps, adapt Qwen3-8B with LoRA on CLIP or LLM2CLIP text embeddings, and evaluate on Charades-STA and QV-Highlights without training on videos from either benchmark. LLM2CLIP, whose text encoder supports longer inputs, represents detailed descriptions better than CLIP (61.54 versus 53.86 mtIoU on Temporal-TextVid) and transfers them better to video. Still, replacing text with frame embeddings at inference yields only 4.99 mtIoU on Charades-STA and 11.12 on QV-Highlights, revealing a large modality gap. To bridge it, we propose Nearest Entity Replacement (NER), a training-free mapping that represents each video frame by the normalized mean of its nearest text-entity embeddings, so that the model receives the kind of input it was trained on. NER reaches 14.45 mtIoU on Charades-STA and 20.72 on QV-Highlights, outperforming the strongest of four families of modality-gap baselines by 5.00 and 1.71 points. NER thus enables partial transfer from text to video, while more reliable grounding will likely require encoders with better-aligned text and visual representations.

</details>


### [49] [DSRS: Teaching MLLMs to Perceive, Reason, and Segment in Complex Driving Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06160)
*Sicen Guo,Zhengfei Song,Kuanhsun Chen,Xu Huang,Ping Zhong,Wei Ye,Shaoyi Du,Rui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向自动驾驶场景的推理分割任务DSRS，构建基准数据集，并提出DSRS-Net，通过注入几何特征增强MLLM的场景感知、空间推理与分割能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理分割框架主要依赖多模态大语言模型的世界知识生成掩码，但缺乏对特定场景尤其是自动驾驶场景的感知与理解，难以处理隐式指令、空间关系、风险检测和交通规则。

Method: 论文定义DSRS任务并建立基准数据集；提出DSRS-Net，在不破坏原有MLLM多模态融合机制的前提下引入几何特征，强化几何、上下文与语言特征融合，以缓解RGB预训练导致的模态表征不匹配问题。

Result: 实验表明DSRS-Net在自动驾驶场景推理分割以及多种公开数据集上的语言驱动分割任务中均取得优越性能。

Conclusion: DSRS任务推动了场景特定推理分割研究，DSRS-Net通过几何特征融合有效提升了自动驾驶复杂场景下的理解与分割能力，代码与数据集将公开发布。

Abstract: Existing reasoning segmentation frameworks typically employ multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with broad world knowledge to extract semantics in a human-like manner and generate corresponding segmentation masks. Nevertheless, these frameworks lack the necessary scene-specific awareness. This paper introduces driving scene reasoning segmentation (DSRS), a scene-specific task that requires models to interpret implicit instructions, infer spatial relations, detect potential risks, handle challenging conditions, and comprehend underlying traffic rules, ultimately generating accurate segmentation masks. Meanwhile, we establish a benchmark dataset to facilitate future research on the DSRS task. To pioneer this task, we propose DSRS-Net, a novel architecture with advanced reasoning segmentation capability for automated driving. Specifically, DSRS-Net seamlessly infuses geometric features for deeper reasoning segmentation, without compromising the original multimodal fusion mechanism. This strategy effectively mitigates the modality representation mismatch in MLLMs caused by RGB-only pretraining, while enhancing the integration of geometric, contextual, and linguistic features. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of DSRS-Net in both driving scene reasoning segmentation and other language-driven segmentation tasks across various public datasets. The source code and dataset will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [50] [Reasoning-Aware Cross-Modal Association Unlearning in Multimodal Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06168)
*Xinghao Li,Chenchen Tan,Youyang Qu,Shujie Cui,Xingliang Yuan,Cunjian Chen,Chi Liu,Longxiang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究多模态大推理模型中的跨模态关联遗忘：目标不是删除知识本身，而是在指定视觉上下文下抑制该知识被访问，同时保留在合法上下文中的可用性与非敏感推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大推理模型可能通过视觉输入与文本/参数知识之间的关联间接泄露敏感信息；现有机器遗忘方法多针对样本、概念或知识内容，难以区分知识与跨模态访问通路，尤其在推理模型中可能出现最终答案不泄露但中间推理仍泄露、或被错误替代知识污染的问题。

Method: 提出Visual-Keyed Counterfactual Rebinding（VKCR）参数高效框架：首先将编辑定位到紧凑的关联条件化参数子空间；其次在任务输出端降低目标信息支持；再通过反事实重绑定，抑制含目标或缺乏支持的推理延续，并鼓励有效且非敏感的替代路径。同时引入CLEAR-R和MLLMU-R评估目标泄露、幻觉重关联、推理保持和模态特定效用。

Result: 实验表明VKCR显著降低图像条件下的目标信息泄露和幻觉性重关联，在异构任务表述下仍有效，并保持非敏感视觉推理及允许的纯文本知识能力。

Conclusion: 跨模态关联遗忘是多模态大推理模型隐私保护的关键问题；VKCR通过参数子空间定位、输出抑制和反事实重绑定实现对视觉触发知识访问的精细编辑，兼顾隐私、推理连贯性与实用效用。

Abstract: Multimodal large reasoning models (MLRMs) integrate visual inputs with textual and parametric knowledge, allowing sensitive information to be accessed through cross-modal associations during reasoning. This raises privacy concerns when a visual input should no longer provide access to such information. Machine unlearning can remove this influence without retraining the model from scratch. However, multimodal unlearning methods mainly target samples, concepts, or knowledge content, without separating the knowledge from the cross-modal association through which it is accessed. In reasoning models, target information may disappear from the final answer while remaining in intermediate reasoning, or be replaced by incorrect substitute knowledge. We therefore study cross-modal association unlearning, where the forget target is a designated visual-to-knowledge association: the model should suppress target-bearing behavior under the designated visual context while preserving access to the same knowledge in permitted contexts. We propose Visual-Keyed Counterfactual Rebinding (VKCR), a parameter-efficient framework for cross-modal association editing. VKCR first localizes the edit to a compact association-conditioned parameter subspace. It then reduces support for the designated target at task outputs and counterfactually rebinds the affected reasoning behavior by discouraging target-bearing or unsupported continuations while favoring valid non-sensitive alternatives. We further introduce CLEAR-R and MLLMU-R to evaluate target leakage, hallucinated reassociation, reasoning preservation, and modality-specific utility. Experiments show that VKCR substantially reduces image-conditioned target leakage and hallucinated reassociation, including across heterogeneous task formulations, while preserving non-sensitive visual reasoning and permitted text-only knowledge.

</details>


### [51] [Rohbau3D-Semantics: Dense Semantic and Instance Labels for Point Clouds of Indoor Shell Construction Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06180)
*Lukas Rauch,Thomas Braml*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Rohbau3D-Semantics数据集，为毛坯施工现场的三维激光扫描点云添加密集语义与实例标注，并配套全景图、立方体贴图等多模态表示、标注协议、质量流程及语义分割基准实验，用于研究复杂建成环境中的三维场景理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内或建筑相关点云数据集常来自重建网格或合成模型，难以反映真实激光扫描中的非均匀采样、测量伪影、反射和精细几何细节；同时施工场景专用、高质量、密集且具备实例级标注的大规模数据集仍不足，限制了三维语义理解、施工现场分析和多模态建成环境研究的发展。

Method: 作者基于此前发布的Rohbau3D毛坯施工域数据，采集504个来自14个勘测现场的点云场景，保留扫描仪中心局部坐标系中的真实激光扫描特性；通过人工逐点标注、专家监督和全场景复核，为17类建筑构件和1类背景添加密集语义标签及对象级实例标签；同时提供脚本，从三维阵列生成与几何对齐的彩色、LiDAR反射强度、深度、表面法线、语义标签和实例标签的全景图与立方体贴图表示；进一步描述采集流程、标注协议、质量控制、全景生成过程，并构建基准划分和语义分割基线实验。

Result: Rohbau3D-Semantics提供了一个高真实感、高密度标注且覆盖多样毛坯施工现场的基准数据集，包含504个三维点云场景、18类语义标签、实例级标注，以及多种与几何对齐的多模态全景和立方体表示；该数据集能够弥合合成室内数据集、网格派生基准和较小规模施工专用资源之间的差距，为三维语义场景理解及相关基线研究提供支撑。

Conclusion: 该数据集通过真实激光扫描数据、严格标注流程、密集语义/实例标签和配套多模态表示，为复杂建成环境特别是毛坯施工现场的三维场景理解、施工分析与多模态研究提供了高质量基准，适合用于训练、评估和比较三维语义分割及相关方法。

Abstract: We present the Rohbau3D-Semantics dataset, which adds dense semantic and instance-level labels to laser scans of the previously released Rohbau3D domain data from shell construction sites. The dataset comprises 504 point cloud scenes from 14 survey sites, each captured with a terrestrial laser scanner and stored in a scanner-centered local coordinate system. Unlike point clouds sampled from reconstructed meshes or synthetic building models, the data preserve the characteristic properties of real laser scanning, including non-uniform sampling, measurement artifacts, reflections, and fine geometric detail. Combined with manual labels, expert oversight during pointwise annotation, and full-scene review, this yields a high-quality benchmark for semantic scene understanding in complex built environments. The dataset includes 17 class labels of building components and one background class, along with instance annotations for object-level analysis. In addition to the 3D point clouds, the release also provides scripts for deriving geometrically aligned panoramic and cube-map representations of color, LiDAR reflection intensity, depth, surface normals, semantic labels, and instance labels from the 3D arrays. We describe the acquisition pipeline, annotation protocol, quality-control process, and panorama-generation procedure, and provide benchmark splits and baseline experiments for semantic segmentation. By combining precise terrestrial laser scans, dense annotations, and a wide variety of shell-construction scenes, Rohbau3D-Semantics bridges the gap among synthetic indoor datasets, mesh-derived benchmarks, and smaller construction-specific resources. The dataset is intended to support research on 3D scene understanding, construction-site analytics, and multimodal scene analysis in the built environment. Access and download the full dataset via this https URL

</details>


### [52] [Annotator-Free Object Detection via Foundation Model Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06195)
*Yasiru Ranasinghe,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要提出一种免目标数据集标注的开放集目标检测框架，利用预训练分割与视觉-语言基础模型、自精炼训练循环和基于地标点的图像聚类机制，自动生成高质量伪标注，并在 COCO、LVIS、ODinW-35 和 Roboflow-100 上取得优于现有开放词表与长尾检测方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督式目标检测依赖大量人工标注且通常受限于封闭类别集合，难以扩展到未知或新类别。现有开放词表检测虽然缓解类别固定问题，但仍常需要昂贵的大语言/多模态模型查询、人工标注或领域适配。该研究旨在实现完全不依赖目标数据集人工标注，同时保持可扩展性、标签一致性和较低计算成本。

Method: 该方法构建模块化流水线：首先利用预训练基础模型自动生成分割候选区域；其次通过视觉-语言多模态推理为候选区域赋予语义标签；随后使用轻量开放集检测器进行自精炼迭代训练，联合改进定位与分类。为减少昂贵 LVLM 查询，引入基于地标点的聚类机制，对图像簇共享或摊销标注，提高标签一致性并降低计算开销。整体流程无需目标数据集人工标注，可适配完全未知的物体类别和域。

Result: 在 COCO、LVIS、ODinW-35 和 Roboflow-100 等基准上的零样本检测评估显示，该方法相比现有先进开放词表和长尾检测方法取得显著性能提升，其中 COCO 达到 60.8 AP，LVIS 达到 47.4 AP。

Conclusion: 该框架将基础模型的感知与推理能力转化为自动伪标注和自精炼学习流程，实现了无目标数据集标注的开放集检测，为未知环境和不可标注场景中的可扩展真实世界检测系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Object detection research remains constrained by high annotation costs and the closed-set assumptions of supervised models, limiting scalability to novel categories. We present a target-dataset annotation-free object detection framework that eliminates the need for human labeling on the target dataset by leveraging the complementary strengths of pretrained foundation models, including segmentation and vision-language systems, combined with a self-refining training loop. To further reduce reliance on expensive LVLM queries, we introduce a landmark-based clustering mechanism that amortizes labeling across image clusters, improving label consistency while lowering computational cost. Our method autonomously generates segmentation proposals, assigns semantic labels through multimodal reasoning, and iteratively improves both localization and classification using a lightweight open-set detector. This modular pipeline enables adaptation to completely unseen object classes and domains without any human-provided target-dataset annotations, while maintaining compatibility with existing detection architectures. Evaluations on COCO, LVIS, ODinW-35, and Roboflow-100 demonstrate substantial performance gains over state-of-the-art open-vocabulary and long-tail detection methods, achieving 60.8 AP on COCO and 47.4 AP on LVIS under zero-shot settings. Beyond strong benchmark results, our approach provides a scalable foundation for real-world detection systems in unknown environments, where annotation is infeasible.

</details>


### [53] [BiCG-Net: Target-Specific Image Quality Assessment via Observation Modeling and In-Image Differencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06203)
*Yibu Yang,Tingqiong Cui,Liming Lei,Hao Xiong,Jun Xu,Siyuan Liu,Guanghui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BiCG-Net，针对图像中特定目标区域（如列车车身）的质量估计问题，通过目标框向外扩展的嵌套采样和11个教师模型集成蒸馏，构建一个紧凑学生模型，以抑制背景污染并实现目标级质量评分与跨数据集迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无参考图像质量评估（NR-IQA）通常对整幅图像打分，但当目标区域在画面中占比较小时，整图分数容易被背景主导，无法准确反映目标区域质量；同时公开基准也缺乏目标级人工MOS标注。因此需要一种面向局部目标区域、能够降低背景干扰并生成目标质量分数的方法。

Method: 作者以目标框为起点构造向外扩展的嵌套采样补丁序列，利用该序列的相对结构推断目标质量。具体提出BiCG-Net学生网络，通过线性观测模型对11个教师模型的输出进行蒸馏，并设计图像内差分机制，在理论上消除目标项和教师偏置的影响。学生模型在每个数据集上独立训练与测试，并进一步评估零样本跨数据集迁移能力。

Result: 在CSIQ和LIVEC上，BiCG-Net的学生模型分别与人工MOS达到0.9009和0.9133的PLCC，优于最佳单个教师和教师共识结果。在自建无MOS铁路数据集上，学生模型相对弱标签达到0.9855的PLCC。残差分析表明，预测残差与背景污染、教师分散度和标签极端性显著相关，说明学生模型提取了教师共识中未包含的信息。在CSIQ到LIVEC的零样本迁移中，冻结学生模型达到0.8416的PLCC，较共识高0.1947。

Conclusion: BiCG-Net可作为离线目标级质量审计和标签生成引擎，其仅39,482个参数，紧凑且能作为统一层将异质教师读数映射到一致的质量尺度，证明基于相对结构的蒸馏优于简单模仿教师输出。

Abstract: We estimate the quality of a specific target region (e.g., a train body) in an image, rather than the whole image. This differs fundamentally from whole-image No-Reference Image Quality Assessment (NR-IQA): when the target occupies a small fraction of the frame, the whole-image score is dominated by background, and no public benchmark provides target-level human Mean Opinion Score (MOS). We estimate target quality via nested sampling - a sequence of patches expanding outward from the target box - and infer quality from the relative structure of this sequence. This motivates BiCG-Net, a student network that distills an 11-teacher ensemble through a linear observation model and an in-image differencing mechanism, which provably cancels the target term and teacher-specific bias. We train and test the student independently on each dataset, and evaluate zero-shot transfer across benchmarks. On CSIQ and LIVEC, the student reaches PLCCs of 0.9009 and 0.9133 against human MOS, exceeding the best single teacher and the teacher consensus. On a self-built MOS-free railway dataset, the student reaches a PLCC of 0.9855 against a weak label. The student exceeds its teachers because it learns a new mapping from the relative structure of teacher readings to the ground-truth label, rather than mimicking them. Residual analysis on CSIQ and LIVEC shows the prediction residual is significantly correlated with background contamination, teacher dispersion, and label extremity, demonstrating the student extracts information the consensus does not contain. Under CSIQ-to-LIVEC transfer, the frozen student reaches a PLCC of 0.8416, exceeding the consensus by 0.1947. We position BiCG-Net as an offline target-level quality auditing and label-generation engine; its 39,482-parameter student is compact by design, acting as a unifying layer mapping heterogeneous teacher readings onto a common quality scale.

</details>


### [54] [CrossPressor: Scalable Cross-View Fusion for Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06258)
*Yuxi Hu,Wenhao Dong,Jun Zhang,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossPressor 是一种免训练的锚点融合方法，用于缓解前馈式 3D Gaussian Splatting 在长多视角序列中因固定锚点导致的表示—融合失配问题，从而提升可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式 3D-GS 扩展到长多视角序列时面临表示规模增长问题。基于锚点的压缩方法如 ZPressor 仅从少量紧凑锚点特征图解码 Gaussian，但固定锚点会导致每个锚点必须汇聚越来越多的支持视角，造成支持视图增长与表示容量不匹配，即 representation–fusion mismatch。

Method: CrossPressor 保留所有面向解码器的锚点查询，不改变锚点表示；同时通过 coverage-cycled evidence routing 在 K/V 投影前对不断增长的支持视角进行证据路由，并将保留的空间证据分布到支持视图和融合层中，确保所有支持视图都被表示。该方法还采用联合上下文打包，在不重新训练的情况下降低支持侧融合开销，实现可扩展的锚点融合。

Result: CrossPressor 在重建质量上接近 dense fusion，同时在长输入序列上实现最高 26.12 倍的跨视图融合加速，以及最高 1.43 倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: CrossPressor 证明在固定锚点、不解码额外表示的前提下，通过支持侧证据路由与上下文打包即可缓解长多视角序列中的融合瓶颈，为前馈式 3D-GS 的可扩展推理提供了免训练优化方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS) becomes increasingly difficult to scale to long multi-view sequences. Anchor-based compression methods such as ZPressor alleviate representation growth by decoding Gaussians only from a compact set of anchor feature maps, but keeping these anchors fixed forces each one to aggregate an increasing number of support views. We identify this representation--fusion mismatch and introduce \textbf{CrossPressor}, a training-free method for scalable anchor-based fusion. CrossPressor preserves all decoder-facing anchor queries while constraining the view-growing support sequence through coverage-cycled evidence routing, distributing retained spatial evidence across support views and fusion layers while keeping all support views represented. Routing before K/V projection, together with joint context packing, further reduces support-side fusion cost without retraining. CrossPressor maintains reconstruction quality close to dense fusion while achieving up to $26.12\times$ faster cross-view fusion and $1.43\times$ end-to-end acceleration on long input sequences. Our project page is at: this https URL.

</details>


### [55] [NextGAN: Towards Simple and Scalable GAN Training for Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06270)
*Xinhao Lou,Dongye Xie,Peng Sun,Chenyi Zhuang,Kaicheng Yu,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NEXTGAN 提出一种直接约束 critic 梯度的训练目标，将预训练图像/视频生成模型高效适配为单步或少步生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对抗损失主要监督 critic 的输出值，但没有直接规定用于更新生成器的梯度方向，难以把大规模预训练生成模型稳定、简洁地适配到一步或少步采样。

Method: NEXTGAN 在冻结表示空间的插值位置构造参考特征与生成特征的独立配对位移，并回归标量 critic 的输入梯度以拟合该位移。该目标在固定生成器下等价于带梯度能量惩罚的对抗目标，生成器仍采用标准基于势函数的 GAN 更新。方法不需要辅助损失、在线教师评分或生成轨迹，可适配参数量达 14B 的预训练图像与视频生成器。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256×256 上，NEXTGAN 的一 step FID 分别为像素空间 JiT-B 的 2.87 和潜空间 DiT-XL/1 的 1.54；其对抗形式进一步提升到 1.36。在文本生成视频中，单一训练得到的 Wan2.1-1.3B 模型支持一、二、四步采样，并在一步和两步条件下取得对比 1.3B 方法中最高的 VBench 总分；两步为 83.13，较 rCM 高 1.25，较 AnyFlow 高 0.47。

Conclusion: NEXTGAN 通过梯度回归建立 critic 与生成器更新方向之间的显式联系，为大规模预训练生成模型提供了简洁、可扩展的单步/少步图像与视频适配方案。

Abstract: Adversarial training enables one- and few-step image and video generation, but conventional adversarial losses supervise critic values without directly specifying the gradients used to update the generator. We present NEXTGAN, which trains a scalar critic by regressing its input gradient onto displacements between independently paired reference and generated features at interpolated locations in frozen representation spaces. For a fixed generator, this regression is equivalent to an adversarial objective with a gradient-energy penalty, while the generator retains the standard potential-based GAN update. The same objective adapts pre-trained image and video generators with up to 14B parameters, without auxiliary losses, online teacher scores, or trajectories. On ImageNet $256 \times 256$, NEXTGAN achieves one-step FIDs of 2.87 with pixel-space JiT-B and 1.54 with latent-space DiT-XL/1; its adversarial form further reaches 1.36. For text-to-video generation, a single trained Wan2.1-1.3B model supports one-, two-, and four-step sampling, achieving the highest VBench total scores among the compared 1.3B methods at one and two steps. At two steps, NEXTGAN scores 83.13, exceeding rCM and AnyFlow by 1.25 and 0.47 points, respectively. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [56] [Quo Vadis, Driving Scene Reconstruction? Occlusion-Aware Gaussian Densification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06273)
*Dung Do Tien,Kien Nguyen Do Trung,Kiet Dang Vu,Trung Thai Tran,Duc Dung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OccSplat：一种面向自动驾驶环视场景的前馈式 3D Gaussian Splatting 框架，通过“遮挡感知的高斯加密”显式补全被遮挡区域缺失的 3D 高斯几何，从而改善新颖视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式 3DGS 方法主要依赖单次输入观测，难以处理被遮挡区域：当新视角需要可见的几何内容在输入图中被遮挡时，重建的高斯会缺少约束，导致不完整和渲染失真。仅对渲染图像做图像空间修复不能从根本上补全缺失的三维几何。

Method: OccSplat 核心包含两个模块：1）Gaussian Occlusion Mapping：识别当前高斯表示中遮挡导致缺乏支撑的区域；2）Gaussian Occlusion Completion：针对这些区域直接预测新的 3D Gaussian primitives，并将其整合回原始高斯场景表示。该方法在三维表示层面补全场景几何，同时保持前馈推理，无需逐场景优化。

Result: 在 nuScenes 基准上的实验表明，OccSplat 相比对比方法取得更好性能，尤其是在遮挡区域带来显著提升，使驾驶场景的新颖视角合成更准确、视觉上更一致。

Conclusion: 将遮挡处理从图像空间修复推进到三维高斯几何补全，是提升单观测前馈 3DGS 在复杂自动驾驶场景中鲁棒性和完整性的有效路径；OccSplat 通过遮挡感知高斯加密实现了更高效、更完整的新颖视角重建。

Abstract: While recent feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting methods enable efficient driving-scene reconstruction from surround-view images, their reliance on a single observation fundamentally hinders novel-view synthesis. Regions occluded in the input can become visible from novel viewpoints, leaving the reconstructed Gaussians under-constrained and incomplete. Moving beyond image-space inpainting for rendered views, we introduce OccSplat, a feed-forward framework whose core contribution is Occlusion-Aware Gaussian Densification, explicitly targeting missing scene geometry rather than missing pixels. By predicting new Gaussian primitives for unsupported regions, OccSplat completes the scene directly in the 3D representation. Specifically, OccSplat first identifies occluded regions that lack Gaussian support through Gaussian Occlusion Mapping. Gaussian Occlusion Completion then generates new primitives for these regions. The predicted primitives are integrated into the original representation to improve novel-view synthesis while retaining feed-forward inference without per-scene optimization. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark demonstrate that OccSplat improves on the compared methods and yields particularly large gains in occluded regions, leading to more accurate and visually coherent driving-scene reconstruction.

</details>


### [57] [MonoLaDN: Language-Guided DeNoising for Monocular 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06282)
*Kiet Dang Vu,Cao Le Minh Khoa,Nguyen Viet Tri Pham,An Nguyen Gia,Tam Le Thuc Ho,Duc Dung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MonoLaDN 利用视觉语言模型在训练阶段生成物体遮挡、深度、尺寸和角度等几何推理，并通过带噪推理重构任务将先验知识蒸馏到纯视觉的单目3D检测器中，推理时无需保留语言模型、不增加延迟和参数，且在 KITTI 3D 与 nuScenes 上优于先进单目3D检测方法并具备良好跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目3D物体检测需要从单一图像推断深度，本质上是病态问题。现有基于Transformer的检测器主要依赖图像特征和3D框标注学习几何关系，缺少视觉语言模型在大尺度预训练中获得的语义与几何先验。然而直接部署VLM进行3D检测会带来过高推理成本，因此需要在保持检测器纯视觉和高效推理的前提下，将VLM中的几何先验有效迁移给单目3D检测器。

Method: MonoLaDN提出语言引导的去噪蒸馏框架。训练时冻结一个视觉语言模型，根据图像和真实3D框生成关于物体遮挡、深度、尺寸和角度的结构化几何推理。随后将该推理编码为查询向量，并施加噪声扰动，训练单目3D检测器从被污染的特征表示中重建干净推理。通过重构损坏的VLM推理，检测器基于深度特征的表示被强迫内化VLM的几何先验。推理阶段丢弃语言模型和推理路径，仅部署纯视觉检测器，因此不增加推理延迟和模型参数。

Result: 在 KITTI 3D 和 nuScenes 数据集上的实验表明，MonoLaDN 一致优于最先进的单目3D物体检测器，并在新领域中表现出较好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将视觉语言模型的几何推理知识通过去噪重构任务蒸馏到单目3D检测器中，可以在不牺牲部署效率的情况下增强纯视觉模型的3D感知能力。该方法为解决单目3D检测中的深度歧义问题提供了一种利用语义先验且无需在线调用VLM的有效途径。

Abstract: Monocular 3D object detection offers a low-cost route to 3D perception, but it remains fundamentally ill-posed, since depth must be inferred from a single view. Recent transformer-based detectors learn geometry purely from image features and 3D box annotations, lacking the high-level semantic priors that vision-language models (VLMs) acquire from large-scale pretraining. VLMs offer such priors, but deploying one directly as a detector is impractical due to its heavy inference cost. We propose MonoLaDN, a language-guided denoising framework that transfers the geometric reasoning of a VLM into a monocular 3D detector while keeping the deployed model purely vision-based. During training, a frozen VLM produces structured reasoning about each object's occlusion, depth, dimension, and angle from the image and its ground-truth boxes. We encode this reasoning into queries, perturb them with noise, and train the detector to reconstruct the clean reasoning. Reconstructing the corrupted reasoning forces the detector's depth-guided features to internalize the VLM's geometric priors, so this knowledge remains available even after the reasoning path is discarded at inference, adding no latency or parameters to the deployed model. Experiments on KITTI 3D and nuScenes show that MonoLaDN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art detectors and generalizes well to new domains.

</details>


### [58] [Beyond Scene Shortcuts: Action-Centric Video-Text Alignment for Fine-Grained Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06302)
*Zhiliang Zhu,Jiazheng Huang,Dawei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对细粒度动作视频中“环境替代动作”的幻觉问题，提出 STARR 框架与 FineActionR&R 数据，通过动作-环境解耦和注意力一致性奖励强化以动作为中心的视频文本对齐，提升动作理解与描述的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度动作理解要求模型基于时空证据回答“做了什么、如何做、为何做”，但更强监督可能诱导模型依赖静态场景和显著物体而非真实运动，产生环境引导的动作幻觉（EGAH），因此需要显式打破场景与动作之间的捷径关联。

Method: 提出动作中心的视频文本对齐框架 STARR，包括 Action-Environment Dissociation 以削弱场景捷径，以及在 Group Relative Policy Optimization 中引入 Attention Consistency Reward 来强化动作聚焦的 grounding。同时构建 FineActionR&R，融合时间局部化字幕、层级化推理和时序因果推理数据，并基于 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 训练得到 FineActionVLM-R1。

Result: FineActionVLM-R1 在 MotionBench 上的平均准确率较基座模型提升 3.01 个百分点，在 HAU-MCQ-Bench 上达到 91.50%；定性比较显示细粒度动作描述更忠实于视频证据，由环境引发的幻觉减少。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦环境与动作、优化注意力分配并引入高质量时序推理监督，可以有效缓解细粒度动作理解中的环境引导动作幻觉，提升视频语言模型对动作内容、方式和因果关系的理解能力。

Abstract: Fine-grained action understanding requires vision-language models to ground what an agent does, how it acts, and why it acts in spatio-temporal evidence. However, richer supervision can backfire: fine-tuned models may generate detailed action descriptions grounded in static scenes and salient objects rather than motion, a failure we call Environment-Grounded Action Hallucination (EGAH). To mitigate EGAH, we propose Staged Attention Rectification and Reinforcement (STARR), an action-centric video-text alignment framework that breaks scene-action shortcuts through Action-Environment Dissociation and reinforces action-focused grounding with an Attention Consistency Reward within Group Relative Policy Optimization. We further construct FineActionR&R, combining temporally localized captions with hierarchical and temporal-causal reasoning data. Built on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, FineActionVLM-R1 improves average MotionBench accuracy by 3.01 percentage points over its base model and achieves 91.50% on HAU-MCQ-Bench. Qualitative comparisons further indicate more faithful fine-grained action descriptions with fewer environment-induced hallucinations.

</details>


### [59] [Prism: Bidirectional Self-Teaching for Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06343)
*Peng Zhang,Qian Chen,Xianyin Zhang,Yimin Tian,Ruite Fei,Dongyi Lv,Lifan Guo,Chi Zhang,Shuai Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Prism，一种面向统一多模态模型（UMMs）的双向自监督后训练方法：从模型自生成的交错轨迹中解耦文本链与图像链，并依据各链可靠性构造训练目标，从而同时提升视觉理解与视觉生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型后训练方法通常依赖外部理解模型作为固定教师，或依赖真实标注轨迹同时监督理解与生成，缺乏让模型从自身生成的交错轨迹中挖掘双向监督信号、实现理解与生成协同提升的机制。

Method: Prism 的核心是从自生成的 interleaved rollouts 中解耦出文本链和图像链，分别评估其可靠性，并基于可靠性状态构造对应训练目标，实现“双向自教学”后训练。该方法不依赖外部 verifier，也不依赖外部标注的纠正轨迹。

Result: 在 SenseNova-U1 上，生成指标 GenEval 从 85.8 提升到 89.2（+3.4），DPG 从 86.1 提升到 87.3（+1.2）；理解指标 MME 总分从 2282.6 提升到 2311.6，MMVP 从 58.0 提升到 62.0。在 BAGEL 上也观察到一致提升。

Conclusion: Prism 证明，通过可靠性引导的自训练目标构建，统一多模态模型可以从自身生成的交错轨迹中同时改进视觉理解与视觉生成，实现二者协同提升，而无需额外外部监督或人工纠正轨迹。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) aim to support visual understanding and generation within a single model. Existing post-training methods either use understanding as a fixed teacher to improve generation, or rely on ground-truth trajectories to supervise both understanding and generation. Prism instead derives bidirectional supervision from self-generated interleaved rollouts, constructing training targets according to the reliability of their text and image chains. We propose Prism, a bidirectional self-teaching post-training method for UMMs. Like a prism decomposing mixed light, Prism decouples a coupled interleaved trajectory into a text chain and an image chain, assesses the reliability of each chain, and constructs a corresponding training target from their reliability states. Experiments on both SenseNova-U1 and BAGEL show that Prism improves both understanding and generation. On the generation side, GenEval improves from 85.8 to 89.2 (+3.4), and DPG improves from 86.1 to 87.3 (+1.2) on SenseNova-U1. On the understanding side, the total MME score improves from 2282.6 to 2311.6, and MMVP improves from 58.0 to 62.0. Consistent improvements are observed on BAGEL. These results highlight that Prism enables coordinated improvements in generation and understanding by constructing reliability-guided self-training targets from self-generated trajectories, without relying on an external verifier or externally annotated corrected trajectories during target construction.

</details>


### [60] [Traffic-VL: Reinforced Vision-Language Models for Generalizable End-to-End Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06353)
*Xingchen Zou,Maonan Wang,Kemou Jiang,Jie Li,Yiqi Chen,Xi Chen,Hai Yang,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SynTra-V 与 Traffic-VL，以合成照片级多视角监控图像和视觉语言模型直接完成交通信号控制，避免依赖中间结构化交通状态，并在仿真、真实场景与路口部署中展现稳健泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制方法通常依赖专用感知管线输出的结构化交通状态，而已有视觉方法多使用理想化仿真图像并先转换为中间表征，难以直接利用真实路侧视觉观测，导致视觉域差距和感知到决策差距较大。

Method: 作者构建 SynTra-V，将基于物理的交通仿真转换为覆盖多种道路拓扑和感知条件的照片级多视角监控图像，并通过自动语义审计校验几何结构、车辆运动和车道语义是否与仿真状态一致。在此基础上提出 Traffic-VL，一个 80 亿参数的视觉语言模型，直接以同步多视角图像和自然语言相位描述作为输入输出信号控制决策。训练沿 Traffic-R1 的两阶段强化推理框架：先在 SynTra-V 上通过离线强化学习调整视觉编码器，再在闭环仿真中冻结编码器并进行在线视觉步进轨迹策略优化。

Result: 实验表明 Traffic-VL 在控制性能上可匹配使用结构化状态的 Traffic-R1，并优于基于图像的控制器以及包括 Gemini 3 Pro 在内的通用视觉语言模型。在未见过道路拓扑、退化感知条件、真实监控视频以及十个路口为期三周的部署中，Traffic-VL 表现出较强的视觉泛化能力和有效的在线约束控制能力。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，通过高质量照片级多视角视觉数据生成、自动语义校验和分阶段强化学习训练，视觉语言模型可以直接从路侧监控图像进行交通信号控制，从而缩小感知到决策的差距，并为复杂交通环境中的鲁棒部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: Existing traffic signal control methods typically rely on structured traffic states produced by dedicated perception pipelines. Emerging visual approaches often use idealized simulator images and convert them into intermediate representations before control, limiting their ability to directly exploit roadside visual observations. To bridge both the visual-domain and perception-to-decision gaps, we introduce SynTra-V and Traffic-VL. SynTra-V converts physics-based traffic simulations into photorealistic multi-view surveillance imagery across diverse road topologies and sensing conditions, and automatic semantic auditing checks that control-critical geometry, vehicle movements, and lane semantics match the simulator state. Traffic-VL is an eight-billion-parameter vision-language model that directly maps synchronized multi-view images and natural-language phase descriptions to signal decisions without intermediate structured states. Building on Traffic-R1's two-stage reinforced reasoning framework, Traffic-VL adapts its vision encoder via offline reinforcement learning on SynTra-V and freezes it during online visual stepwise trajectory policy optimization in closed-loop simulation. Experiments show that Traffic-VL matches structured-state Traffic-R1 while outperforming image-based controllers and general-purpose vision-language models, including Gemini 3 Pro. Evaluations on unseen topologies, degraded sensing conditions, real surveillance footage, and a three-week deployment across ten intersections further demonstrate robust visual generalization and effective constrained online control.

</details>


### [61] [FlashRelight: Portrait Video Relighting with Dynamic Lighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06355)
*Jiahui Sheng,Le Li,Limin Lin,Jiahao Li,Wansen Feng,Hong Gu,Shuhan Chen,Xiaorun Li,Rui Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FlashRelight，一种用于动态人像视频重打光的两阶段框架，通过先去除源光照、再合成目标动态光照，实现时间稳定且真实感强的光照编辑，并支持环境贴图、文本描述和参考视频等多种光照控制方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人像视频重打光需要在改变光照的同时保持照片级真实感和时间一致性。当光照方向、强度和颜色随时间动态变化时，任务更具挑战，因为模型不仅要估计并分离光照，还要在视频帧间稳定地合成目标光照，避免出现闪烁、抖动或光照不一致等问题。

Method: FlashRelight 采用两阶段流程：首先通过去光照（delighting）抑制原始人像视频中的源光照，得到光照相对中性的中间表示；随后在该中间表示上合成目标动态光照。框架支持三种互补控制方式：环境贴图序列用于逐帧显式控制光照，动态光照文本用于语义化光照编辑，光照参考视频用于跨视频视觉光照迁移。训练采用低成本混合策略，将静态 one-light-at-a-time（OLAT）数据与生成的人像动态光照视频相结合，以同时覆盖受控光照变化和真实人像运动。

Result: 大量实验表明，FlashRelight 能够实现时间连贯且照片级真实感的动态人像视频重打光，并支持灵活的光照参考迁移和多种光照控制输入。

Conclusion: FlashRelight 通过“先去光照、再重打光”的两阶段设计以及多模态光照控制和混合训练策略，有效应对动态光照条件下的人像视频重打光难题，在真实感、时间稳定性和可控性方面取得良好效果，为视频级光照编辑提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Portrait video relighting aims to modify the illumination of a portrait video while preserving photorealism and temporal stability. The task becomes particularly challenging under dynamic lighting, where illumination direction, intensity, and color vary over time. To tackle this problem, we introduce FlashRelight, a novel two-stage framework for dynamic portrait video relighting. FlashRelight first suppresses the source illumination through delighting, and then synthesizes the target dynamic lighting on an illumination-neutral portrait video. FlashRelight supports three complementary control modalities: envmap sequences for explicit frame-level lighting control, dynamic lighting text for semantic illumination editing, and lighting reference videos for visual lighting transfer. Training follows a low-cost hybrid strategy that combines static one-light-at-a-time (OLAT) data with generated dynamic-lighting portrait videos, covering both controlled illumination variation and realistic portrait motion. Extensive experiments show that FlashRelight achieves temporally coherent and photorealistic dynamic relighting while enabling flexible cross-video lighting transfer. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [62] [NanoKV: High-Ratio KV Cache Compression for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06377)
*Jiacheng Lu,Haoyi Zhu,Sipei Yi,Enze Xie,Cheng Zhuo,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 NanoKV，一种针对自回归视频模型的高压缩比 KV Cache 压缩框架，在保持视频生成质量的同时显著降低历史 KV 缓存的存储开销；其变体 NanoKV-Fast 还能进一步减少注意力计算并带来端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频模型依赖历史键值缓存来维持长时序场景一致性，但 KV 缓存的存储和对历史内容的重复注意力计算已成为内存与计算瓶颈。过度压缩会损害生成质量，而仅恢复完整维度 KV 又难以降低注意力计算成本，因此需要在压缩率、生成质量和计算效率之间取得更好的权衡。

Method: NanoKV 在不修改视频生成主干网络的条件下，对 KV 缓存进行面向生成目标的高比例压缩。该方法利用 token 与 attention head 之间的冗余信息，将共享内容存储为 cluster center，并将 head-channel 联合残差编码为低位宽系数。针对 Key 和 Value 在注意力中的不同作用，采用不同优化目标：Key 解码器以保留下游模块输出为目标进行拟合，Value 编解码器则在实际部署量化路径下追求忠实重建。NanoKV-Fast 进一步复用压缩系数构造 mixed-dimensional attention，在不改变视频主干的情况下降低注意力计算量。

Result: 在三种自回归视频模型上，NanoKV 均改善了生成质量与缓存存储之间的权衡。在 HY-WorldPlay 上，NanoKV 实现 26.09 倍的有效 KV 压缩，约为 KVTC 方法的两倍，同时 PSNR 提升 1.0 dB。NanoKV-Fast 仍保持 20.53 倍有效压缩，并相比 KVTC 取得约 1.15 倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: NanoKV 表明，通过利用 KV 缓存中的结构冗余，并区分 Key 与 Value 在注意力计算和生成质量中的作用进行针对性压缩，可以在不重训或修改视频主干的前提下实现更高压缩比与更好生成质量。其可选的高效注意力变体还能进一步缓解计算瓶颈，为长时序自回归视频生成中的 KV 缓存管理提供了更优的质量-存储-速度平衡。

Abstract: Autoregressive video models rely on historical key-value (KV) caches to preserve scene consistency over extended rollouts, making KV storage and repeated attention over history a growing memory and computational bottleneck. Aggressive compression can degrade generation quality, while reconstructing full-dimensional KV leaves attention computation largely unchanged. We introduce NanoKV, a frozen-backbone framework for generation-aware high-ratio KV compression. NanoKV exploits redundancy across tokens and attention heads by storing shared content in cluster centers and encoding joint head-channel residuals into low-bit coefficients. Crucially, NanoKV uses different fitting objectives for Keys and Values according to their distinct roles in attention: Key decoders are optimized to preserve downstream block outputs, whereas Value encoders and decoders are fitted for faithful reconstruction through the deployed quantization path. Building on the same compressed representation, the optional NanoKV-Fast variant reuses the stored coefficients for mixed-dimensional attention, reducing computation without modifying the video backbone. Across three autoregressive video backbones, NanoKV consistently improves the quality-storage trade-off. On HY-WorldPlay, NanoKV achieves $26.09\times$ effective KV compression, nearly twice that of KVTC, while improving PSNR by 1.0 dB. NanoKV-Fast retains $20.53\times$ effective compression and achieves an approximately $1.15\times$ end-to-end speedup over KVTC.

</details>


### [63] [A Bag of Tricks for Defending Deep Learning-based Face Recognition Pipelines against Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06412)
*Quentin Le Roux,Marti Jimenez,Yannick Teglia,Philippe Loubet Moundi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 面向训练数据可能来自第三方且存在投毒/后门风险的成熟人脸识别系统，提出三种可在工业部署中实际使用的防御方法：训练期检测被污染的个体身份，推理期过滤可疑输入，并评估其动机、算法、计算开销和在真实人脸识别工作负载上的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中，人脸识别模型虽在内部训练，但训练数据可能来自不受信任或已泄露的第三方，因此容易受到基于数据投毒的后门攻击。需要为系统提供方提供可落地、可部署、可评估计算成本的加固手段。

Method: 提出三种实用防御“技巧”：第一种在训练阶段运行，用于在训练过程中或系统上线前检测被污染的身份证据/训练样本；第二和第三种在推理阶段运行，在将识别结果返回给潜在攻击者之前过滤可疑输入。每种方法均给出动机、算法流程和计算影响，并在现实人脸识别工作负载上进行评估。

Result: 报告评估了这些防御方法在真实人脸识别场景中的可行性和实用性，表明其能够为系统提供方提供可操作的加固技术；摘要未给出具体数值指标。

Conclusion: 针对数据投毒导致的多阶段人脸识别后门威胁，训练期身份检测与推理期输入过滤相结合，可在不依赖模型训练完全受控的前提下，提高工业人脸识别系统的可部署安全性。

Abstract: The protection of mature, multi-stage Face Recognition Systems is crucial to system providers. This technical report targets the threat model most relevant to industry practitioners: although model training is performed in-house, training data is sourced from possibly-compromised third parties. Given this setting, we present three practical defense "tricks" against data poisoning-based backdoor attacks. The first operates at training-time, detecting poisoned identities during training or prior to a pipeline's deployment. The last two operate at inference-time, filtering suspicious inputs before an answer can be sent back to a potential attacker. Each method is presented with their motivation, algorithms and computational impact, and evaluated on realistic face recognition workloads. Our goal is to provide actionable hardening techniques to system providers.

</details>


### [64] [Entropogram-guided Visual Realignment Decoding: Capturing the Rhythm of Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06415)
*Jiaqi Deng,Zhe Fu,Yakun Chen,Dichang Zhang,Zonghan Wu,Xiaoshui Huang,Huan Huo,Guandong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种熵感知的自反思框架，通过持续监测解码过程中的熵动态来检测多模态大语言模型推理中的幻觉风险，并在可疑阶段进行视觉重新对齐，从而提升长链推理的准确性和抗幻觉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在跨模态推理任务上表现优异，但在长链推理过程中仍容易出现幻觉。传统上熵主要被视为静态不确定性指标，难以刻画模型生成过程中模态信息融合与推理稳定性的动态变化。作者希望从生成动力学角度重新理解幻觉成因，并利用熵作为结构化信号定位和缓解幻觉。

Method: 作者首先系统分析解码过程中的熵动态，识别出两类与幻觉相关的熵模式：一是整体高置信度状态，反映模型过早收敛到不恰当的多模态交互；二是局部突然的熵跳变，反映推理不稳定和幻觉开始发生。基于这些发现，提出熵感知自反思框架，在推理时持续跟踪熵动态，当检测到可疑幻觉状态时触发针对性的视觉重新对齐，促使模型重新审视关键视觉证据。

Result: 在覆盖六个多模态推理基准、九种评测设置的实验中，该方法在推理准确性和幻觉鲁棒性方面均取得稳健提升，为理解多模态大语言模型失败背后的生成动态提供了新视角。

Conclusion: 熵不仅是静态不确定性度量，而是能够反映多模态信息整合过程的结构化生成信号。通过监控解码期熵动态并在异常阶段引入视觉重新对齐，可以在不依赖外部监督的情况下有效降低多模态长链推理中的幻觉风险。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable progress on cross-modal reasoning tasks, yet they continue to suffer from hallucinations in long-chain reasoning processes. In this work, we revisit hallucination in MLLMs from the perspective of reasoning generation dynamics. We argue that entropy is not merely a static measure of uncertainty but a structured signal that reflects how modality information is integrated throughout decoding. Through systematic analysis, we identify two characteristic entropy patterns associated with hallucination behaviors: (1) a generally high-confidence regime corresponds to premature convergence to inappropriate multimodal interactions; and (2) local abrupt entropy transitions are associated with reasoning instability and hallucination onset. Building on these observations, we propose an entropy-aware self-introspective framework for MLLM reasoning. Our method continuously tracks entropy dynamics during decoding and performs targeted visual re-alignment when suspicious hallucination states are detected, encouraging the model to re-examine salient visual evidence at critical reasoning stages. Extensive experiments across six multimodal reasoning benchmarks spanning nine distinct evaluation settings demonstrate that our approach delivers robust improvements in reasoning accuracy and hallucination robustness, while providing new insights into the generation dynamics underlying MLLM failures. The code is available at: this https URL.

</details>


### [65] [Unaligned Image Guided Denoising via Cross-modal Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06422)
*Runmin Zhang,Linshan Li,Si-Yuan Cao,Zhu Yu,Lingyu Zhu,Huaqi Zhang,Hui-Liang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UDG-FM：面向未对齐跨模态引导去噪的渐进式条件流匹配框架，实现少步高效图像修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像引导去噪方法通常假设引导图像与目标空间对齐，或仅处理小基线校正后的配对，难以应对严重目标噪声与大范围二维跨模态错位并存的复杂情况。

Method: 将引导去噪建模为渐进式流匹配过程，使图像恢复与对齐引导聚合相互增强；采用聚焦关键去噪阶段并保持训练—推理一致性的少步推理训练策略；提出自适应稀疏引导传播，并用感知损失和几何辅助损失约束大范围匹配。

Result: 实验表明UDG-FM在仅一步或两步推理下取得领先去噪性能，并兼容现代单图像修复主干网络。

Conclusion: UDG-FM通过跨模态条件流匹配、渐进式对齐聚合与高效少步推理策略，为严重噪声与大范围未对齐引导的去噪任务提供了统一且实用的解决方案。
