Abstract: This work presents UGD-FM, a novel unaligned image guided denoising framework based on cross-modal conditional flow matching. Unlike previous approaches that assume spatially aligned inputs or only handle small-baseline rectified pairs, we address a more challenging setting where severe target noise and large-range two-dimensional cross-modal misalignment coexist. Specifically, we formulate guided denoising as a progressive flow matching process, allowing image restoration and aligned guidance aggregation to mutually reinforce each other. To support efficient few-step inference, we introduce a stage-focused and inference-consistent training strategy that focuses on task-critical denoising stages, and mitigates the training-inference mismatch. We further propose adaptive sparse guidance propagation for efficient and reliable large-range matching, regularized by perceptual and geometric auxiliary losses. Experiments demonstrate that UGD-FM achieves state-of-the-art denoising performance with only one or two inference steps, and is compatible with modern single-image restoration backbones.

</details>


### [66] [DreamRealm: Exploring and Materializing Hallucination Worlds for Trustworthy Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06433)
*Benlei Cui,Chunyang Chai,Yiliang Zhang,Xiaoqian Xia,Chengwen Yao,Song Li,Longtao Huang,Yuwen Zhai,Haiwen Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出DreamRealm，一种通过挖掘模型潜在幻觉并用最小图像编辑构造“幻觉-图像匹配”监督信号的post-training框架，用于缓解多模态大语言模型的视觉幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM即使整体表现较强，在依赖细微视觉细节的任务中仍易产生视觉幻觉。已有方法一类通过图像检索、生成或编辑构造视觉对比数据，但对比并非围绕模型自身真实幻觉形成，监督信号可能无法精准纠正其实际感知混淆；另一类通过固定图像条件下的回答修正或偏好学习抑制幻觉输出，但对图像输入侧监督信号的设计探索不足。作者受人类纠正错误认知启发，希望将模型“错误回答”反向映射到“使该回答成立的图像证据”，从而构建更贴合模型自身幻觉的细粒度监督。

Method: 框架包含三个核心部分：第一，数据构造流程RealmForgePipe，从目标模型（如Qwen3.5-9B）中挖掘显性和潜在幻觉回答，并通过最小化编辑原始图像，使每张图像成为某个幻觉回答可被合理支持的正例；第二，构建Realized Hallucination Dataset（RealmSet），显式关联图像与潜在幻觉回答；第三，训练目标RealmMatch，在图像—回答组合上构建likelihood score matrix，并联合监督学习与双向对比损失，强化“回答与其真实支持图像”的正匹配、抑制“回答与不相关图像”的错配。

Result: 相较原始Qwen3.5-9B，DreamRealm在六个视觉感知与幻觉基准上取得提升，其中HallusionBench准确率提高7.26个百分点，MMVP pair accuracy提高11.34个百分点。数据构造和训练方法的消融实验进一步支持了该设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文表明，将视觉幻觉缓解问题重构为“幻觉回答—支持图像”的匹配与归因学习，是一种有前景的post-training范式；其优势在于监督直接针对模型自身易混淆的细粒度视觉证据，而非泛化地纠正答案或偏好。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remain prone to visual hallucinations when correct responses depend on subtle visual details. One line of post-training methods constructs visual contrasts through image retrieval, generation, or editing to improve fine-grained perception. However, these contrasts are not built around the model's own hallucinations, making it difficult to ensure that supervision targets its actual perceptual confusions. Another line suppresses hallucinated outputs through response correction or preference learning under fixed image conditions, but leaves the design of supervision signals on the image-input side underexplored. Inspired by how humans correct misconceptions, we introduce DreamRealm, a post-training framework that explores diverse latent hallucinated responses, minimally edits original images to make them valid, and trains the model to assign each response to its supporting image. Our data construction pipeline, RealmForgePipe, mines observed and latent hallucinations from a target model and materializes them through image editing. Using hallucinations mined from Qwen3.5-9B, we construct the Realized Hallucination Dataset (RealmSet). Our training objective, RealmMatch, constructs a likelihood score matrix across image--response combinations and combines supervised learning with bidirectional contrastive losses to reinforce correct matches and suppress mismatches. Compared with the original Qwen3.5-9B model, DreamRealm improves performance across six visual perception and hallucination benchmarks, including gains of 7.26 percentage points in HallusionBench accuracy and 11.34 percentage points in MMVP pair accuracy. Together with ablation studies on data construction and training methods, these results demonstrate that hallucination assignment is a promising paradigm for improving visual perception and mitigating hallucinations.

</details>


### [67] [CoDecide: Content-Adaptive Video Decoding for High-Throughput Video Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06455)
*Shristy Shah,Thomas B. Moeslund,Kamal Nasrollahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoDecide 是一种面向视频存档回顾式分析的解码优化方法：利用压缩比特流和语义 novelty 信号，只解码出现新内容的帧和区域，并通过运动向量传播检测结果，从而大幅降低解码开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频存档分析的主要瓶颈在解码而非检测：当检测器只处理选定帧时，解码会成为主要成本。传统基于运动的压缩域选择器只能识别“有运动”，难以区分场景中一直存在的移动目标和真正出现的新内容。

Method: 使用冻结的 DINOv2 教师模型生成无标签语义 novelty 信号，训练轻量压缩域学生模型预测应解码的帧与区域；推理时仅解码新内容所在的帧/区域，再将检测结果通过运动向量传播到跳过的帧。

Result: 在 VIRAT、MEVA 和 ShanghaiTech 上采用 leave-one-dataset-out 协议评估。相同解码预算下，CoDecide 比既有压缩域选择器恢复更多移动目标检测结果，并优于在目标摄像头数据上直接训练的对比方法；相比逐帧解码和检测的密集流程，速度提升 15 到 24 倍。

Conclusion: 通过压缩域语义 novelty 学习，CoDecide 能在不牺牲检测覆盖的情况下显著减少解码成本，并可跨摄像头部署而无需逐摄像机重新训练。

Abstract: Analytics on video archives are bottlenecked by decoding. In a dense pipeline that runs a detector on every frame, decoding is a small fraction of the runtime, but once the detector is restricted to selected frames, decoding takes over as the main cost. We introduce CoDecide, a retrospective analytics system that only decodes the frames where new content appears, using signals from the compressed bitstream to find them. Motion tells us something is moving, but not whether it is new to the scene or has been there the whole time. To make that distinction, we supervise a lightweight compressed-domain student on a label-free novelty signal from a frozen DINOv2 teacher, which fires on semantically new regions rather than on any motion. Consequently, the student model generalizes to new camera deployments without requiring per-camera retraining. At inference, it decides which frames to decode and which regions to reconstruct. Detections from the decoded frames are propagated to the skipped frames using motion vectors. We evaluate CoDecide with a leave-one-dataset-out protocol on VIRAT, MEVA, and ShanghaiTech. Against a dense per-frame detector, CoDecide recovers more moving-object detections than prior compressed-domain selectors at an equivalent decoding budget, outperforming even a competitor trained directly on the target camera. CoDecide runs 15 to 24x faster than a pipeline that decodes and detects every frame. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [68] [EmoReward: Self-Evolving Reward Calibration for Emotional Video Generation RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06518)
*Mingzhe Zheng,Yue Wu,Jing Tan,Chang Zou,Dengyang Jiang,Zeyue Tian,Xuanhua He,Ryan Xu,Yunyang Ge,Sihui Ji,Yunlong Lin,Kaihang Pan,Hongru Yan,Sixiang Chen,Dianyi Wang,Julian Zhang,Weijie Kong,Changlin Li,Lanqing Guo,Zhao Zhong,Qifeng Chen,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EmoReward：一种面向视频生成情感表达的多奖励自适应校准机制，通过训练轨迹中的证据可靠性与反思门控，动态分配不同情感奖励信号的权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成中的情感质量依赖主观审美，缺乏唯一正确目标；现有固定奖励容易忽略情感意图并诱发奖励作弊，而RLHF又受个体审美差异和面部、动作、色彩线索跨叙事语境校准困难影响。

Method: EmoReward首先从情感提示中规划具体接地线索，再用异构信号原语对生成视频打分；Reflective Reward Gating通过阶段、人脸置信度、冲突、细腻度等实例级门控校准贡献，并对活跃奖励权重归一化得到融合奖励；RewardMemory在已完成训练运行中根据rollout分数与结果估计各原语可靠性，为下一轮训练设置有界乘子，在不更新奖励模型的情况下调整多奖励融合权重。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo 1.5上，多个RL算法均表现出改进，并在SAGE-GRPO下优于其他agentic reward方法；消融实验与迁移到SD 3.5和FLUX的结果进一步支持自适应奖励校准的有效性。

Conclusion: EmoReward通过“证据可靠性蒸馏 + 反思门控 + 奖励记忆校准”实现了面向视频情感生成的自适应多奖励融合，可提升主观、非可验证场景下RL后训练的稳定性与情感表达质量。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has advanced generative model post-training in verifiable domains such as code and mathematics, but evaluating video quality, especially emotional expression, often depends on subjective aesthetics and has no uniquely correct target. Existing approaches typically rely on fixed rewards for visual quality, motion quality, and text alignment that poorly capture emotional intent and can invite reward hacking, while RLHF is difficult to scale and subject to individual aesthetic differences that complicate the calibration of facial, body motion, and chromatic cues across narrative contexts. We introduce EmoReward, a new self-evolving training mechanism that distills evidence reliability from reward traces collected during an actual training run to adaptively guide multi-reward weight allocation in subsequent training by determining which emotional evidence to trust and how much to weight it. EmoReward first plans specific grounding cues from the emotional prompt to guide evaluation, then uses heterogeneous signal primitives to score the generated video during the act stage. Reflective Reward Gating calibrates their contributions through instance-level gates for phase, face confidence, conflict, and subtlety, then renormalizes weights over the active set to produce the sample's fused reward. RewardMemory then estimates each primitive's reliability from rollout scores and outcomes within each emotional regime in the finished training run, then fixes bounded multipliers that calibrate fusion weights in the next training run without updating reward models. Experiments on HunyuanVideo 1.5 show improvements across multiple RL algorithms and compare EmoReward with other agentic reward methods under SAGE-GRPO. Ablations and transfer experiments on SD 3.5 and FLUX further support adaptive reward calibration for visual generation.

</details>


### [69] [MKD2World: A Typed Spatial MarkDown World Model For Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06544)
*Zhihao Nan,Sifan Zhou,Yiming Cheng,Jinkai Zheng,Zheng Zhu,Si Shi,Ming Li,Fei Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种面向桌面 Web 界面的文本化状态表示 Typed Spatial Markdown（TSM），并基于此训练了两阶段桌面 Web 世界模型 MKD2World。TSM 能同时编码界面布局、控件语义、元素类型、显式坐标、控制状态和层级关系，从而提升 GUI 智能体对“执行动作后界面会变成什么样”的预测能力。实验显示，MKD2World-9B 在域外测试和在线 Web 任务上均显著改善了 GUI 智能体的决策效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI 世界模型依赖准确预测动作后的界面状态，但已有状态表示难以同时刻画网页界面的空间布局、语义类型和显式位置，限制了智能体进行可靠的动作后果预测和任务规划。作者希望通过一种更结构化、可渲染、可训练的文本化界面状态表示，解决截图到结构化状态转换、以及动作条件状态预测中的表示不足问题。

Method: 1. 提出 Typed Spatial Markdown（TSM）：一种 Markdown 风格的桌面 Web UI 状态表示，按空间关系序列化 block 级和 atomic 级 UI 元素，统一编码元素类型、视觉语义、显式位置、可选控制状态和层级归属。 2. 构建 AtomBlock-WebUI 数据集：在两种粒度上提供精确标注，通过向 HTML 注入元素类型标签并在渲染后直接提取 bounding box，避免从网页结构反推标注造成的误差。 3. 训练 screenshot-to-TSM 渲染器：将网页截图转换为 TSM 结构化状态。 4. 提出 MKD2World 桌面 Web 世界模型，采用两阶段训练：第一阶段学习当前界面状态描述，第二阶段学习动作条件下的下一状态预测。 5. 推理时，Web 智能体按需查询 MKD2World 预测未来界面，并执行“digest”步骤，从预测状态中解释和抽取任务相关证据，用于选择最佳动作。

Result: MKD2World-9B 在域外测试集中取得 26.50 的 EleF1 分数，比 GPT-5.6-Sol 的 14.98 高 11.52 分，说明其在界面元素理解或状态预测方面具有较强优势。在 Online-Mind2Web 任务中，引入 MKD2World-9B 后，Qwen3.5-35B Web 智能体的任务成功率从 22.94% 提升到 37.61%，表明世界模型前瞻能力能够显著改善实际 GUI 任务决策。整体来看，MKD2World 在领先的开源 GUI 世界模型中取得有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将 GUI 状态表示为兼具类型、语义、空间位置和层级结构的文本化 Markdown 形式，有助于构建更可靠的网页世界模型。通过高质量标注数据和两阶段训练，MKD2World 能够较好地预测动作后的界面状态，并通过预测状态的解释与证据抽取增强 Web 智能体的决策能力。TSM 与 MKD2World 为 GUI agent 的世界模型前瞻机制提供了可复现、可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents can improve decision-making with world-model foresight, provided that predicted states accurately reflect post-action interfaces. Existing state representations fail to jointly capture interface layout, UI semantics, and explicit locations. We introduce Typed Spatial Markdown (TSM), a Markdown-style textual state representation for desktop web interfaces. TSM serializes block-level and atomic-level UI elements according to their spatial relationships, jointly encoding their types, visual semantics, explicit locations, optional control states, and hierarchical membership. To train a screenshot-to-TSM renderer, we construct AtomBlock-WebUI, a dataset with precise annotations at both granularities. By injecting element-type labels into HTML and directly extracting bounding boxes after rendering, we avoid annotation errors caused by post-hoc inference from webpage structures. Building on TSM, we propose MKD2World, a desktop web world model trained in two stages for current-state description and action-conditioned next-state prediction. During inference, the web agent queries MKD2World as needed and performs a ``digest'' step, in which it interprets each predicted state and extracts task-relevant evidence for selecting the best action. Experiments show that MKD2World-9B achieves an EleF1 score of 26.50 on the out-of-domain test set, outperforming GPT-5.6-Sol(14.98) by 11.52 points. Across Online-Mind2Web tasks, MKD2World-9B improves the success rate of the Qwen3.5-35B web agent from 22.94% to 37.61%, achieving competitive performance among leading open-source GUI world models. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [70] [LungMend: A Healthy-Only Latent Diffusion Shape Prior for Lung Mask Correction in Severe Tuberculosis CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06556)
*Rezkellah Noureddine Khiat,Pierre-Yves Brillet,Catalin Fetita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LungMend，一种基于潜在扩散模型的肺部CT分割掩膜后处理方法，用于修复R231等模型在重症肺结核CT中漏掉空腔和实变区域的错误。该方法仅使用健康肺部解剖结构和合成损坏掩膜进行训练，无需病理数据或CT强度信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肺部CT分割模型在重症肺结核图像上表现不佳，因为肺内空腔和实变组织在视觉上可能类似于非肺组织，导致分割模型将其从肺部掩膜中错误排除。这会影响后续肺部体积、病灶区域和量化分析等任务的准确性。

Method: 作者设计LungMend作为分割掩膜的后处理修正模型。其核心是潜在扩散模型，在健康肺部图像对应的损坏掩膜上进行合成训练，使其学习从错误分割掩膜和骨骼解剖信息中恢复完整肺部轮廓。该方法不依赖肺结核病理图像，也不使用CT原始强度信息。

Result: 在专家标注的CT切片上，LungMend能够找回R231漏掉的肺内空腔和实变区域，提高肺部掩膜的分割准确性和完整性。论文同时提供了代码。

Conclusion: LungMend表明，即使不使用病理CT数据或原始强度信息，仅依赖健康解剖先验和合成损坏掩膜训练，也可以有效修正肺部分割模型在重症肺结核CT中的掩膜错误，是一种实用的后处理方案。

Abstract: Lung segmentation models such as R231 are widely used, but they fail on severe tuberculosis (TB) CT: cavities and consolidations look like non-lung tissue and are left out of the mask. We present LungMend, a post-processing model that corrects such masks using only healthy anatomy. A latent diffusion model, trained on healthy lungs with synthetically corrupted masks, learns to recover the complete lung from the faulty mask and the bone anatomy, without any pathological data or CT intensities. LungMend recovers cavities and consolidations that R231 misses and improves accuracy on expert-annotated slices. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [71] [BudgetSplat: Budget-Controllable Generalizable Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06593)
*Yuxi Hu,Jun Zhang,Wei Mao,Yuchen Rao,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BudgetSplat提出一种预算可控的前馈3D Gaussian Splatting框架，通过联合学习高斯重要性、空间覆盖和跨视图冗余，将最终高斯表示压缩到用户指定的保留比例，在保持渲染质量的同时显著降低表示体积和渲染开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈像素对齐式3D Gaussian Splatting方法通常生成高度冗余的高斯基元，导致最终表示大小和渲染成本随图像分辨率及输入视图数量快速增长，不利于高效存储、部署和新视角合成。

Method: 提出BudgetSplat，将高斯压缩建模为重要性估计、空间覆盖和跨视图冗余的联合问题。采用单一训练模型支持不同上下文视图数量和多个测试时预算，无需按预算重新训练或按场景压缩。具体包括：Intra-Cross Mask Supervision提供结构、覆盖和冗余感知的高斯重要性监督；budget-conditioned feature modulation根据保留比例调节高斯不透明度和属性；Coverage-Aware Budget Selection结合patch级预选、视图级不透明度排序和3D冗余抑制，构建紧凑且空间分布合理的高斯表示。

Result: 在室内重建和跨数据集泛化实验中，该方法在不同输入视图数和保留比例下均呈现稳定的质量与体积权衡，能够显著降低最终表示的存储成本和渲染成本，同时保持较好的渲染质量。

Conclusion: BudgetSplat通过预算可控、学习驱动的高斯压缩机制，为前馈3D Gaussian Splatting提供了更紧凑、更高效的通用新视角合成表示，适合作为一种无需重新训练或场景级压缩的可部署方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting enables efficient generalizable novel view synthesis, but existing pixel-aligned methods often generate highly redundant Gaussian primitives, causing the final representation size and rendering cost to grow rapidly with image resolution and the number of input views. We propose BudgetSplat, a budget-controllable feed-forward framework that adapts the final Gaussian representation to a user-specified preservation ratio. Rather than treating compaction as simple importance ranking, we formulate it as a joint problem of importance estimation, spatial coverage, and cross-view redundancy. A single trained model supports varying numbers of context views and multiple test-time budgets without per-budget retraining or per-scene compression. To realize this formulation, Intra-Cross Mask Supervision provides structure-, coverage-, and redundancy-aware guidance for learning Gaussian importance, while budget-conditioned feature modulation adapts Gaussian opacity and attributes to the requested preservation ratio. Coverage-Aware Budget Selection further combines patch-wise pre-selection, view-wise opacity ranking, and 3D redundancy suppression to construct compact yet spatially distributed Gaussian representations. Experiments on indoor reconstruction and cross-dataset generalization demonstrate consistent quality--size trade-offs across different input view counts and preservation ratios, substantially reducing final representation storage and rendering cost while preserving rendering quality.

</details>


### [72] [SpikePose: Lightweight Video Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06594)
*Yunhao Huang,Kaushik Bhargav Sivangi,Tianyi Yu,Fani Deligianni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpikePose是一种轻量级视频人体姿态估计方法，采用单步脉冲编码器与运动对齐热图记忆模块，以极低能量消耗实现空间定位精度与运动质量的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效视频人体姿态估计需要在定位精度、运动质量和能耗之间取得平衡。脉冲神经网络虽具有潜在能效优势，但在大规模非事件驱动视频任务中仍与经典人工神经网络存在性能差距，因此需要一种能增强时序/运动推理且开销较小的架构。

Method: SpikePose将脉冲编码器用于逐帧空间证据提取，并引入运动对齐热图记忆模块（MAM），用于估计关节位移、对齐历史热图，并自适应融合当前与历史预测。时序推理被限制在脉冲主干之外的轻量模块中，从而避免重型时序计算带来的能耗负担。

Result: 在MPII和NTU60 Cross-Subject固定子集上，与带MAM的同架构ANN相比，SpikePose估计算术能耗降低91.4%，代价仅为阈值0.5下的头骨归一化PCK下降1.12点。加入MAM后，速度和加速度误差分别降低28.13%和31.46%，同时定位精度略有提升，但仅增加0.003M参数和每帧0.0023解析GFLOPs。

Conclusion: SpikePose表明，通过轻量级运动对齐热图记忆与单步脉冲编码结合，可以在视频人体姿态估计中显著降低能耗，并有效改善运动估计质量，有助于缩小脉冲网络与传统ANN在大规模视频任务上的差距。

Abstract: Efficient video human pose estimation must balance localisation accuracy, motion quality, and energy cost. We present SpikePose, a lightweight video pose estimator that pairs a frame-reset, single-step spiking encoder with motion-aligned heatmap memory (MAM). The encoder supplies per-frame spatial evidence, while MAM estimates joint displacement, aligns historical heatmaps, and adaptively fuses them with current predictions. By confining temporal reasoning to a compact, lightweight module outside the spiking backbone, SpikePose helps bridge the gap between spiking and classical Artificial Neural Networks (ANN) on large-scale, non-event-driven video tasks. We evaluate spatial accuracy, motion errors, and estimated arithmetic energy on MPII and a fixed NTU60 Cross-Subject subset. Against the architecture-matched ANN with MAM, SpikePose cuts estimated arithmetic energy by 91.4% at a cost of just 1.12 points in head-bone-normalised PCK at 0.5 threshold (PCK_HB(0.5)). Within SpikePose, adding MAM reduces velocity and acceleration errors by 28.13% and 31.46% while slightly improving localisation, at a cost of only 0.003M parameters and 0.0023 analytical GFLOPs per frame. Code will be released on GitHub upon acceptance.

</details>


### [73] [Uncertainty-Driven Efficient Communication for UAV-Based Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06635)
*Jiuwu Hao,Yuting Wan,Liguo Sun,Zao Zhang,Yueyang Wu,Pin Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 UCP：面向无人机协同感知的不确定性驱动通信框架，通过观测不确定性与判别不确定性筛选关键区域，并用 U-Fusion 实现高效跨智能体信息融合，提升性能—带宽权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机协同感知可弥补遮挡与感知范围不足，但通信带宽和算力受限；现有空间选择方法多聚焦前景区域以降低漏检，却忽视感知歧义区域，难以解决误检问题。

Method: UCP 利用观测不确定性识别传感器受限区域，利用判别不确定性捕获模型在决策边界附近的歧义区域，据此生成空间请求图与供给图以控制智能体间通信；接收到的特征消息由不确定性引导的交叉注意力模块 U-Fusion 聚合。

Result: 在三个基准上的三维目标检测与轨迹预测任务中，UCP 取得了更优的性能—带宽折中，并表现出较高可解释性与强泛化能力。

Conclusion: UCP 将不确定性作为协同价值度量，兼顾漏检与误检相关的模糊区域，为带宽受限的无人机协同感知提供了高效、可解释的通信与融合机制。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based collaborative perception enables multiple agents to share complementary perceptual information, overcoming limitations such as occlusion and limited sensing range. Communication efficiency is a critical bottleneck, as UAVs operate under stringent bandwidth and computational constraints. However, existing spatial selection methods focus predominantly on foreground regions to reduce missed detections, neglecting perceptually ambiguous regions and leaving false detections unresolved. We propose UCP, an uncertainty-driven framework that characterizes collaborative value through observational uncertainty, identifying sensor-limited regions, and discriminative uncertainty, capturing model-level ambiguity near the decision boundary. These estimates drive spatial request and supply maps for efficient inter-agent communication, and the received messages are aggregated via U-Fusion, an uncertainty-guided cross-attention module. Experiments on three benchmarks across 3D object detection and trajectory prediction demonstrate a superior performance-bandwidth trade-off with high interpretability and strong generalizability.

</details>


### [74] [Human Motion Prediction via Continual Prior Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06637)
*Jianwei Tang,Jian-Fang Hu,Tianming Liang,Xiaotong Lin,Jiangxin Sun,Wei-Shi Zheng,Jianhuang Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向人体运动预测的时序持续学习框架CPC与CPC++，将不同未来时刻的预测拆分为多个子任务并渐进式训练，以缓解长期预测对短期预测学习的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HMP方法通常同时学习多个未来时刻的预测，容易让长时程预测干扰短时程预测，并且渐进式训练会带来先验知识遗忘问题。

Method: 提出Continual Prior Compensation，把不同时刻的运动预测划分为子任务并进行多阶段训练；引入可学习的Prior Compensation Factor显式建模先验信息损失，并通过最小化合理上界实现高效联合学习；进一步提出CPC++，利用Fine-Grained Prior Compensation Factor为每个子任务估计先验信息损失。

Result: 在三个HMP基准数据集上，结合多个SOTA骨干模型进行实验，结果显示所提框架具有有效性和灵活性，可适配不同模型、数据集和应用场景。

Conclusion: CPC与CPC++为人体运动预测提供了一种灵活且有效的持续学习范式，能够改善多时刻预测训练中的相互干扰与先验遗忘问题，提升不同HMP模型的预测表现。

Abstract: Human Motion Prediction (HMP) aims to predict future human poses at different moments according to observed past motion sequences. Previous approaches mainly treated the prediction of different temporal moments as a single prediction task and learned the predictions of varied moments simultaneously, which would encounter a main limitation: the learning of short-term predictions (referring to ``near-future'' prediction) could be hindered by the predictions of long-term (referring to ``far-future'' prediction) motions. In this paper, we develop a novel temporal continual learning framework called Continual Prior Compensation (CPC) to progressively train HMP models, in which we divide the prediction task of motions corresponding to varied temporal moments into several subtasks and train the model in a multi-stage manner. To mitigate the prior information forgetting in the progressive training, we further introduce a learnable random variable Prior Compensation Factor (PCF) to explicitly measure the prior knowledge loss. We theoretically show that the PCF can be efficiently learned together with the model parameters by minimizing a reasonable upper bound of the objective function. The proposed CPC is further enhanced to estimate the prior information loss for each subtask and a new framework called Continual Prior Compensation++ (CPC++) with Fine-Grained Prior Compensation Factor (FGPCF) is finally developed. Our CPC and CPC++ frameworks are quite flexible and can be easily integrated with different HMP backbone models and adapted to various datasets and applications. Extensive experiments on three HMP benchmark datasets using multiple SOTA HMP backbones (PGBIG, siMLPe, MotionMixer, and LTD) demonstrate the effectiveness and flexibility of our frameworks.

</details>


### [75] [Dynamical State-Space Diffusion for Spatiotemporal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06652)
*Woojun Lee,Hankyeol Kim,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出动力学状态空间扩散模型 DSSD，将时间相关性从去噪器中剥离，转而学习扩散噪声过程本身的时域结构；其核心是用稳定对角状态空间模型的阻尼振荡模态构造时间卷积算子，并通过平稳协方差的 Cholesky 因子实现因果、可逆且方差匹配的扩散过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列扩散模型通常对每个未来帧施加相互独立的噪声，时间依赖完全交给去噪器建模。这种设计忽略了噪声过程自身也可以携带时间结构，并且有限长度片段中初始状态未观测会导致早期帧噪声量与扩散调度不一致。

Method: DSSD 使用稳定对角状态空间模型的阻尼振荡模态学习时域核，使其能够表达多时间尺度和振荡相关性，同时保持去噪器架构不变。为了正确处理有限片段，作者将状态空间模型的平稳协方差矩阵的 Cholesky 因子作为时间算子，证明该算子具有因果性、精确可逆性、帧级噪声方差与标准扩散一致，并在干净端退化为恒等映射。训练时让去噪器预测时间相关噪声，采样时逐步移除并重新施加时间混合。

Result: 在科学领域视频和机器人视频数据集上，DSSD 优于共享相同去噪器的扩散基线。训练过程中学习到的模态演化表明，该时间算子能够根据数据集中的时间结构自适应调整。

Conclusion: DSSD 表明扩散模型的时间相关噪声过程本身可学习，而非完全依赖去噪器。该设计在不改变去噪器结构的情况下提升了时间预测性能，为面向视频、时序和科学数据的扩散模型提供了一种更合理的时间相关性建模方式。

Abstract: Diffusion models for temporal prediction typically corrupt each future frame with independent noise, leaving temporal dependence entirely to the denoiser. We propose Dynamical State-Space Diffusion (DSSD), which learns the temporal structure of the corruption process itself. DSSD builds a temporal kernel from the damped oscillatory modes of a stable diagonal state-space model, whose learned decay rates and frequencies let the corruption capture multiple timescales and oscillatory correlations, while the denoiser architecture remains unchanged. Running this model on a finite clip, however, requires the state before the first frame, which is unobserved; starting from a zero state leaves the early frames with less noise than the diffusion schedule prescribes. We avoid this by using the Cholesky factor of the model's stationary covariance as the temporal operator. We prove that this operator is causal, exactly invertible, keeps each frame's noise variance equal to that of standard diffusion, and reduces to the identity at the clean end of the process, and that it is exactly the Kalman innovations form of the state-space model. The denoiser is trained to predict the temporally correlated noise, and sampling removes and reapplies the temporal mixing at each step. On scientific-field and robot-video datasets, DSSD outperforms diffusion baselines that share its denoiser, and the evolution of its learned modes during training shows that the temporal operator adapts to each dataset.

</details>


### [76] [CardioDrift: Automatic Cardiac Motion Tracking on a 3D Atlas Mesh via Shape-aware Coherent Point Drift with Slice Shift Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06664)
*Reza Akbari Movahed,Abuzar Rezaee,Shatha Alamri,Edmond S.L. Ho,Fani Deligianni,Ali Gooya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出 CardioDrift，一种全自动、基于图谱的概率化框架，用于从电影心脏磁共振（cine CMR）图像中实现三维心脏运动追踪，并支持具有全局对应点的跨受试者心脏运动分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心脏运动追踪方法存在两类局限：一类缺乏跨受试者的解剖对应关系，限制了对不同个体运动模式的比较；另一类虽使用全局对应点，但通常需要耗时且易出错的半自动步骤将心脏图谱拟合到受试者空间。

Method: CardioDrift 将相干点漂移（Coherent Point Drift, CPD）进行推广，在概率模型中联合注册图谱中多个心脏区域的表面与从 cine CMR 分割图中提取的解剖对应轮廓，从而构建受试者特异性的三维心脏运动。同时，该方法在同一概率模型中嵌入切片位移校正，估计逐切片平移以抑制影响图谱拟合精度的切片错配伪影。

Result: 在从英国生物样本库随机选择的 1301 名受试者和 M&M 数据集上的评估显示，CardioDrift 对左心室（LV）、心肌（Myo）和右心室（RV）的 Dice 相似性系数分别达到 92.23±8.70%、82.11±7.56% 和 88.69±4.13%。在两个数据集的所有距离与重叠指标上，该方法均优于最强基线。

Conclusion: CardioDrift 实现了完全自动、基于图谱、具有全局对应点的三维心脏运动表示，克服了现有方法在跨受试者运动评估和图谱拟合上的局限，为基于 CMR 的心血管疾病心肌运动异常定量分析提供了更准确高效的工具。

Abstract: Automatic cardiac motion tracking from cine cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging is essential for quantifying myocardial motion abnormalities associated with cardiovascular disease. Existing methods fall into two groups: those that represent motion without anatomical correspondence across subjects and therefore limit inter-subject motion assessment; and those that represent motion with globally corresponding points, which typically require a time-consuming and error-prone semi-automatic step to fit a cardiac atlas to the subject space. To address these limitations, this study proposes CardioDrift, a fully automatic, atlas-based probabilistic framework that represents subject-specific cardiac motion on a 3D atlas mesh with globally corresponding points. The Coherent Point Drift (CPD) formulation is generalised to jointly register the atlas surfaces of multiple cardiac regions to the anatomically corresponding contours extracted from cine CMR segmentation maps throughout the cardiac cycle. Slice-shift correction is embedded in the same probabilistic model, estimating slice-wise translations that suppress the misalignment artefact limiting atlas-fitting accuracy. Evaluation on 1301 randomly selected UK Biobank subjects and the M&M dataset, CardioDrift attains Dice similarity coefficients (DSCs) of $92.23\pm8.70\%$, $82.11\pm7.56\%$, and $88.69\pm4.13\%$ for the left ventricle (LV), myocardium (Myo), and right ventricle (RV), exceeding the strongest baseline on every distance and overlap metric of both datasets.

</details>


### [77] [Sekai: Incremental Event Memory for Generalized Reasoning Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06685)
*Zhengtao Liu,Jinrong Xu,Shiu-Hong Kao,Huixin Sun,Yicong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出更贴近流式视频推理的“广义推理视频分割”（GRVS）任务：目标集合会随查询相关事件在视频历史中不断累积而动态变化，并构建诊断基准Isekai与在线框架Sekai，在目标集合演化与有效目标定位上显著优于现有在线RVS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线推理视频分割（Online RVS）大多假设目标集合在整个视频中固定，但流式推理场景中，随着新事件发生，满足查询的对象可能从无到有、由少变多或随时间演化，因此需要模型在因果历史约束下持续维护目标有效性，并定位当前可见的有效目标。

Method: 提出GRVS任务，将目标集合定义为由查询相关事件在已观察视频历史中累积决定。构建Isekai基准：60个视频、197条查询、11类时间查询族、12,569个逐时刻评分点，并对目标集合随时间的因果演变进行标注。提出Sekai在线框架，包含两个互补模块：Event Memory Reasoner（EMR）以结构化记忆维护查询相关事件及演化目标状态；Memory-Grounded Decoder（MGD）依据该记忆识别当前有效目标，并在当前帧中定位这些目标。

Result: 实验表明Sekai显著优于现有在线RVS方法，达到50.33 J&F，相比最强基线提升3.2分，验证了事件记忆建模和记忆引导解码对动态目标集合分割的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作指出在线RVS的关键局限是固定目标假设，推动流式视频分割从“固定目标识别”转向“历史驱动的目标集合动态维护与定位”；Isekai和Sekai为评测和建模动态查询相关目标提供了新的基准与方法路径。

Abstract: Online reasoning video segmentation (Online RVS) requires a model to segment query-referred objects as a video streams in, using only the frames observed so far. Existing formulations largely assume a fixed target set throughout the video, overlooking a fundamental property of streaming reasoning: as new events occur, the objects that satisfy a query may change over time. To address this, we propose a new task, Generalized Reasoning Video Segmentation (GRVS), where the target set depends on query-relevant events accumulated over the observed video history. As new evidence accumulates, the target set can evolve from empty to one or multiple objects, requiring a model to continuously maintain target validity while localizing the valid objects currently in view. To evaluate GRVS, we build Isekai, a diagnostic benchmark of 60 videos and 197 queries across 11 temporal query families, with 12,569 scoring points whose target sets are causally annotated over time. We further propose Sekai, an online framework with two complementary components: an Event Memory Reasoner (EMR) that maintains query-relevant events and evolving target states in structured memory, and a Memory-Grounded Decoder (MGD) that identifies valid targets from this memory and localizes them in the current frame. Experiments show that Sekai significantly outperforms existing online RVS methods, achieving 50.33 J&F and improving over the strongest baseline by 3.2 points. Code, example annotations, and evaluation scripts are available at this https URL.

</details>


### [78] [Turns Matter: Advancing Streaming Video RL via Turn-Level Credit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06728)
*Shuai Dong,Kanghui Tian,Linghan Chen,Wenqi Shao,Hongrui Wu,Haowen Hou,Yongfu Zhu,Xuehui Wang,Ziyue Wang,Wenpu Liu,Weichu Xie,Yuqi Xu,Congcong Wang,Kaiwen Tuo,Caoyuan Ma,Taiqiang Wu,Ji Zhao,Wenzheng Chang,Dianyi Wang,Siyuan Wang,Zhongyu Wei,Yu Qiao,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TurnWise，一种面向流式视频理解的回合级 GRPO 框架，通过将优势估计和策略优化从整条轨迹细化到单个决策回合，提升模型在“回应”与“继续观察”之间的决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解要求模型在多轮交互中反复决定是回答还是继续等待。现有 GRPO 方法虽然重视奖励设计，但通常仍使用整条轨迹级别的共享优势信号，容易把早期成功或失败的信用错误扩散到后续回合：在已经成功回答后仍鼓励冗余回复，或在早期失败后抑制有价值的后续追问与纠正。

Method: TurnWise 从两个层面重构学习信号：第一，采用回合级信用分配，通过“静默补全前缀打分”为每个决策构造基于组内相对优势的剩余回报估计，使每个回合的学习信号与其后续真实收益对齐，而不被早期奖励污染；第二，采用回合平均策略优化，先在每个回合内部平均 token 损失，再跨回合平均，从而让“回复”和“等待/静默”决策获得相等权重，避免输出长度主导训练信号。该设计无需额外学习 critic，也无需额外 rollout。

Result: 在流式视频理解任务上的大量实验表明，TurnWise 优于已有的 RL 方法，能够更有效地产生有用回应并减少冗余输出。

Conclusion: 将 RL 训练信号从轨迹级优势细化到回合级决策，是提升流式视频理解策略学习的关键。TurnWise 通过回合级信用分配和回合平均优化缓解了反馈粒度与交互粒度不匹配的问题，为无 critic、无额外 rollout 的流式决策训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires models to repeatedly decide whether to respond or continue observing. Recently, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has been applied to this setting, with existing approaches emphasizing reward design while retaining a single trajectory-level advantage across turns. Such shared credit can reinforce redundant replies after earlier successes and discourage useful follow-ups after earlier failures. We propose TurnWise, a turn-level GRPO framework that aligns learning with individual streaming decisions. TurnWise addresses the mismatch between trajectory-level feedback and turn-level interaction, promoting informative responses and reducing redundancy. Specifically, turn-level credit assignment uses silence-completed prefix scoring to construct group-relative advantages from each decision's remaining return, separating its learning signal from earlier rewards. Turn-mean policy optimization averages token losses within each turn and then across turns, giving response and waiting decisions equal weight regardless of output length. These complementary designs improve streaming policy learning without a learned critic or additional rollouts. Extensive experiments show our method outperforms prior RL approaches for streaming video understanding.

</details>


### [79] [Coupled Scene Graph Anticipation: When With-Constraint Recall Hides Structural Inconsistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06738)
*Zeno Nongmaithem,Anushttha Srivastava,Anshit Sinha,Apoorv Sisodia,Suman Chowdhury,Prerana Mukherjee,Rajesh P. Barnwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoupledSGA，用于连续时间场景图预测（Scene Graph Anticipation）中相互耦合、互斥关系约束不足的问题；同时分析现有 With-Constraint 评测指标为何无法充分反映结构一致性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间 SGA 模型通常为每个 subject-object pair 使用独立的 Neural ODE 演化关系，导致共享实体的关系在积分过程中无法相互影响，也可能同时预测互斥谓词（如“sitting on”和“standing on”）。此外，常用 With-Constraint 评测通过每组只保留一个谓词来抑制冲突，可能掩盖模型内部是否真正学到结构一致性。

Method: 作者引入 CoupledSGA，通过一个 Relationship Interaction Graph 将相关关系连接起来，并在 ODE 向量场中加入 graph attention，使同一实体相关的关系能够彼此影响。模型还加入基于互斥谓词矩阵的 Structural Consistency Loss（L_SC），用于惩罚同一关系组内互斥谓词同时激活。作者进一步从评测指标和梯度角度分析 L_SC 的行为与 With-Constraint R@K / mR@K 的局限。

Result: 在 Action Genome 上，两个 warm-up epoch 后激活 L_SC 会显著降低 No-Constraint contradiction rate，从 45.92% 降至 4.07%，但 R@20 从 42.21% 降至 35.18%。在 matched-budget 设置下，CoupledSGA 的 R@20 为 40.48%，低于 SceneSayerODE 的 44.78%。作者证明，当每个关系组中胜出谓词及其分数固定时，With-Constraint 指标不会反映组内互斥违规；同时 L_SC 的梯度依赖谓词分数而非真值标签，可能通过放大现有排序或抑制被更多互斥规则涉及的谓词来提升一致性但牺牲召回。

Conclusion: CoupledSGA 主要作为研究连续时间 SGA 中关系耦合与结构一致性的实验平台，而非追求当前最佳性能。With-Constraint 召回率更能反映施加一致性约束带来的代价，而非改进本身。对于限制谓词共激活的方法，建议同时报告 No-Constraint 结果和 contradiction rate，以完整评估结构一致性与预测性能。

Abstract: Scene Graph Anticipation (SGA) predicts object relationships in future video frames. Continuous-time SGA models evolve each subject-object pair with an independent Neural ODE, so relationships sharing an entity cannot influence one another during integration, and nothing prevents mutually exclusive predictions, such as "sitting on" and "standing on", for the same pair. We introduce CoupledSGA, which links such relationships through a Relationship Interaction Graph with graph attention inside the ODE vector field, and adds a Structural Consistency Loss ($\mathcal{L}_{SC}$) based on a matrix of mutually exclusive predicates. Developing CoupledSGA revealed a limitation of the evaluation protocol. With-Constraint evaluation keeps one predicate per group for each subject-object pair, so its R@K and mR@K cannot capture the within-group violations targeted by $\mathcal{L}_{SC}$: we prove they are unchanged whenever each group's winning predicate and its score are held fixed. We further show that the gradient of $\mathcal{L}_{SC}$ depends on predicate scores rather than ground-truth labels: for two-predicate rules it amplifies the ordering the model already holds, and when all scores in a group are equal it suppresses predicates in more exclusion rules more strongly, explaining why the penalty can lower recall while improving consistency. On Action Genome, activating $\mathcal{L}_{SC}$ after two warm-up epochs reduces R@20 from 42.21% to 35.18% while lowering the No-Constraint contradiction rate from 45.92% to 4.07% (matched-budget R@20: 40.48% CoupledSGA vs. 44.78% SceneSayerODE). CoupledSGA is a testbed, not a state-of-the-art claim: With-Constraint recall captures the cost of enforcing structural consistency but not the improvement. We recommend reporting No-Constraint results alongside a contradiction rate for methods that constrain predicate co-activation.

</details>


### [80] [TailSteer: Trajectory-Tail Steering for Drift-Resistant Multi-turn Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06741)
*Ruikang Li,Zixuan Chen,Jiarui Wu,Mingde Yao,Yiyuan Zhang,Yujin Wang,Hanming Deng,Lewei Lu,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TailSteer是一种面向多轮图像编辑的末端训练框架，通过在去噪轨迹尾端保留早期模型行为并加入编辑模型自身rollout中学到的保持修正，显著降低15–20轮编辑中的累积视觉漂移，同时尽量保持基础模型的生成与单轮编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强单轮编辑性能不能保证多轮交互可靠：被编辑后的图像反复作为后续编辑输入时，细小 unintended changes 会逐轮累积成严重视觉漂移。现有方法通常依赖多轮数据训练或跨轮rollout训练，可能改变预训练先验；或依赖推理时特殊机制，难以扩展到基础模型。

Method: TailSteer采用“保留早期去噪动态、集中修正轨迹尾端”的原则。在训练后期介入，使模型在早期去噪阶段尽量接近基础模型，仅在尾端学习细粒度保持修正。修正信号来自编辑器自身的on-policy去噪rollout，以未修改的源内容作为preserve reference，不需要精心准备的ground-truth编辑对。

Result: 在FLUX、SenseNova和Qwen系列模型上，TailSteer在15–20轮编辑中显著降低累积漂移，同时在大量文生图和单图编辑基准上基本保持基础模型原有性能。

Conclusion: 长时程多轮编辑鲁棒性可以通过“最后阶段”的定向校正解锁，而不必重学基础模型已有能力；这为提升图像编辑基础模型的多轮稳定性提供了可扩展训练方案。

Abstract: Strong single-turn editing performance does not guarantee reliable multi-turn interaction: as edited outputs are repeatedly reused, small unintended changes can accumulate into severe visual drift. This long-term drift is often mitigated through training on multi-turn data or cross-turn rollouts, potentially altering pretrained priors, or through specialized inference-time mechanisms that are difficult to scale to foundation models. We introduce TailSteer, a final-stage training framework that improves multi-turn robustness while retaining the generative and editing capabilities acquired through earlier training. TailSteer follows a simple principle: keep early denoising dynamics close to those of the base model and concentrate preservation corrections near the trajectory tail, where fine-grained deviations can be suppressed. These corrections are learned from the editor's own on-policy denoising rollouts, using unchanged source content as a preservation reference without requiring curated ground-truth editing pairs. Experiments across the FLUX, SenseNova, and Qwen families show that TailSteer substantially reduces cumulative drift over 15-20 editing turns while largely preserving base-model performance on extensive text-to-image and single-image editing benchmarks. These results demonstrate that long-horizon editing robustness can be unlocked in foundation models after TailSteer, rather than by relearning their existing capabilities.

</details>


### [81] [EvoWorld: Towards Out-of-Sight World Evolution in Generative Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06748)
*Yequan Bie,Dengyang Jiang,Zhixuan Chen,Xiaozhuan Liang,Haoxuan Che,Quande Liu,Xi Chen,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出“视外因果演化”问题，并设计EvoWorld框架，让视觉语言模型负责维护世界状态和因果推理，视频模型负责渲染，从而提升生成视频中的事件驱动因果一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式视频生成模型虽然能生成视觉上合理的画面，但缺乏显式维护世界状态和推理事件后果的机制，导致其在面对动态世界演化时容易出现行为停滞、因果不一致或后续状态不合理的问题。例如，场景中野兽出现后，模型不仅应生成野兽，还应根据世界状态推理附近人类是逃跑、躲避还是反击。

Method: 作者提出Out-of-Sight Causal Evolution任务，要求模型在事件发生后推理其对世界实体的影响，并在相关实体暂时离开视野后仍能生成因果合理的世界状态。为此，提出EvoWorld“大脑-渲染器协作”框架：视觉语言模型作为世界大脑，观察生成片段、维护动态世界状态并推理状态转移；自回归分段视频渲染器负责可视化生成这些状态。同时构建EvoWorld-Data用于训练，以及EvoWorld-Bench用于评估事件生成、因果反应和重访一致性。

Result: 实验表明，EvoWorld相比近期视频生成模型显著提升了事件条件下的因果演化能力，同时保持了较好的视觉质量和一致性，为当前视野之外的自主世界演化提供了可衡量的推理引导方案。

Conclusion: 生成式视频世界模拟器不能只追求像素真实，还需要显式的世界状态表示与因果推理机制。EvoWorld通过视觉语言模型与视频渲染器协作，将因果推理引入视频世界演化，为构建更一致、可交互、可持续演化的视频世界模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Generative video models are increasingly used as visual world simulators, yet their primary strength lies in rendering plausible pixels rather than reasoning about how a world should evolve. Coherent world evolution requires more than visual fidelity: when an event occurs, a model must infer its consequences for the entities in the world and then render the resulting state. For example, after a wild beast appears, the model should not only depict the wild beast, but also infer whether a nearby person should flee, take cover, or fight back based on the realized scene. Existing diffusion-based video models lack an explicit mechanism for maintaining world state and reasoning over such event-conditioned consequences, often producing visually plausible but behaviorally static or causally inconsistent outputs. To make this reasoning gap concrete and measurable, we propose a new problem, Out-of-Sight Causal Evolution, which requires a model to render an event, reason about its consequences for the evolving world state, and reveal a causally plausible outcome as the world continues to evolve, even out of sight. As a baseline reference solution, we introduce EvoWorld, a brain-renderer collaboration framework in which a vision-language model serves as the world brain: it observes generated segments, maintains an evolving account of the world, and reasons about plausible state transitions, while a segment-autoregressive video renderer realizes them visually. We further introduce EvoWorld-Data and EvoWorld-Bench for training and evaluating event realization, causal reactions, and revisit consistency. Experiments show that EvoWorld substantially improves event-conditioned causal evolution over recent models while preserving visual quality and consistency, establishing a reference point for reasoning-guided, autonomous world evolution beyond the current field of view.

</details>


### [82] [Route First, See Later: Enabling Vision Agents to Reason Scientifically by Separating Routing Policy from Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06760)
*Shubhabrata Mukherjee,Jeffrey Donatelli,Daniela Ushizima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出ZETA，一种面向科学图像分析的多工具视觉智能体架构，核心是将意图路由策略与具体视觉执行解耦，使路由错误与感知/工具执行错误可分别审计和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学图像分析智能体通常把“理解用户意图并选择工具”和“执行检测、计数、分割等视觉操作”混在一个黑箱流程中，导致失败时无法判断问题来自错误路由、意图误解析，还是下游视觉模型/工具执行失效。这种不可审计性使智能体难以可靠应用于高风险科学自动化场景。

Method: ZETA构建可扩展架构，在调用专业视觉工具前先解析科学意图并确定路由策略，将测量类任务建模为基于显式几何结构的确定性操作，而非直接依赖生成式输出。该设计固定合法动作接口，仅改变显式路由策略，从而在零样本科学意图查询基准上隔离并评估路由正确性与下游执行性能，形成可检查的执行轨迹。

Result: 在包含51条科学意图查询的零样本评测中，显式路由语义显著提升路由准确率，尤其是在语义相近的动作边界上，但提升幅度因模型而异。以Qwen 3.8为例，分割路由准确率从6.2%提升至75.0%，检测路由准确率从60.0%提升至100%。ZETA的执行轨迹可分别观察路由决策与下游产物，有助于区分意图选择错误和视觉执行失败。

Conclusion: ZETA为科学视觉智能体提供了可审计的基础设施，通过解耦路由策略与动作执行，使研究者能够识别错误来源并改进系统可靠性。该工作表明，显式意图路由是提升科学图像分析智能体可控性和可解释性的关键路径，也为高可靠科学自动化部署奠定了必要基础。

Abstract: Scientific image-analysis workflows require agents to select among diverse capabilities like description, detection, counting, and segmentation. Current multi-tool architectures conflate intent interpretation with visual execution, masking whether a failure arises from flawed reasoning (misrouting) or failed perception (invalid tool execution). This lack of auditability renders agents unreliable for high-stakes scientific automation. We introduce ZETA, an extensible architecture that operationalizes the decoupling of routing policy from action execution. ZETA resolves scientific intent before invoking specialized visual tools, treating measurement as a deterministic operation grounded in explicit geometry rather than generative output. This architecture exposes the "black box" of agent decision-making, allowing routing correctness and downstream execution to be evaluated as independent failure modes. To our knowledge, we provide the first controlled study in scientific vision-agent routing that isolates explicit routing-policy specification while holding the legal action interface fixed. Evaluation on a zero-shot benchmark of 51 scientific-intent queries shows that explicit routing semantics can substantially improve routing accuracy, particularly at semantically adjacent action boundaries, although improvements vary across models. For Qwen 3.8, explicit routing-policy specification improves segmentation routing accuracy from 6.2% to 75.0% and detection routing accuracy from 60.0% to 100%. ZETA's execution traces make routing decisions and downstream artifacts separately observable, providing a basis for distinguishing intent-selection errors from visual execution failures. ZETA establishes the necessary infrastructure to isolate execution errors from interpretive ones, a critical requirement for deploying reliable scientific vision agents.

</details>


### [83] [Semantic Residual Vector Quantization for Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06781)
*Chang Liu,Ke Han,Niccolò Biondi,Gloria Isotton,Elisa Ricci,Davide Talon,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出SRVQ，将手语生成建模为从语义到动作的层级化残差量化过程，并通过两阶段生成显著提升了文本对齐手语视频/动作生成的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于姿态或仅重建的运动分词方法常将“表达什么手语”和“如何执行动作细节”耦合在一起，缺少与文本对齐的显式语义中间表示，导致生成模型难以同时保证词汇内容准确和手形、轨迹、表情、肢体协调等细粒度表达。

Method: 作者提出Semantic Residual Vector Quantization，即语义残差向量量化框架。其SRV量化器在一个统一模块中学习层级化残差编码：第一层残差编码手语语义内容，后续层进一步细化姿态、手形、运动轨迹和身体协调。生成过程分为两个阶段：先由带词感知条件的掩码语义生成器预测语义码，再由运动学生成器补全剩余残差码，最终合成完整SMPL-X运动表示。

Result: 在CSL-Daily和Phoenix-2014T两个公开手语生成基准上，SRVQ在共享文本-运动嵌入空间中取得R@1分别为82.3和70.3，相比此前最优方法分别提升约30分和46分，同时结合标准几何指标进行评估。

Conclusion: SRVQ通过显式区分语义选择与动作实现，为手语生成提供了可对齐文本的层级化中间表示，显著改善了语义准确率和整体生成质量，验证了“从语义到运动”的两级建模对手语生成任务的有效性。

Abstract: Sign language generation (SLG) maps written sentences into signing motion while preserving both lexical content and fine-grained articulation. This requires a model to decide what signs should be expressed and to realize them through precise handshape, trajectory, facial expression and body coordination. Existing pose-based systems and reconstruction-only motion tokenizers often entangle these two decisions, leaving the generator without an explicit text-aligned intermediate target for what to sign before refining how to sign it. To address this issue, we introduce Semantic Residual Vector Quantization (SRVQ), a framework for SLG that frames generation as a two-level process moving from semantics to kinematics. The newly introduced SRV Quantizer learns a hierarchy in which the first residual level encodes sign content and subsequent levels refine pose, handshape, trajectory, and body coordination, all within a single quantizer and without separate semantic representations or external planning stages. Through word-aware conditioning for hierarchical code generation, a masked semantic generator predicts semantic codes and a kinematic generator completes the residual codes for full SMPL-X motion synthesis. We evaluate using retrieval and FID in a shared text-motion embedding space, alongside standard geometric metrics. On CSL-Daily and Phoenix-2014T, SRVQ achieves R@1 of 82.3 and 70.3, outperforming the previous SOTA by 30 and 46 points, respectively.

</details>


### [84] [VA-TDM: A Unified View for Improving Few-Step Diffusion Models by Velocity Adversarial Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06796)
*Yihong Luo,Tianyu Pang,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Velocity Adversarial Distillation（VAD）及VA-TDM，通过学生采样过程中的速度场构建判别器监督，统一多种分布匹配目标，实现高质量少步扩散/流模型生成，并在文生图和文生视频上取得四步SOTA与强扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏可提升扩散和流模型生成效率，但传统score distillation依赖对学生分布的准确估计，学生得分估计困难且易与更新所用分布不一致。Flow matching允许从学生采样过程构造速度场，为少步蒸馏提供更灵活的监督基础。

Method: 提出VAD：用学习的critic比较学生和教师的velocity fields，将两者不一致转化为学习反馈，并统一score distillation、Fisher-based matching、moment matching及MMD启发式更新。进一步提出VA-TDM：沿学生轨迹进行trajectory distribution matching，提供部分去噪监督，结合transition-aware online velocity learning和dual-form VAD监督。

Result: 实验显示VA-TDM能有效学习少步生成器，在多种backbone的文生图和文生视频任务上达到四步SOTA，并可扩展到33B MiniMax-H3。VA-TDM-H3在人类偏好评测中显著优于few-step Fast-H3，更接近原始多步H3质量。

Conclusion: 该工作将flow matching的速度场监督引入对抗式蒸馏，缓解了少步蒸馏中分布估计偏差问题，并通过统一多个目标增强了理论与方法可解释性，为高效高质量少步生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Few-step distillation offers efficient generation with diffusion and flow models, but preserving quality with only a few sampling steps remains challenging. Distribution-matching methods via score distillation provide a strong foundation, yet they rely on accurately tracking the student's changing distribution. At intermediate generation steps in few-step sampling, student-score estimation can be difficult, and commonly used estimators may describe a different distribution from the one used to update the student. Flow matching allows a more flexible approach to supervision through velocity fields constructed from the student's sampling process. Building on this perspective, we introduce Velocity Adversarial Distillation (VAD), which uses a learned critic to compare student and teacher fields and turn their disagreement into feedback for learning. VAD unifies score distillation, Fisher-based matching, and moment matching, also explaining existing heuristic MMD updates and delivering new training objectives. We further develop VA-TDM, which follows trajectory distribution matching: providing supervision for partial denoising along the student trajectory. Beyond this, VA-TDM combines transition-aware online velocity learning and dual-form VAD supervision across intermediate student transitions. Extensive results show that VA-TDM is powerful for learning few-step generators, achieving state-of-the-art four-step performance in both text-to-image and text-to-video generation across various backbones and scaling effectively to 33B MiniMax-H3. In particular, our VA-TDM-H3 notably outperforms the few-step Fast-H3 and is closer to the original many-step H3 according to human preference.

</details>


### [85] [Open-HumanScale: An Open Recipe for Scaling Human and Robot Experience for Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06817)
*Juncheng Ma,Jiaxin Li,Jiankai Tu,Bo Liang,Guodong Lv,Yufan Deng,Junzhe Tang,Liangzhu Yang,Chen Bian,Zicong Cao,Baohao Zhu,Jianxin Bi,Tengfei Yan,Yinyuan Zhang,Kewei Zhang,Xinglong Chen,Kaiqi Chen,Duomin Wang,Xiaotian Tang,Liang Tan,Li Li,Yuan Xu,Haosen Yang,Yuqi Wang,Jingfei Xia,Songyan Huang,Zhiyang Dou,Zhen Dong,Enze Xie,Tat-Seng Chua,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Open-HumanScale：一个完全开放的第一人称人类视频预训练配方，覆盖 5 万小时数据、严格数据策划、预训练流程与开源语料/模型/代码，并用于构建 HumanWAM 世界-动作模型，展示人类视频规模与机器人操作成功率之间的对数线性缩放规律，以及人类-机器人混合预训练带来的更强泛化与零样本能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称人类视频可为机器人操作学习提供可扩展的先验数据，但要将原始数据规模转化为真正泛化的机器人策略，仍需要系统的数据整理、训练配方和评估标准。当前领域缺少完全开放、可复现的大规模人类视频预训练研究，限制了具身智能缩放规律的验证。

Method: 作者构建 Open-HumanScale-50K 密集标注语料，包含手部运动与自然语言注释；随后在闭环模拟评估中系统研究纯人类预训练，并拟合预训练数据小时数与下游机器人成功率之间的关系；基于该规律训练 HumanWAM-Vanilla，仅使用 5 万小时第一人称人类视频进行预训练，再进行机器人后训练；进一步提出 HumanWAM-Goal，以视觉目标条件化并采用人类-机器人联合预训练，在仿真与真实机器人基准上评估后训练性能和零样本泛化。

Result: HumanWAM-Vanilla 在 RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Clean 上达到 92.08% 成功率，在 Clean2Random 上达到 68.88%；在 RoboDojo 上平均成功率 14.17%、平均进度分 19.00。HumanWAM-Goal 通过人类-机器人混合数据进一步取得 92.70% Clean2Clean 和 84.60% Clean2Random 的新高水平，并在真实机器人上表现出面向未见过视觉目标的涌现式具身上下文学习能力，无需更新参数即可调整操作行为。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，大规模、高质量、完全开放的人类视频预训练可以为机器人操作提供可缩放的先验，并揭示了数据规模与成功率之间的经验对数线性关系；结合视觉目标条件化和人类-机器人联合预训练，可显著提升后训练性能、跨任务泛化与零样本适应能力，为具身智能的可复现缩放研究提供了开放基座。

Abstract: Egocentric human video offers a scalable source of manipulation priors for robot learning, yet translating raw data scale into generalizable robot policies demands rigorous data curation and training recipes. We introduce Open-HumanScale, the first fully open recipe for scaling egocentric pretraining to 50,000 hours, anchored by Open-HumanScale-50K---a curated egocentric video corpus densely annotated with hand motion and natural language. Leveraging this corpus, we systematically investigate pure human pretraining via closed-loop simulation evaluations and uncover an empirical log-linear scaling law linking pretraining data hours to downstream robot success rates. Grounded in these findings, we develop HumanWAM-Vanilla, a world--action model pretrained exclusively on 50k hours of egocentric human video. Upon robot post-training, HumanWAM-Vanilla reaches 92.08% success on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Clean and 68.88% on Clean2Random, alongside a 14.17% mean success rate and a 19.00 mean progress score on RoboDojo. To push the capability frontier further, we explore joint human--robot pretraining with HumanWAM-Goal, a visual-goal-conditioned world--action model. Hybrid data scaling unlocks broad zero-shot generalization across both simulation and physical benchmarks, propelling post-trained RoboTwin 2.0 performance to new state-of-the-art levels: 92.70% on Clean2Clean and a substantial leap to 84.60% on Clean2Random. On physical robots, HumanWAM-Goal further exhibits emergent embodied in-context learning, dynamically adapting its manipulation behavior to unseen visual goals without parameter updates. To support reproducible scaling research in embodied AI, we open-source the Open-HumanScale-50K corpus, data curation and pretraining pipelines, alongside the pretrained and post-trained HumanWAM checkpoints, inference code, and simulation evaluation suites.

</details>


### [86] [Context-Aware Autoregressive Image Generation for Emerging Reasoning Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06838)
*Jixuan Ying,Haoyu Liu,Timing Yang,Tingyu Zhu,Predrag Neskovic,Anand Bhattad,Cihang Xie,Zeyu Zheng,Alan Yuille,Feng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种上下文感知的自回归图像生成框架，使模型能够根据全局上下文和局部信息动态决定下一个生成 token 的位置，而非采用固定或随机顺序，从而提升图像结构鲁棒性与语义推理生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归图像生成方法通常采用预定义或启发式生成顺序，如逐 patch 预测、随机顺序预测或固定尺度一步生成。这种固定顺序可能忽略图像中重要的结构与语义依赖，限制模型的结构鲁棒性和复杂指令理解能力。

Method: 提出上下文感知自回归生成框架，在生成过程中动态选择下一个 token 的位置。模型结合全局上下文与当前局部信息决定生成顺序，使生成过程适配图像结构和语义依赖。该方法可应用于多种图像自回归范式，包括 masked autoregressive 和 scale-wise autoregressive 模型。

Result: 实验表明，上下文感知生成显著提升了不同自回归图像生成范式下的结构鲁棒性。除定量指标改善外，模型还表现出增强的推理能力，能够更好理解复杂语义指令，并生成更符合给定引导条件的图像。

Conclusion: 生成顺序是图像自回归建模中关键但尚未充分研究的因素。上下文感知解码不仅提升结构鲁棒性，还能释放更强的推理感知视觉生成能力，为更可控、更语义一致的图像生成提供了新方向。

Abstract: Autoregressive image generation has recently emerged as a competitive visual generation paradigm, yet its generation order is often predefined or heuristic, such as next-patch prediction, random-order prediction, or one-step generation for specific scales. We observe that these strategies can cause models to overlook important structural dependencies in images, thereby limiting both structural robustness and reasoning capability. In this work, we propose a context-aware autoregressive generation framework, where the model dynamically determines the next token location based on global context and current local information. Rather than following a fixed or random order, our method allows the generation process to adapt to image structure and semantic dependencies. Extensive experiments show that context-aware generation significantly improves structural robustness across different image AR paradigms, including masked autoregressive and scale-wise autoregressive models. Beyond quantitative improvements, we further observe emerging reasoning properties: the model can better understand complex semantic instructions and generate images that more faithfully follow the given guidance. These findings suggest that generation order is a critical yet underexplored factor in image autoregressive modeling, and that context-aware decoding can unlock stronger reasoning-aware visual generation capabilities. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [87] [Same Layout, Different Count: What Number Words Do Past Ten in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06840)
*Liang Yue,Eric A. Vance*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究开放文生图模型在“绘制较多物体”（尤其是超过约 10 个）时能否可靠跟随数字词控制数量，发现模型在少量对象时能计数，但达到数量上限后数字词只短暂影响初始去噪，数量变化高度受起始噪声支配且不一致，并提出更有效的可视化计数提示与评测方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型虽能在小数量下生成正确对象个数，但在请求数量较大时计数能力是否可靠、数字词如何影响生成过程尚不清楚。论文旨在揭示数量控制失败的原因，并评估不同模型、指标和起始噪声条件对“数字词跟随”的影响。

Method: 在五个开放文生图模型上，固定对象类别和起始噪声，仅改变数字词，比较同一噪声下不同数量的生成结果；通过粗粒度与细粒度噪声重绘分析布局与数量的来源；人工盲评 3,088 张未显示数字请求的图像；比较精确匹配分数、相对误差分数以及同噪声多请求测试，并尝试将数字以加噪网格形式写入起始潜在表示。

Result: 所有模型在小数量时能遵循数字，但超过各自“数量感知范围”后表现下降：SD3.5-M 和 FLUX.1-dev 在约 10 之后很快失效；FLUX.2 [klein] 和 Qwen-Image 可松散跟随到约 25。超出范围后，粗粒度起始噪声主要决定画面布局但不决定物体数量；数字词能影响初始去噪步骤，但缺乏稳定方向。将数量以加噪网格写入起始潜在表示后，人眼可识别比例为 59%，而仅靠文本提示时仅 4%。精确匹配或相对误差指标可能无法区分松散跟随与忽略数字的模型。

Conclusion: 大数量文生图计数不能只归因于模型“没看见数字”，更关键的是数字词在去噪早期影响后无法稳定持续作用，且布局主要由起始噪声决定。论文建议用同噪声比较和可视化数量提示进行评测与控制，并警惕常用数量指标在大数量场景下的误导。

Abstract: Ask SD3.5-Medium for twelve of an object and then, from the same starting noise, for twenty-five. The picture keeps its layout, but the count changes about as much as it would under a new random seed, and in no consistent direction. We study what number words do once a request passes about ten objects, in five open text-to-image models. Holding the object and the starting noise fixed, we change one thing at a time, and people who never see the request count 3,088 of the images. Every model follows the number for small crowds, but only up to a point, its reach: SD3.5-M and FLUX.1-dev stop following it soon after ten, while FLUX.2 [klein] and Qwen-Image follow it loosely up to twenty-five. Beyond SD3.5-M's reach, the coarse part of the starting noise fixes the composition of the picture but not how many objects it holds: redrawing only the noise finer than an eighth of the image keeps the layout as well as a new number does, and already moves the count two-thirds as far. The number word still reaches the denoiser and acts in its first steps, but nothing we tried gives it a clear direction. Shown instead as a noised grid in the starting latent, the count is drawn: people find it in 59% of images, against 4% when it is only told. Past ten, an exact-match score barely separates a model that follows the number loosely from one that ignores it, and a relative-error score can even prefer a constant guess of sixteen. A same-noise test with a few requests per object tells them apart.

</details>


### [88] [Where to Connect? Boosting MLLMs via Dynamic Gated Pathways across ALL ViT and LLM Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06844)
*Yingying Yan,Jiaqi Tang,Wei Wei,Qianzhou Wang,Lei Zhang,Jianmin Chen,Yuyang Xia,Botong Geng,Jinjian Wu,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出DGP动态门控通路，用于在多模态大语言模型中建立ViT各层与LLM各层之间的自适应全连接，从而解决传统MLLM只用单一视觉特征或预设多层连接的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流多模态大语言模型通常将视觉编码器某一固定层的特征注入LLM，并假设所有LLM层需要相同粒度的视觉信息；而不同LLM层实际上可能需要不同抽象层级的视觉表示。现有多层融合方法要么在编码器侧压缩特征，要么依赖预定义的稀疏或分层连接，难以充分建模ViT层级与LLM层级之间的动态跨层交互。

Method: 论文提出DGP，即动态门控通路机制。该机制在视觉编码器每一层与大语言模型每一层之间建立全对全连接，使每个LLM层能够根据自身的语义状态动态选择或加权不同视觉层的表示，从而实现自适应、可解释的多层级视觉信息融合。该方法基于LLaVA-1.5-7B和13B进行构建与验证。

Result: 实验表明，DGP在多个多模态基准上相比LLaVA-1.5-7B基线取得稳定提升，例如SQA-I提升4.2、MM-Vet提升3.3、VizWiz和LLaVA-W提升3.1，并在多数基准上优于已有的多层视觉融合方法。逐层路由分析进一步显示，不同LLM层确实会根据需求选择不同粒度的视觉层特征，支持了方法的有效性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了MLLM中“视觉信息应如何接入LLM各层”这一关键但此前被忽视的问题，提出了一种动态门控跨层连接范式。其结果表明，自适应视觉层级路由不仅提升性能，也增强了模型对视觉与语言层级交互的解释能力。未来多模态模型设计应更加重视视觉表示与LLM各层语义需求之间的动态匹配。

Abstract: A central yet under-explored question in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) is where to connect---how visual information from a hierarchical vision encoder should be wired into the layer-wise semantics of a large language model. Mainstream MLLMs inject a fixed single-layer visual representation into the LLM and treat all LLM layers as a single consumer, ignoring that different layers demand different visual granularities. Recent multi-layer fusion methods either compress hierarchical features on the encoder side or rely on predefined sparse or hierarchical layer-to-layer connections, and therefore stop short of modeling adaptive cross-layer interactions between the ViT and LLM hierarchies. We address this gap with DGP (Dynamic Gated Pathways), a routing mechanism that establishes all-to-all connections between every ViT layer and every LLM layer, with each LLM layer dynamically gating its preference over visual layers conditioned on its own semantic state. Built on top of LLaVA-1.5-7B and 13B, DGP delivers consistent gains over the LLaVA-1.5-7B baseline (e.g., +4.2 on SQA$^{I}$, +3.3 on MM-Vet, +3.1 on VizWiz and LLaVA$^{W}$) and surpasses prior multi-layer visual fusion methods on most benchmarks. Layer-wise routing analyses further show that different LLM layers do select different visual granularities on demand, supporting both the effectiveness and interpretability of the proposed dynamic gated pathways. The code is available: this https URL.

</details>


### [89] [Trainspotting: Learning Object-Centric 3D Reconstruction from Found Photos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06849)
*Hanyu Chen,Noah Snavely*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Trainspotting 数据集，用于研究真实世界多用户、跨环境、跨相机拍摄的对象中心照片集合的前馈三维重建能力，并表明在此数据上微调可显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先进前馈重建模型主要在单次拍摄、对象与环境基本固定的合成或受控多视角数据上训练，难以泛化到公共照片档案中常见的“野生”多视角数据，即同一对象或设计由不同人、不同时间、不同相机甚至不同物理个体拍摄。

Method: 作者构建 Trainspotting 数据集，包含来自 Wikimedia Commons 的 12,068 个对象和 311,886 张照片，并重建相机位姿、多视图立体深度和对象掩码。数据按三种场景分类：对象固定于一个地点、对象在不同地点移动、同一设计对应多个物理个体。随后评估近期前馈重建模型，并微调 VGGT-Ω 模型。

Result: 实验显示，近期前馈重建模型在“对象移动”和“多物理个体共享设计”两类场景中最容易失败。在 Trainspotting 上微调 VGGT-Ω 后，其在留出对象上的相机位姿、深度和三维几何精度显著提升；在独立的 NAVI 野生照片基准上也有类似改进，同时常规基准性能基本保持不变。

Conclusion: Trainspotting 揭示了前馈重建模型在真实世界开放照片集合上的泛化瓶颈，并证明利用对象级野外多视角数据进行微调和训练可改善三维重建鲁棒性，而不明显损害既有标准基准性能。

Abstract: State-of-the-art feedforward reconstruction models perform poorly on in-the-wild object-centric photo collections. They are trained largely on single-session object captures whose images vary in viewpoint while the object and its environment remain nearly fixed. Public photo archives contain a different form of multi-view data. They hold found photos of the same object or design: photos taken independently by many people across years, environments, cameras, and sometimes physical units. In this work, we introduce Trainspotting, a dataset of 12,068 objects and 311,886 photos from Wikimedia Commons. We reconstruct each object to recover camera poses, multi-view stereo depth, and object masks. We categorize the objects into three regimes: objects fixed at one site, objects that move between sites, and designs shared by many physical units. Evaluating recent feedforward reconstruction models on Trainspotting shows that they fail most severely in the last two regimes. Fine-tuning VGGT-$\Omega$ on Trainspotting significantly improves camera pose, depth, and 3D geometry on held-out objects. We observe similar improvements on the independent NAVI in-the-wild benchmark, which contains photos of each object in different environments. Performance on conventional benchmarks remains largely unchanged.

</details>


### [90] [Visual Token Routing: Learning to keep tokens that matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06854)
*Pranjal Aggarwal,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Visual Token Routing（VTR），通过联合训练视觉语言模型与轻量级时空路由器，使计算机使用智能体仅保留对动作预测必要的视觉 token，从而在长时任务和实时场景中显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体虽然在一些长时基准上取得超人类表现，但其瓶颈在于每张截图会被编码为大量视觉 token，并在长交互历史中累积成长上下文，导致计算成本过高。作者观察到，多数视觉 token 对下一步动作语义无关，或在多张截图之间时间上冗余，因此可通过稀疏化表示提升效率。

Method: VTR 是一个联合训练框架：视觉语言模型与一个轻量级时空路由器同时训练。路由器检查当前截图和上一截图中的图像区域，并决定对应视觉 token 是否进入语言模型。训练时使用自适应损失，在保持动作预测性能的前提下尽量压缩视觉 token 数量，学习稀疏但信息充分的视觉表示。

Result: 仅需约 2,000 步训练，VTR 在 OSWorld、CUA-World 以及视频游戏动作预测任务上达到与全分辨率模型相当的性能，同时减少 2.5 至 5.8 倍的视觉 token 数量。相关节省可将推理成本降低最多 3.3 倍，并将 prefill 阶段加速最多 2.9 倍。与降低图像分辨率及其他视觉 token 丢弃基线相比，VTR 在 OSWorld 上最高领先 12.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: VTR 能够让计算机使用智能体只处理真正必要的视觉信息，显著缓解长上下文和高分辨率截图带来的计算开销，使其更适合长时任务和实时交互环境，如视频游戏等低延迟应用。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have recently achieved superhuman performance on several long-horizon benchmarks. However, their computational cost limits their use in long-horizon tasks and real-time domains such as video games. A key bottleneck is representing each screenshot as thousands of visual tokens, which accumulate into long contexts as agents reason over extended interaction histories. We observe that most of these visual tokens are either semantically irrelevant to the agent's next action or temporally redundant across screenshots. Building on this observation, we introduce Visual Token Routing (VTR), a joint training framework for learning sparse visual representations that preserve the information needed for action prediction. In VTR, we train the vision-language model together with a lightweight spatio-temporal router that examines each image region in the current and previous screenshots and decides whether its visual token enters the language model. We optimize the router using an adaptive loss to keep the number of visual tokens as small as possible while minimizing performance degradation. With just 2,000 training steps, VTR matches the performance of the full-resolution model on OSWorld, CUA-World, and video game action prediction, while using 2.5-5.8x fewer visual tokens. These savings reduce inference cost by up to 3.3x and speed up prefill by up to 2.9x. VTR also outperforms lowering the image resolution and other baselines for dropping tokens, by up to 12.8 points on OSWorld. Overall, VTR enables computer-use agents to process only the visual tokens they need, allowing them to be used in long-horizon tasks and real-time domains at a fraction of the computational cost. Code and models are available at this https URL

</details>


### [91] [LongBanana: An Expert-Verified Benchmark for Long-Context Multi-Reference Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06856)
*Haoxiang Cao,Yuxuan Zhang,Penghui Du,Bo Li,Jiajiong Cao,Yingying Fan,Zimeng Wu,Yifu Luo,Mingzhe Zheng,Junwen Miao,Huaisong Zhang,Yang Yao,Yu Wang,Dongfu Jiang,Ping Nie,Wenhu Chen,Chaoqun Wang,Changqian Yu,Kelsey R. Allen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LongBanana：面向长上下文多参考图像合成的专家设计与验证基准，并配套LongBanana-Gen智能体生成框架与图式可审计评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成评测难以覆盖智能体驱动场景下需要跨多张参考图检索证据、排除干扰、实体关系绑定并合成一致长上下文图像的能力。

Method: 构建90个任务，采用3×3分类体系，每任务约32张参考图、20个约束和50个评估项，共4670个专家指定原子评估项；以实体关系图为基础，将节点、边和全图义务转化为内容保真、关系绑定和全局约束满足的检查清单，并进行专家标注和一致性验证。

Result: 基准具有较高专家标注一致性（15%双标子集精确一致率89.4%，二次加权Kappa 0.83）；实验显示最小智能体框架优于直接生成，ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect工作流可通过强化证据 grounding 与修复带来依赖框架的性能提升。

Conclusion: LongBanana为密集视觉证据利用和智能体驱动图像生成系统提供了可审计测试床与共同目标，但当前方法仍有框架依赖性，长程多参考生成评测与能力发展仍需进一步系统化推进。

Abstract: We introduce LongBanana, to our knowledge the first expert-designed and expert-verified benchmark for long-context multi-reference image synthesis in the age of agent-driven image generation. The task requires retrieving dispersed visual evidence, rejecting distractors, binding entities and relations, and composing one coherent final image from multiple references. LongBanana contains 90 carefully designed tasks organized by a 3-by-3 taxonomy; each task includes about 32 reference images, 20 constraints, and 50 evaluation items, yielding 4,670 expert-specified atomic evaluation items in total. Its graph-grounded evaluation design instantiates each task with reference images and an entity-relation graph, then converts node, edge, and whole-graph obligations into checklist items for Content Fidelity, Relational Binding, and Global Constraint Satisfaction. Reviewed expert labels include a 15% double-labeled subset with 89.4% exact agreement and 0.83 quadratic weighted kappa. We further introduce LongBanana-Gen, an agent-harness framework for long-horizon image generation; experiments show that minimal agent harnesses improve over direct generation, while a ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect (RPR) workflow yields harness-dependent gains by strengthening evidence grounding and repair. Together, LongBanana provides an auditable testbed for measuring dense visual evidence use and a shared target for developing stronger agent-driven image-generation systems. Code and data are available at the project page: this https URL.

</details>


### [92] [CounterFactual Retrieval for Evidence-Aware Individual Animal Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06864)
*Ankit K. Upadhyay,Aleksandr Algasov,Ekaterina Nepovinnykh,Fedor Zolotarev,Tuomas Eerola,Heikki Kälviäinen,Charles V. Stewart,Lukas Picek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Counterfactual Foreground Retrieval（CFR），一种面向动物个体识别检索的“证据感知”评测协议，通过原图、前景-only和shortcut-only三种视图区分模型是否真正利用动物特征，还是依赖背景或轮廓等身份相关捷径；并发布约0.5M掩码、34个数据集的mask数据及评测代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化动物个体识别模型虽然检索准确率较高，但传统评测无法说明模型匹配证据的来源：高准确率可能来自动物本体特征，也可能来自背景、轮廓等与身份相关的捷径。因此需要一种能够解耦“真实身份特征”和“虚假捷径信号”的评测方式。

Method: 作者提出Counterfactual Foreground Retrieval（CFR）评测协议，将同一query-gallery检索任务放在三个反事实视图下评估：（1）full image：完整图像，衡量标准检索性能；（2）foreground-only：仅保留动物前景，衡量前景支持的检索性能；（3）shortcut-only：移除动物前景，保留背景或轮廓等潜在捷径信息，衡量身份信号在捷径中的可恢复程度。通过比较三种视图的检索结果，得到模型的“evidence profile”，即模型依赖真实动物特征与依赖背景/轮廓捷径的证据画像。

Result: 论文发布约0.5M个掩码，覆盖34个数据集，并提供掩码生成和CFR评测代码，使研究者能够在多种动物个体识别任务上统一评估模型是否依赖动物本体特征，而非背景或轮廓等身份相关捷径。

Conclusion: CFR将单一检索准确率扩展为可解释的证据画像，有助于暴露动物个体识别模型中可能存在的捷径依赖问题；所发布的掩码、数据集和代码为可复现、可共享的评测基准和基础模型研究提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Automated identification of individual animals is moving from small curated studies toward shared benchmarks and reusable foundation models. This progress is useful, but current evaluation leaves the evidence behind a match unresolved, i.e., high retrieval accuracy does not show whether a model used animal features or identity-correlated shortcuts. We address this with Counterfactual Foreground Retrieval (CFR), an evidence-aware evaluation protocol. CFR evaluates the same query-gallery task under different views: full image, foreground-only, and shortcut-only. The full-image measures standard retrieval performance; the foreground-only measures foreground-supported retrieval; and shortcut-only view measure how much identity signal is recoverable from background, or silhouette. This turns a retrieval score into an evidence profile: how well the model performs on the original images, how much of that performance remains when only the animal is visible, and how much identity signal remains recoverable when the animal is removed. We release around 0.5M masks for 34 datasets and code for mask generation and CFR evaluation.

</details>


### [93] [PixelSpline: Continuous Space-Time Video Super-Resolution via Endpoint-Anchored Shared Pixel Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06870)
*Wanqi Yuan,Nianyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种连续时空视频超分辨率框架，通过长上下文编码、连续高分辨率特征升采样和端点锚定的 PixelSpline 轨迹建模，提升运动估计可靠性与插帧/超分一致性，并在空间与时间上采样任务中取得质量与效率的较好折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时空视频超分辨率方法通常仅利用两个端点帧推断运动，并独立建模两个运动方向，容易受到帧间对应关系模糊和轨迹不一致的影响，导致中间帧或高分辨率重建质量不稳定。

Method: 该方法结合三类组件：首先使用长上下文编码获取更可靠的端点表征；其次通过连续上采样直接在请求的高分辨率网格上构建细节外观特征；最后引入端点锚定的 PixelSpline，将每个像素的运动表示为由两个端点共享的连续轨迹，并利用该轨迹将端点颜色扭曲到粗中间帧，再与高分辨率特征对齐后进行残差重建。

Result: 在不同空间和时间上采样设置下的实验表明，所提框架能够在重建质量与计算效率之间取得较好的平衡。

Conclusion: 将长时上下文、连续高分辨率特征构建与像素级连续轨迹建模相结合，可以缓解传统两端点独立运动建模带来的歧义与不一致问题，为连续时空视频超分辨率提供更稳健且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing continuous space-time video super-resolution methods often infer motion from only two endpoint frames and model the two motion directions independently, making them vulnerable to ambiguous correspondence and inconsistent trajectories. We introduce a continuous STVSR framework that combines long-context encoding, continuous HR feature lifting, and an endpoint-anchored PixelSpline. Long-range observations provide more reliable endpoint representations, while continuous upsampling constructs detailed appearance features directly on the requested HR grid. PixelSpline explicitly models each pixel's motion as a single continuous trajectory shared by both endpoints. Using this trajectory, our method warps endpoint colors into a coarse intermediate frame and aligns the HR features for residual reconstruction. Experiments across spatial and temporal upsampling settings demonstrate that the proposed framework offers a favorable quality--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [94] [Procedural Adversarial Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06890)
*Jack Nugent,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Simple-PAT 算法，在程序化输入空间中搜索困难样本，通过对抗式训练与评估降低计算机视觉模型的最坏场景错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多计算机视觉模型在平均误差上表现良好，但在看似简单的新场景中仍会失败。由于这些失败样本不一定存在于固定数据集中，因此需要先高效地发现并生成它们，以提升模型的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Method: 作者研究程序化对抗学习（PAL），利用可解释的程序化生成器将低维控制参数（如布局、光照、姿态）映射为图像及标签。提出 Simple-PAT 算法，在生成器参数空间中采用零阶优化搜索对抗样本，并按需生成训练样本。同时构建 PAL-Dojo，包含面向表面法线估计、图像分割和人体姿态估计的高效程序化生成器集合。

Result: 在跨模型黑盒对抗攻击下，攻击方累计使用 5000 万次查询，Simple-PAT 相比标准训练将最坏情况误差降低约 3.15 倍，相比最强鲁棒训练基线降低约 2.63 倍。

Conclusion: 程序化生成空间中的零阶对抗搜索可有效发现模型易失败的简单场景样本，显著提升计算机视觉模型在对抗评估和训练中的最坏情况鲁棒性；PAL-Dojo 为该研究方向提供了可复用的实验平台。

Abstract: For many computer vision tasks, neural networks can be trained to produce a low average error, yet they still fail on seemingly simple scenes. Reducing these failures requires first finding them, but they may not be in a fixed dataset. We study procedural adversarial learning (PAL), a framework of minimax training and adversarial evaluation which searches the input space of a procedural generator for difficult instances. The procedural generator is an interpretable program mapping a low-dimensional vector of control parameters (e.g. layout, lighting, pose) to a rendered image and its label. We introduce the Simple-PAT algorithm, which uses zeroth-order optimization in the procedural generator's parameter space to propose adversarial training samples that are generated on demand. We also create PAL-Dojo, a collection of efficient procedural generators for surface normal estimation, image segmentation, and human pose estimation. Against a cross-model black-box adversary with 50M aggregate queries, Simple-PAT reduces worst-case error by a geometric mean of 3.15x compared to standard training and 2.63x compared to the strongest robust training baseline. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [95] [Motion-guided Contrastive Regularization For Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06901)
*Yiwen Chen,Qiqi Hou,Jisoo Jeong,Yinhao Zhu,Huaizu Jiang,Amir Said*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对 JEPA 风格世界模型中的“静态坍缩”问题，提出一种运动引导的对比正则化方法，通过强调动作相关的状态变化而非静态背景信息，显著提升了随机背景下视觉操控任务的规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 式世界模型通过在潜在空间中学习动作条件动态模型，避免直接重建观测。然而，仅依赖分布正则化并不保证表示保留控制所需信息。研究发现一类失败模式：编码器倾向保留场景中跨 episode 多样但时间可预测的静态内容（如背景），同时抑制与操控相关的动态状态变化。该问题即使在使用 SIGReg 等正则项时仍可能存在，并通过 OGBench-Cube 中随机背景实验得到验证：仅改变背景即会显著损害规划表现，而底层操控任务本身并未改变。

Method: 作者提出 motion-guided contrastive regularization（运动引导的对比正则化）。其核心是比较模型预测的潜在转移与来自同一 episode 的候选观测转移，从而降低静态背景身份在区分动作结果时的作用。方法使用光流相似度提供软对比目标，使运动相似的候选转移获得相似监督。该目标与潜在预测损失和 SIGReg 结合，鼓励表示保留 episode 内部状态区分，并在相关转移之间保持相似性，从而让潜变量更聚焦于动作相关的动态变化而非静态外观。

Result: 在 OGBench-Cube 随机背景设置下，该方法将规划成功率从 LeWorldModel 的 52% 提升至 78%；在 LIBERO-Object 上从 14% 提升至 54%。同时在其他四个视觉控制基准上保持相当的性能，说明该方法在不损害一般任务表现的情况下，显著缓解了静态背景诱发的静态坍缩问题。

Conclusion: JEPA 风格世界模型的关键风险之一是表示“静态坍缩”：保留与操控无关但可预测的静态内容，抑制任务相关状态变化。运动引导的对比正则化通过光流相似度和候选观测转移监督，能够有效引导表示关注动作相关动态信息，从而显著提升随机背景下视觉操控世界模型的规划性能。

Abstract: JEPA-style world models learn action-conditioned latent dynamics without reconstructing observations, but distributional regularization alone does not ensure that their representations preserve information needed for control. We identify static collapse, a failure mode in which the encoder preserves episode-specific static content while suppressing manipulation-relevant state changes. Because static content is temporally predictable yet diverse across episodes, this failure can persist despite regularizers such as SIGReg. A controlled study on OGBench-Cube shows that introducing randomized backgrounds substantially degrades planning performance while leaving the underlying manipulation task unchanged. To address this problem, we propose motion-guided contrastive regularization. Our approach compares predicted latent transitions with transitions to candidate observations from the same episode, reducing the usefulness of static background identity for distinguishing action outcomes. Optical-flow similarities provide soft contrastive targets, allowing candidates with similar motion to receive similar supervision. Combined with latent prediction and SIGReg, our objective encourages representations to preserve within-episode state distinctions and similarities between related transitions. Our method improves planning success over LeWorldModel (LeWM) from 52% to 78% on OGBench-Cube with randomized backgrounds and from 14% to 54% on LIBERO-Object, while maintaining comparable performance on four other visual-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [96] [From Pixels to Actions - Towards Native Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06918)
*Weichen Fan,Haiwen Diao,Penghao Wu,Siyuan Hu,Ziqi Huang,Tianqi Liu,Mingze Sun,Ziyi Wang,Zhongang Cai,Hanming Deng,Quan Wang,Lei Yang,Lewei Lu,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Dahua Lin,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 NEO-VLA，一种无需固定视觉编码器的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过让图像 token 在主干网络中与语言信息共同演化，使动作专家可从不同层级的视觉表示中提取动作相关信号，从而提升机器人操作的控制性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型通常先通过视觉编码器压缩感知信息，再开始动作推理。然而，机器人操作常依赖局部几何、边界、深度和细微状态变化等容易被抽象压缩丢失的视觉信号，因此固定视觉输出端可能限制动作策略对关键感知信息的利用。

Method: NEO-VLA 采用结构上无编码器的框架：使用轻量级 patch 卷积对图像进行 token 化，并让视觉表示在主干网络中与语言指令联合演化；随后由 flow-matching 动作专家从不同深度的指令条件化视觉表示中获取动作相关信息。作者还通过受控比较、全量微调实验和逐层探测分析验证该设计。

Result: 在受控实验中，原生无编码器架构一致优于基于视觉编码器的对照方法；全量微调可缩小差距但无法完全消除，单纯增加规模也不能恢复差距。逐层探测显示，动作相关关键信号常在最终视觉表示之前达到峰值。在多种操作任务和分布偏移下，NEO-VLA 实现了更强且更鲁棒的控制性能。

Conclusion: 具身控制不应只依赖固定视觉编码器的最终输出，而应能访问整个计算层级中的视觉表示。NEO-VLA 通过联合演化的视觉-语言计算和动作专家跨层读取表示，为更鲁棒的机器人操作提供了新结构范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models are typically built on a simple premise: perception is first compressed by visual encoders, and action reasoning begins only afterwards. Yet manipulation often hinges on precisely the visual signals most vulnerable to this abstraction, e.g., local geometry, boundaries, depth, and subtle state changes. This raises a fundamental question: should action policies inherit a fixed visual endpoint, or directly access evolving visual computation? We introduce NEO-VLA, a structurally encoder-free vision-language-action framework. Images are tokenized by lightweight patch convolutional layers and evolve jointly with language throughout the backbone, allowing a flow-matching action expert to draw from instruction-conditioned visual representations at different depths. This simple change reveals a consistent pattern. Native architectures outperform encoder-based counterparts under controlled comparisons; full fine-tuning reduces but does not erase the gap, and additional scale alone cannot recover it. More strikingly, layer-wise probing shows that the action-critical signals often peak well before the final visual representation. Across diverse manipulation settings and distribution shifts, NEO-VLA consequently achieves stronger and more robust control. Together, these results suggest that embodied control benefits from access to visual representations across the computational hierarchy, rather than a fixed encoder output.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [OdorSpaces: Visual-Olfactory Embodied Navigation in 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05738)
*Changyeon Lee,Kelin Yu,Zheng Wei,Shuna Ni,Ruohan Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 OdorSpaces，一个基于物理仿真的视觉-嗅觉具身导航平台与基准，用于研究智能体在真实感室内环境中利用视觉和气味线索进行源定位与导航的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嗅觉能够提供空气中化学线索的重要信息，但在具身人工智能中仍被严重低估，现有机器嗅觉研究多局限于语义层面的气味识别或感知，较少关注气味在真实三维空间传输、扩散以及空间导航中的作用。因此，需要构建兼具物理真实性和可评估性的视觉-嗅觉具身导航平台，以推动智能体在复杂环境中利用多模态信息完成气味源定位任务。

Method: 作者基于 Habitat-Sim 构建 OdorSpaces 仿真平台，并引入 Fire Dynamics Simulator（FDS）来模拟三维空间中的气味传输与扩散过程，使智能体能够在照片级真实的室内环境中同时接收视觉观测和嗅觉观测。基准任务包括两类代表性的源定位场景：不可见气体泄漏定位和早期火灾定位。作者进一步设计了一个多模态强化学习策略，该策略随时间融合视觉信息和嗅觉测量值，用于指导智能体进行导航和气味源搜索。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的多模态强化学习策略在视觉-嗅觉具身导航任务上优于多种基线方法，能够有效结合视觉和嗅觉信息进行气体泄漏或火源定位。该结果为后续视觉-嗅觉具身导航研究建立了较强的参考基线。

Conclusion: OdorSpaces 通过引入物理驱动的气味扩散仿真和多模态具身导航基准，弥补了现有机器嗅觉研究中偏重语义感知、缺乏空间导航交互的不足。实验结果验证了结合视觉与嗅觉信息对于提升具身智能体源定位能力的价值，为未来多模态具身导航、智能感知和危险气体或火灾定位等研究提供了重要平台和基准。
