Abstract: Olfaction provides critical information about airborne chemical cues, yet it remains largely underexplored in embodied AI, with limited prior machine olfaction work focusing primarily on semantic odor perception. We introduce OdorSpaces, a physics-based simulation platform and benchmark for visual-olfactory embodied navigation. OdorSpaces augments Habitat-Sim with 3D odor transport and dispersion simulated by Fire Dynamics Simulator (FDS), enabling agents to navigate photorealistic indoor environments using both visual and olfactory observations. The benchmark includes two representative source-seeking scenarios: invisible gas-leak localization and early-stage fire localization. We develop an initial multimodal reinforcement learning policy that integrates visual observations and olfactory measurements over time. Experiments show that the policy outperforms a diverse set of baselines, establishing a strong reference for future research on visual-olfactory embodied navigation.

</details>


### [98] [Time-to-Goal: Learned Temporal Metrics for Long-Horizon Planning with JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05847)
*Ruilong Yu,Fei Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出 Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures（JEPA）中的规划目标代价若使用欧氏距离，不能直接保证“离目标越近、代价越低”的时序单调性；作者提出 Time-to-Goal（T2G）这一轻量级排序度量，仅利用离线轨迹中的潜状态三元组和 margin ranking loss 学习时序距离，并将其替换欧氏规划代价，在冻结 JEPA 编码器和预测器的情况下显著提升潜在规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 式规划通过比较预测得到的潜状态与目标编码来选择动作或规划路径。为了让代价函数引导模型朝目标逐步推进，时间上越接近目标的状态应获得越低的代价。然而，现有表示学习方法主要关注预测准确性和分布正则化，并未显式约束目标代价随时间距离单调变化。论文特别考察 flat LeWorldModel（LeWM）中欧氏目标代价与时间间隔的关系，发现其可能弱化不同时刻状态的时序区分，导致规划信号不足。

Method: 作者首先分析 flat LeWM 的欧氏目标代价如何随时间分离变化，识别出在较大时间偏移下不同状态可能具有相似平均代价、从而削弱时序可区分性的条件。为解决这一问题，提出 Time-to-Goal（T2G）度量：从离线轨迹中构造潜状态三元组，用轻量级排序目标训练，并通过 margin ranking loss 鼓励时间上更接近目标的状态具有更小距离。该过程不需要奖励标注或策略标签。训练完成后，JEPA 的编码器和预测器保持冻结，仅用学习到的 T2G 度量替换原有欧氏规划代价。

Result: 在 AntMaze 和 FourRoom 上的诊断实验表明，T2G 比欧氏代价保持更强的时序对比，并改善了长偏移下的近—远排序性能。在四个导航与操作基准上，T2G 将 flat LeWM 的平均规划成功率从 32.7% 提升到 63.0%，接近甚至超过分层 H-JEPA 基线方法。结果说明，即使不使用分层世界模型，仅显式学习目标代价的时间排序也能显著增强 JEPA 的潜在规划能力。

Conclusion: JEPA 规划中的目标代价不能仅依赖欧氏距离或隐式表示学习来形成正确的时序结构。显式学习“离目标时间越近、代价越低”的度量关系，是提升离线潜在规划有效性的关键。T2G 提供了一种无需奖励或策略标签、可替换原有规划代价的轻量方法，使平坦世界模型也能获得接近分层模型的规划表现。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) support planning by comparing predicted latent states with an encoded goal. For this comparison to guide temporal progress, states closer in time to the goal should receive lower costs. However, training representations for prediction and distribution regularization does not directly enforce this ordering. We examine this mismatch in flat LeWorldModel (LeWM) by analyzing how its Euclidean goal cost changes with temporal separation. Our analysis identifies conditions under which states at different long offsets receive similar average costs, weakening their temporal distinction. To learn the missing ordering explicitly, we introduce Time-to-Goal (T2G), a lightweight metric trained on latent triples from offline trajectories. A margin ranking loss encourages smaller distances for temporally nearer states, requiring no rewards or policy labels. The learned metric then replaces the Euclidean planning cost while the JEPA encoder and predictor remain frozen. Diagnostics on AntMaze and FourRoom show that T2G preserves stronger temporal contrast and improves near--far ranking at long offsets. Across four navigation and manipulation benchmarks, T2G improves the average planning success rate from 32.7\% (flat LeWM) to 63.0\% and approaches or even surpasses the performance of the hierarchical H-JEPA baselines. These results show that learning temporal ordering for the goal cost can substantially improve latent planning even with a flat world model.

</details>


### [99] [UniLoop: Unified Loop Closure Detection from Retrieval to Geometric Verification and Relative Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06378)
*Jingwen Yu,Pengyu Wang,Jin Wu,Weinan Chen,Ping Tan,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 UniLoop，将回环检测中的全局检索、几何验证和相对位姿估计统一到同一个基于预训练几何基础模型的框架中，通过共享分层图像表征减少冗余计算并提升回环检测的鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回环检测通常将全局检索、几何验证和相对位姿估计作为独立模型级联处理，导致计算开销重复、视觉表征彼此割裂，难以充分利用底层几何先验和跨任务信息共享。

Method: UniLoop 复用预训练几何基础模型的分层表征：共享图像编码器生成特征，一方面用于构建紧凑的全局描述子以完成地点检索，另一方面将同一特征输入跨视图解码器进行成对几何验证，同时利用基础模型原有的几何预测结果提供相对相机约束，用于位姿图优化。

Result: 在地点识别、回环验证和相对位姿估计实验上，UniLoop 相比模块化流程提升了回环检测的鲁棒性与精度，同时减少了主干网络的冗余计算。其在全局检索任务上取得了 MSLS 96.6 Recall@10 和 Pitts30k 99.3 Recall@10 的领先结果，验证分支达到 95.63 AP 与 96.29 ROC-AUC，优于现有几何验证基线。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将预训练几何基础模型的分层表征作为共享骨干，统一回环检测的多阶段流程，是一种更高效且更鲁棒的方案，有望替代传统级联式独立模块设计。

Abstract: Loop closure detection conventionally cascades separate models for global retrieval, geometric verification, and relative pose estimation, leading to redundant computation and disconnected visual representations. We propose UniLoop, a unified framework that reuses the hierarchical representations of the pretrained geometric foundation model throughout this pipeline. A shared image encoder produces features for compact global descriptors; the same features are passed to the cross-view decoder for pairwise verification; and the original geometric predictions provide relative camera constraints for pose-graph optimization. Experiments on place recognition, loop verification, and relative pose estimation show that UniLoop improves loop-closure robustness and accuracy while reducing redundant backbone computation relative to modular pipelines. UniLoop achieves the best Recall@10 on MSLS (96.6) and Pitts30k (99.3) among the compared methods, while its verification branch reaches 95.63 AP and 96.29 ROC-AUC, surpassing the existing geometric verification baselines.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Infraction Scores: Failure-Boundary Profiles for Closed-Loop Evaluation of Driving and Robot Navigation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06453)
*Milan Bulat,Gino Ardelean,Ellington Kirby*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出“failure-boundary profiles”评估框架，沿可测量因素扫描场景并认证“应通行/应停车”，分别估计 commitment 与 competence 曲线，并用 decision accuracy 揭示 CARLA driving score 无法区分冒险通过与安全停车的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CARLA 闭环驾驶基准使用 route completion 乘以 infraction penalty 的 driving score，但该指标可能奖励带接触强行通过障碍的代理，而不能反映在不可通行场景中应主动停车的安全决策能力。

Method: 将场景按单一测量因素构造为多个场景族，如走廊宽度或对向交通时间间隔，并通过实测认证每个变体属于可通行或应停车；对每个代理估计沿边界轴的 commitment（尝试通过概率）和 competence（无接触通过概率），定位两者跨过 0.5 的位置并给出考虑模拟器方差的不确定区间；同时利用认证标签计算 decision accuracy，奖励可通行场景中的干净通过和阻塞场景中的停车。最后在七个 CARLA 场景族上评估七个驾驶代理，并将方法不加修改地迁移到 Isaac Sim 中的仓储机器人。

Result: 规则型专家 agent 的 decision accuracy 为 0.74，得分最高的学习型 agent TFv6 为 0.60。具有相近 driving score 的 agent 失败模式不同；即便能在阻塞走廊可靠停车，也很少在对向交通中等待合适间隙。迁移到 Isaac Sim 后，导航策略仅在三个场景族中的一个能停于阻塞间隙；两个微调版本虽能更干净地通过更多间隙，但不再停车。

Conclusion: 单一 driving score 不能充分刻画闭环代理在“该进还是该停”边界上的决策能力；failure-boundary profiles 和 decision accuracy 能更细粒度地评估代理的安全承诺与通过能力，并暴露过度保守或过度冒险的失败模式。该框架具有跨具身智能体基准的迁移潜力。

Abstract: Closed-loop driving benchmarks in CARLA rank agents by a driving score that multiplies route completion by an infraction penalty. This score does not distinguish an agent that stops in front of an obstacle from one that pushes past it. In a corridor closed by two parked cars, for example, one agent that drives through with contact averages 52.2 on the blocked widths, while the agents that stop average between 37.8 and 40.6. We propose failure-boundary profiles, an evaluation in which each scenario family varies one measured factor, such as corridor width or the time gap in oncoming traffic, and each variant is certified from measurement as one where the agent should proceed or one where it should stop. Along this axis we estimate two curves per agent, commitment (the probability of attempting the passage) and competence (the probability of passing without contact), and locate where each crosses one half, with intervals that account for the simulator's run-to-run variance. The certified labels also give a summary metric, decision accuracy, which credits clean passes on passable variants and stops on blocked ones. We evaluate seven driving agents on seven CARLA families. The rule-based expert reaches a decision accuracy of 0.74 and TFv6, the highest-scoring learned agent, 0.60. Agents with similar driving scores fail in different ways, and agents that reliably stop at a blocked corridor still rarely wait for a gap in oncoming traffic. We also apply the method, unchanged, to a warehouse robot in Isaac Sim. The navigation policy we test stops at blocked gaps in only one of three families, and two fine-tuned versions of it pass more gaps cleanly but no longer stop at all.

</details>


### [101] [Dexterous Robotic Hands in the Foundation-Model Era: A Full-Stack Survey of Hardware, Data, Cross-Embodiment Transfer, Learning, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06724)
*Ji Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述围绕灵巧手机器人，系统梳理硬件、数据采集与整理、跨本体迁移、学习与基础模型、评测与应用五层研究，并提出人类数据价值、基础模型局限与评测改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着灵巧手与大规模人类数据及基础模型结合，硬件、表示学习、数据和评测常被分开评估，缺少系统级视角，难以判断技术进展与部署可行性。

Method: 综述2024年1月至2026年11月初相关文献，并纳入更早研究以建立技术脉络；提出五层互依组织框架：硬件、数据采集与整理、跨本体迁移、学习与基础模型、评测与应用。以人手-物体理解和触觉感知为交叉视角，通过系统级架构、比较表和五个案例分析连接设计选择，并区分实验对比、异构语料统计、厂商规格与企业报告结果。

Result: 形成三点结论：人类数据价值取决于运动学、视觉和接触对齐，而非仅录制数量；共享动作表示和基础策略不能消除力、延迟和接触方面的本体特定限制；仅任务完成不足以保证物理正确性和部署就绪。证据在形态差异手之间的接触丰富技能零样本迁移、长期可靠性和跨传感器触觉泛化方面仍有限。

Conclusion: 该综述是结构化叙事综合，而非统一性能排名或穷尽式系统综述；它识别十一项开放问题，并提出标准化报告接口与受控比较，以提升全栈灵巧手研究进展的可衡量性。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands are increasingly coupled to large-scale human data and foundation models, yet progress in hardware, representation learning and evaluation is often assessed separately. This survey reviews work from January 2024 to early November 2026, with earlier studies included to establish technical lineages. We organize the literature into five interdependent layers: hardware; data collection and curation; cross-embodiment transfer; learning and foundation models; and evaluation and applications. Human hand-object understanding and tactile sensing provide two cross-cutting perspectives. A system-level architecture, comparative tables and five case analyses connect design choices across these layers. We distinguish within-study experimental comparisons from heterogeneous corpus statistics, vendor specifications and company-reported results. Three conclusions emerge. First, the value of human data depends on kinematic, visual and contact alignment, not recording volume alone. Second, shared action representations and foundation policies do not remove embodiment-specific constraints on force, latency and contact. Third, task completion is insufficient to establish physical correctness or deployment readiness. Evidence remains limited for zero-shot transfer of contact-rich skills between morphologically different hands, long-duration reliability, and cross-sensor tactile generalization. We identify eleven open problems and propose reporting interfaces and controlled comparisons that can make full-stack progress more measurable. The review is a structured narrative synthesis rather than a pooled performance ranking or an exhaustive systematic review.

</details>


### [102] [Perceptive Humanoid Motion from Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06908)
*Siyuan Hu,Abulikemu Abuduweili,Yue Meng,Shipeng Liu,Quentin Leboutet,Chen Chen,Matthias Müller,Vladlen Koltun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALO是一个面向人形机器人自主性的系统，通过在千个程序生成的住宅中合成视觉-语言-运动数据实现大规模训练，并在未见过的真实环境中零样本迁移，完成360次试验达到91%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人走向通用自主操作的核心瓶颈是训练数据缺失：将图像和自由文本指令映射到低层关节控制需要配对监督数据，但大规模真实像素-语言-动作语料不存在；依赖人类远程操作或穿戴捕捉设备成本高、扩展慢。

Method: 提出HALO，构建一条跨千个程序生成家居环境的合成视觉-语言-运动数据管线，使语料规模随算力扩展而非依赖人工采集。模型从合成数据中学习多类物理技能，包括坐下、导航到指定物体、侧身通过半开门、蹲爬过桌下、推箱子等，并将这些技能编排为复合长时序任务策略。训练过程中不接触真实住宅，再直接零样本迁移到现实物理环境。

Result: 在六个未建图、未训练过的真实环境中进行360次试验，HALO取得91%任务成功率，表明合成程序生成环境中的数据训练可支持真实世界零样本迁移。

Conclusion: HALO展示了以算力驱动的合成数据管线替代昂贵人工演示来训练人形机器人通用自主性的可行路径，并验证其技能习得、任务编排与真实世界零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have the potential to automate a wide range of physical tasks. Progress toward generalist humanoid autonomy, however, remains constrained by a data bottleneck: mapping pixels and free-form language to low-level joint commands requires paired supervision, and no corpus of such data exists at sufficiently large scale. Some approaches rely on human teleoperation and wearable capture devices, which require at least an hour of human time to produce an hour of data. We present HALO, a humanoid autonomy system powered by a pipeline that synthesizes vision-language-motion data across a thousand procedurally generated homes. The corpus scales with compute rather than human labor. HALO acquires a repertoire of physical skills, including sitting on chairs, navigating to named objects, stepping sideways through half-open doors, crouching and crawling beneath tables, and pushing boxes. It orchestrates these skills to solve composite, long-horizon tasks. Without ever seeing a real home during training, HALO transfers zero-shot to the physical world, achieving a 91% success rate across 360 trials in six unmapped real-world environments. See this https URL for code and videos.

</details>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Interactive World Simulation with Cosmos for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06627)
*NVIDIA,Anis Ahmad,Jon Allen,Malik Aqeel Anwar,Jesse Archer,Maciej Bala,Piotr Barejko,Aarti Basant,Dan Blick,Taras Bolzhelarskyi,Henry Che,Jin Chen,Yukang Chen,Tae Eun Choe,Yuzhu Dong,Nat Duca,Liang Feng,Francesco Ferroni,Xiao Fu,Mohit Gambhir,Vivek Goel,Rony Goldenthal,Aryaman Gupta,Ali Hassani,Nathan Hayes-Roth,Yong He,Michael Honke,Shengyu Huang,Wei Huang,DeLesley Hutchins,Suneel Indupuru,Pooya Jannaty,Jingyi Jin,Janmesh Kalra,Ashna Khetan,Xin Kong,Aleksandr Korovko,Hui Kuang,Zhengfei Kuang,George Kurian,Jean-Francois Lafleche,Jayjun Lee,Justin Leung,William Lew,Xiang Li,Zhaoshuo Li,Chen-Hsuan Lin,Ming-Yu Liu,Xiangyu Lu,Yuhang Lu,Martin Ding Ma,Qianli Ma,Aditya Mahajan,Miguel Martin,Janarbek Matai,Jonathan McCaffrey,Qing Miao,Ashkan Mirzaei,Mustafa Munir,Sangmin Oh,David Pankratz,Trung Pham,Marilyn Reeb,Gavriel State,Jiaxiang Tang,Shitao Tang,Yue Tang,Tolou Tavakkoli,Alexander Temirov,Hweimi Tsou,Sergei Vasilev,Thomas Volk,Andrew Z. Wang,Hao Wang,Shijie Wang,Zhonghao Wang,David Wehr,Shumian Xin,Mengyao Xu,Yao Xu,Shuai Yang,Sophia Zalewski,Pengcuo Zeren,Cindy Zha,Jing Zhang,Yuliya Zhautouskaya,Kaiwen Zheng,Andrew Zhu,Rick Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个共享框架，将 Cosmos 世界基础模型适配为可用于 Physical AI 的实时交互式世界模拟器，能够根据动作与场景控制流式生成视觉观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能和物理 AI 的训练与评测需要能够响应用户或智能体动作、动态演化并可控的虚拟环境，而传统离线视频生成或仿真方法难以支持实时、交互式且与物理状态耦合的场景模拟。

Method: 框架将 Cosmos 世界基础模型用于实时视觉观测流生成，使环境和智能体能够基于动作与场景控制持续交互；同时支持两类能力：一是预测动作导致的视觉后果，二是根据外部仿真器维护的物理状态生成相应视觉观测。作者通过相机导航、机器人操作、可控视频转换以及多视角自动驾驶模拟等任务进行验证。

Result: 实验展示了该框架在多种 Physical AI 场景中的适用性，包括相机导航与机器人操控、可控视频转换、多视角自动驾驶模拟等，表明其可作为构建交互式虚拟环境的共同基础，并提供了代码、模型权重和项目网站。

Conclusion: 该研究为 Physical AI 提供了一种实时、可控、交互式的虚拟世界模拟框架，有助于具身智能体和自动驾驶等系统的训练、预测与评测。

Abstract: We present a shared framework that adapts Cosmos world foundation models for real-time interactive world simulation in Physical AI. By streaming visual observations in response to actions and scene controls, the framework enables users and embodied agents to interact with evolving virtual environments. It supports both predicting the visual consequences of actions and generating observations from physical state maintained by an external simulator. We demonstrate this approach across camera navigation and robot manipulation, controllable video transfer, and multi-view autonomous vehicle (AV) simulation. These examples provide a shared foundation for developing interactive virtual environments for Physical AI training and evaluation. Our code and model checkpoints are available at this https URL and this https URL. The project website is available at this https URL.

</details>
